1→# Debug Tool
     2→
     3→One unified tool to investigate tasks, workers, and system health.
     4→
     5→## Quick Reference
     6→
     7→```bash
     8→# Task investigation
     9→debug.py task <task_id>              # Complete task analysis with logs
    10→debug.py task <task_id> --logs-only  # Just the log timeline
    11→
    12→# Worker investigation
    13→debug.py worker <worker_id>                  # Full worker analysis
    14→debug.py worker <worker_id> --check-logging  # Is worker.py running?
    15→debug.py worker <worker_id> --startup        # View initialization logs
    16→debug.py worker <worker_id> --logs-only      # Just the log timeline
    17→
    18→# System overview
    19→debug.py tasks                 # Recent task statistics
    20→debug.py workers               # Recent worker status
    21→debug.py health                # Overall system health
    22→debug.py orchestrator          # Is orchestrator running?
    23→
    24→# Configuration & Infrastructure
    25→debug.py config                # View timing/scaling settings
    26→debug.py config --explain      # With detailed explanations
    27→debug.py runpod                # Find orphaned pods ($$$ leak!)
    28→debug.py runpod --terminate    # Terminate orphaned pods
    29→
    30→# Output formats
    31→--json                         # JSON output for any command
    32→```
    33→
    34→## Common Scenarios
    35→
    36→### "Why did this task fail?"
    37→```bash
    38→debug.py task <task_id>
    39→# Shows: task state, error message, timing, worker assignment, full log timeline
    40→```
    41→
    42→### "Worker isn't doing anything"
    43→```bash
    44→debug.py worker <worker_id> --check-logging
    45→# Verifies if worker.py actually started
    46→
    47→debug.py worker <worker_id> --startup
    48→# Shows installation/initialization process
    49→```
    50→
    51→### "System seems unhealthy"
    52→```bash
    53→debug.py health
    54→# Quick overview: worker status, task queue, recent errors
    55→
    56→debug.py orchestrator
    57→# Is orchestrator running? Recent cycles?
    58→
    59→debug.py workers
    60→# List all recent workers with failure analysis
    61→```
    62→
    63→### "We're wasting money"
    64→```bash
    65→debug.py runpod
    66→# Finds pods running in RunPod but not tracked in database
    67→# Shows daily/monthly cost waste
    68→```
    69→
    70→### "What are my timeout settings?"
    71→```bash
    72→debug.py config --explain
    73→# Shows all timing configuration with explanations
    74→# Grace periods, idle timeouts, scale-up thresholds
    75→```
    76→
    77→## Architecture
    78→
    79→```
    80→debug/
    81→├── README.md          # This file
    82→├── __init__.py        # Package init
    83→├── models.py          # Data models (Task, Worker, LogEntry)
    84→├── client.py          # DebugClient - unified data access
    85→├── formatters.py      # Output formatting (text/JSON)
    86→└── commands/          # Command handlers
    87→    ├── task.py        # Task investigation
    88→    ├── worker.py      # Worker investigation
    89→    ├── tasks.py       # Multi-task analysis
    90→    ├── workers.py     # Multi-worker analysis
    91→    ├── health.py      # System health
    92→    ├── orchestrator.py # Orchestrator status
    93→    ├── config.py      # Configuration display
    94→    └── runpod.py      # RunPod sync
    95→```
    96→
    97→## Data Sources
    98→
    99→All commands use **system_logs** as the primary data source, augmented with current state from `tasks` and `workers` tables. This "logs-first" approach gives you a complete timeline of what actually happened.
   100→
   101→## Tips
   102→
   103→- Use `--json` for scripting/automation
   104→- Use `--hours N` to adjust time window (default: 24h)
   105→- Worker diagnostics show pre-termination VRAM, running tasks, pod status
   106→- RunPod sync can save serious money by finding orphaned pods
   107→- Config command is invaluable for debugging timing issues
   108→

1→#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2→"""
     3→Unified Debug Tool
     4→==================
     5→
     6→One tool to investigate tasks, workers, and system health.
     7→Uses system_logs as the primary data source.
     8→
     9→Usage:
    10→    debug.py task <task_id>             # Investigate specific task
    11→    debug.py worker <worker_id>         # Investigate specific worker
    12→    debug.py tasks                      # Analyze recent tasks
    13→    debug.py workers                    # List recent workers
    14→    debug.py health                     # System health check
    15→    debug.py orchestrator               # Orchestrator status
    16→
    17→Options:
    18→    --json                              # Output as JSON
    19→    --hours N                           # Time window in hours
    20→    --limit N                           # Limit results
    21→    --logs-only                         # Show only logs timeline
    22→    --debug                             # Show debug info on errors
    23→"""
    24→
    25→import sys
    26→import argparse
    27→from pathlib import Path
    28→
    29→# Add project root to path so `import debug` works.
    30→project_root = Path(__file__).parent.parent
    31→sys.path.insert(0, str(project_root))
    32→
    33→from debug.client import DebugClient
    34→
    35→
    36→def create_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    37→    """Create argument parser."""
    38→    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
    39→        description="Unified debugging tool for investigating tasks, workers, and system health",
    40→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    41→        epilog=__doc__,
    42→    )
    43→
    44→    subparsers = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", help="Command to run", required=True)
    45→
    46→    # Task command
    47→    task_parser = subparsers.add_parser("task", help="Investigate specific task")
    48→    task_parser.add_argument("task_id", help="Task ID to investigate")
    49→    task_parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Output as JSON")
    50→    task_parser.add_argument("--logs-only", action="store_true", help="Show only logs timeline")
    51→    task_parser.add_argument("--debug", action="store_true", help="Show debug info on errors")
    52→
    53→    # Worker command
    54→    worker_parser = subparsers.add_parser("worker", help="Investigate specific worker")
    55→    worker_parser.add_argument("worker_id", help="Worker ID to investigate")
    56→    worker_parser.add_argument("--hours", type=int, default=24, help="Hours of history (default: 24)")
    57→    worker_parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Output as JSON")
    58→    worker_parser.add_argument("--logs-only", action="store_true", help="Show only logs timeline")
    59→    worker_parser.add_argument("--startup", action="store_true", help="Show startup logs only")
    60→    worker_parser.add_argument("--check-logging", action="store_true", help="Check if worker is logging")
    61→    worker_parser.add_argument("--check-disk", action="store_true", help="Check disk space via SSH")
    62→    worker_parser.add_argument("--debug", action="store_true", help="Show debug info on errors")
    63→
    64→    # Tasks command
    65→    tasks_parser = subparsers.add_parser("tasks", help="Analyze recent tasks")
    66→    tasks_parser.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=50, help="Number of tasks (default: 50)")
    67→    tasks_parser.add_argument("--status", help="Filter by status")
    68→    tasks_parser.add_argument("--type", help="Filter by task type")
    69→    tasks_parser.add_argument("--worker", help="Filter by worker ID")
    70→    tasks_parser.add_argument("--hours", type=int, help="Filter by hours")
    71→    tasks_parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Output as JSON")
    72→    tasks_parser.add_argument("--debug", action="store_true", help="Show debug info on errors")
    73→
    74→    # Workers command
    75→    workers_parser = subparsers.add_parser("workers", help="List recent workers")
    76→    workers_parser.add_argument("--hours", type=int, default=2, help="Hours of history (default: 2)")
    77→    workers_parser.add_argument("--detailed", action="store_true", help="Show detailed analysis")
    78→    workers_parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Output as JSON")
    79→    workers_parser.add_argument("--debug", action="store_true", help="Show debug info on errors")
    80→
    81→    # Health command
    82→    health_parser = subparsers.add_parser("health", help="System health check")
    83→    health_parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Output as JSON")
    84→    health_parser.add_argument("--debug", action="store_true", help="Show debug info on errors")
    85→
    86→    # Orchestrator command
    87→    orch_parser = subparsers.add_parser("orchestrator", help="Orchestrator status")
    88→    orch_parser.add_argument("--hours", type=int, default=1, help="Hours of history (default: 1)")
    89→    orch_parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Output as JSON")
    90→    orch_parser.add_argument("--debug", action="store_true", help="Show debug info on errors")
    91→
    92→    # Config command
    93→    config_parser = subparsers.add_parser("config", help="Show system configuration")
    94→    config_parser.add_argument("--explain", action="store_true", help="Show detailed explanations")
    95→
    96→    # RunPod command
    97→    runpod_parser = subparsers.add_parser("runpod", help="Check RunPod sync status")
    98→    runpod_parser.add_argument("--terminate", action="store_true", help="Terminate orphaned pods")
    99→    runpod_parser.add_argument("--debug", action="store_true", help="Show debug info on errors")
   100→
   101→    # Storage command
   102→    storage_parser = subparsers.add_parser("storage", help="Check storage volume health")
   103→    storage_parser.add_argument("--expand", type=str, help="Expand specified storage volume")
   104→    storage_parser.add_argument("--debug", action="store_true", help="Show debug info on errors")
   105→
   106→    return parser
   107→
   108→
   109→def main() -> None:
   110→    """Main entry point."""
   111→    parser = create_parser()
   112→    args = parser.parse_args()
   113→
   114→    # Import command handlers lazily (keeps import-time errors localized)
   115→    from debug.commands import task, worker, tasks, workers, health, orchestrator, config, runpod, storage
   116→
   117→    # Create debug client
   118→    try:
   119→        client = DebugClient()
   120→    except Exception as e:
   121→        print(f"❌ Failed to initialize debug client: {e}")
   122→        print("\n💡 Make sure your environment has:")
   123→        print("   - SUPABASE_URL")
   124→        print("   - SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")
   125→        sys.exit(1)
   126→
   127→    # Convert args to options dict
   128→    options = {
   129→        "format": "json" if getattr(args, "json", False) else "text",
   130→        "debug": getattr(args, "debug", False),
   131→    }
   132→
   133→    # Add command-specific options
   134→    for key in [
   135→        "hours",
   136→        "limit",
   137→        "status",
   138→        "type",
   139→        "worker",
   140→        "detailed",
   141→        "logs_only",
   142→        "startup",
   143→        "check_logging",
   144→        "check_disk",
   145→        "explain",
   146→        "terminate",
   147→        "expand",
   148→    ]:
   149→        if hasattr(args, key):
   150→            options[key] = getattr(args, key)
   151→
   152→    # Route to appropriate command handler
   153→    try:
   154→        if args.command == "task":
   155→            task.run(client, args.task_id, options)
   156→        elif args.command == "worker":
   157→            worker.run(client, args.worker_id, options)
   158→        elif args.command == "tasks":
   159→            tasks.run(client, options)
   160→        elif args.command == "workers":
   161→            workers.run(client, options)
   162→        elif args.command == "health":
   163→            health.run(client, options)
   164→        elif args.command == "orchestrator":
   165→            orchestrator.run(client, options)
   166→        elif args.command == "config":
   167→            config.run(client, options)
   168→        elif args.command == "runpod":
   169→            runpod.run(client, options)
   170→        elif args.command == "storage":
   171→            storage.run(client, options)
   172→        else:
   173→            parser.print_help()
   174→            sys.exit(1)
   175→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   176→        print("\n\n👋 Interrupted by user")
   177→        sys.exit(0)
   178→    except Exception as e:
   179→        print(f"\n❌ Command failed: {e}")
   180→        if options.get("debug"):
   181→            import traceback
   182→
   183→            print(traceback.format_exc())
   184→        sys.exit(1)
   185→
   186→
   187→if __name__ == "__main__":
   188→    main()
   189→
   190→

1→"""Unified client for debugging data access."""
     2→
     3→import os
     4→from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
     5→from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
     6→from collections import Counter
     7→from dotenv import load_dotenv
     8→
     9→# Load environment variables
    10→load_dotenv()
    11→
    12→from supabase import create_client
    13→from debug.models import (
    14→    TaskInfo, WorkerInfo, TasksSummary, WorkersSummary,
    15→    SystemHealth, OrchestratorStatus
    16→)
    17→
    18→
    19→class LogQueryClient:
    20→    """Client for querying system logs from Supabase."""
    21→    
    22→    def __init__(self, supabase_client):
    23→        self.supabase = supabase_client
    24→    
    25→    def get_logs(
    26→        self,
    27→        start_time: datetime = None,
    28→        end_time: datetime = None,
    29→        source_type: str = None,
    30→        source_id: str = None,
    31→        worker_id: str = None,
    32→        task_id: str = None,
    33→        log_level: str = None,
    34→        cycle_number: int = None,
    35→        search_term: str = None,
    36→        limit: int = 1000,
    37→        order_desc: bool = True
    38→    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    39→        """
    40→        Query logs with flexible filters.
    41→        """
    42→        # Default time range: last 24 hours
    43→        if not start_time:
    44→            start_time = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(hours=24)
    45→        if not end_time:
    46→            end_time = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    47→        
    48→        # Check if system_logs table exists
    49→        try:
    50→            query = self.supabase.table('system_logs').select('*')
    51→            
    52→            # Apply filters
    53→            query = query.gte('timestamp', start_time.isoformat())
    54→            query = query.lte('timestamp', end_time.isoformat())
    55→            
    56→            if source_type:
    57→                query = query.eq('source_type', source_type)
    58→            if source_id:
    59→                query = query.eq('source_id', source_id)
    60→            if worker_id:
    61→                query = query.eq('worker_id', worker_id)
    62→            if task_id:
    63→                query = query.eq('task_id', task_id)
    64→            if log_level:
    65→                query = query.eq('log_level', log_level)
    66→            if cycle_number is not None:
    67→                query = query.eq('cycle_number', cycle_number)
    68→            if search_term:
    69→                query = query.ilike('message', f'%{search_term}%')
    70→            
    71→            # Order and limit
    72→            query = query.order('timestamp', desc=order_desc)
    73→            query = query.limit(limit)
    74→            
    75→            result = query.execute()
    76→            return result.data or []
    77→        except Exception as e:
    78→            # system_logs table might not exist in this setup
    79→            print(f"[DEBUG] Failed to query system_logs: {e}")
    80→            return []
    81→    
    82→    def get_task_timeline(self, task_id: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    83→        """Get timeline of logs for a specific task."""
    84→        return self.get_logs(
    85→            task_id=task_id,
    86→            limit=1000,
    87→            order_desc=False  # Chronological order
    88→        )
    89→
    90→
    91→class DebugClient:
    92→    """Unified client for all debugging data."""
    93→    
    94→    def __init__(self):
    95→        supabase_url = os.getenv("SUPABASE_URL")
    96→        supabase_key = os.getenv("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY")
    97→        
    98→        if not supabase_url or not supabase_key:
    99→            raise ValueError("SUPABASE_URL and SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY must be set in environment")
   100→        
   101→        self.supabase = create_client(supabase_url, supabase_key)
   102→        self.log_client = LogQueryClient(self.supabase)
   103→    
   104→    # ==================== TASK METHODS ====================
   105→    
   106→    def get_task_info(self, task_id: str, follow_cascade: bool = True) -> TaskInfo:
   107→        """Get complete task information: logs + DB state.
   108→        
   109→        If follow_cascade is True and this is an orchestrator that failed due to
   110→        a child task, also fetch the child task info.
   111→        
   112→        For orchestrator tasks, also fetches all child tasks (segments, stitch, etc.)
   113→        """
   114→        # Get logs for this task
   115→        logs = self.log_client.get_task_timeline(task_id)
   116→        
   117→        # Get task state from DB
   118→        result = self.supabase.table('tasks').select('*').eq('id', task_id).execute()
   119→        state = result.data[0] if result.data else None
   120→        
   121→        # Check for cascaded failure and fetch child task
   122→        child_task_info = None
   123→        child_tasks = None
   124→        
   125→        if follow_cascade and state:
   126→            error_msg = state.get('error_message', '') or ''
   127→            output_loc = state.get('output_location', '') or ''
   128→            
   129→            # Look for "Cascaded failed from related task <uuid>" pattern
   130→            import re
   131→            cascade_pattern = r'[Cc]ascad(?:ed|e) (?:failed |failure )?(?:from )?(?:related )?task[:\s]+([a-f0-9-]{36})'
   132→            
   133→            for text in [error_msg, output_loc]:
   134→                match = re.search(cascade_pattern, text)
   135→                if match:
   136→                    child_task_id = match.group(1)
   137→                    if child_task_id != task_id:  # Avoid infinite recursion
   138→                        child_task_info = self.get_task_info(child_task_id, follow_cascade=False)
   139→                    break
   140→            
   141→            # If this is an orchestrator, fetch all child tasks
   142→            task_type = state.get('task_type', '')
   143→            if 'orchestrator' in task_type.lower():
   144→                child_tasks = self._get_orchestrator_child_tasks(task_id, state)
   145→        
   146→        return TaskInfo(
   147→            task_id=task_id,
   148→            state=state,
   149→            logs=logs,
   150→            child_task_info=child_task_info,
   151→            child_tasks=child_tasks
   152→        )
   153→    
   154→    def _get_orchestrator_child_tasks(self, orchestrator_id: str, orchestrator_state: dict) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
   155→        """Get all child tasks for an orchestrator task.
   156→        
   157→        Looks for tasks that reference this orchestrator via:
   158→        - params.orchestrator_task_id_ref
   159→        - params.orchestrator_details.orchestrator_task_id
   160→        - dependant_on field
   161→        """
   162→        child_tasks = []
   163→        
   164→        # Method 1: Query by dependant_on
   165→        try:
   166→            result = self.supabase.table('tasks').select(
   167→                'id, task_type, status, error_message, output_location, created_at, '
   168→                'generation_started_at, generation_processed_at, worker_id, attempts, params'
   169→            ).eq('dependant_on', orchestrator_id).order('created_at').execute()
   170→            if result.data:
   171→                child_tasks.extend(result.data)
   172→        except Exception as e:
   173→            print(f"[DEBUG] Failed to query child tasks by dependant_on: {e}")
   174→
   175→        # Method 2: Look for orchestrator_task_id in params (for travel segments)
   176→        # This requires a more complex query - check params JSON
   177→        try:
   178→            # Get project_id from orchestrator params
   179→            orch_params = orchestrator_state.get('params', {})
   180→            if isinstance(orch_params, str):
   181→                import json
   182→                orch_params = json.loads(orch_params)
   183→            
   184→            project_id = orchestrator_state.get('project_id') or orch_params.get('project_id')
   185→            
   186→            if project_id:
   187→                # Get recent tasks from same project that might be children
   188→                result = self.supabase.table('tasks').select(
   189→                    'id, task_type, status, error_message, output_location, created_at, '
   190→                    'generation_started_at, generation_processed_at, worker_id, attempts, params'
   191→                ).eq('project_id', project_id).in_(
   192→                    'task_type', ['travel_segment', 'travel_stitch', 'join_clips']
   193→                ).order('created_at').execute()
   194→                
   195→                if result.data:
   196→                    for task in result.data:
   197→                        # Check if this task references our orchestrator
   198→                        task_params = task.get('params', {})
   199→                        if isinstance(task_params, str):
   200→                            import json
   201→                            try:
   202→                                task_params = json.loads(task_params)
   203→                            except Exception as e:
   204→                                print(f"[DEBUG] Failed to parse task params JSON for task {task.get('id')}: {e}")
   205→                                continue
   206→                        
   207→                        # Check orchestrator_task_id_ref or orchestrator_details.orchestrator_task_id
   208→                        ref_id = task_params.get('orchestrator_task_id_ref')
   209→                        if not ref_id:
   210→                            orch_details = task_params.get('orchestrator_details', {})
   211→                            ref_id = orch_details.get('orchestrator_task_id')
   212→                        
   213→                        if ref_id == orchestrator_id and task['id'] not in [t['id'] for t in child_tasks]:
   214→                            child_tasks.append(task)
   215→        except Exception as e:
   216→            print(f"[DEBUG] Failed to query child tasks by project_id/params: {e}")
   217→
   218→        # Sort by segment_index if available, otherwise by created_at
   219→        def sort_key(task):
   220→            params = task.get('params', {})
   221→            if isinstance(params, str):
   222→                import json
   223→                try:
   224→                    params = json.loads(params)
   225→                except Exception as e:
   226→                    print(f"[DEBUG] Failed to parse params JSON for sort_key: {e}")
   227→                    params = {}
   228→            segment_idx = params.get('segment_index', 999)
   229→            return (segment_idx, task.get('created_at', ''))
   230→        
   231→        child_tasks.sort(key=sort_key)
   232→        return child_tasks
   233→    
   234→    # ==================== WORKER METHODS ====================
   235→    
   236→    def get_worker_info(self, worker_id: str, hours: int = 24, startup: bool = False) -> WorkerInfo:
   237→        """Get complete worker information: logs + DB state + tasks."""
   238→        start_time = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(hours=hours)
   239→        
   240→        # Get logs for this worker
   241→        logs = self.log_client.get_logs(
   242→            start_time=start_time,
   243→            worker_id=worker_id,
   244→            limit=5000,
   245→            order_desc=False
   246→        )
   247→        
   248→        # Try to get worker state from DB (workers table may not exist)
   249→        state = None
   250→        try:
   251→            result = self.supabase.table('workers').select('*').eq('id', worker_id).execute()
   252→            state = result.data[0] if result.data else None
   253→        except Exception as e:
   254→            print(f"[DEBUG] Failed to query worker state for {worker_id}: {e}")
   255→
   256→        # Get tasks assigned to this worker
   257→        tasks = []
   258→        try:
   259→            tasks_result = self.supabase.table('tasks').select('*').eq('worker_id', worker_id).order('created_at', desc=True).limit(20).execute()
   260→            tasks = tasks_result.data or []
   261→        except Exception as e:
   262→            print(f"[DEBUG] Failed to query tasks for worker {worker_id}: {e}")
   263→
   264→        return WorkerInfo(
   265→            worker_id=worker_id,
   266→            state=state,
   267→            logs=logs,
   268→            tasks=tasks
   269→        )
   270→    
   271→    def check_worker_logging(self, worker_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   272→        """Check if worker has started logging."""
   273→        logs = self.log_client.get_logs(
   274→            worker_id=worker_id,
   275→            limit=10,
   276→            order_desc=True
   277→        )
   278→        
   279→        return {
   280→            'is_logging': len(logs) > 0,
   281→            'log_count': len(logs),
   282→            'recent_logs': logs[:5] if logs else []
   283→        }
   284→    
   285→    def check_worker_disk_space(self, worker_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   286→        """Check disk space on a live worker via SSH (not supported in this setup)."""
   287→        return {'available': False, 'error': 'SSH disk check not supported in this setup'}
   288→    
   289→    # ==================== MULTI-TASK METHODS ====================
   290→    
   291→    def get_recent_tasks(
   292→        self,
   293→        limit: int = 50,
   294→        status: Optional[str] = None,
   295→        task_type: Optional[str] = None,
   296→        worker_id: Optional[str] = None,
   297→        hours: Optional[int] = None
   298→    ) -> TasksSummary:
   299→        """Get recent tasks with analysis."""
   300→        # Build query
   301→        query = self.supabase.table('tasks').select('*')
   302→        
   303→        if status:
   304→            query = query.eq('status', status)
   305→        if task_type:
   306→            query = query.eq('task_type', task_type)
   307→        if worker_id:
   308→            query = query.eq('worker_id', worker_id)
   309→        if hours:
   310→            cutoff = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(hours=hours)
   311→            query = query.gte('created_at', cutoff.isoformat())
   312→        
   313→        query = query.order('created_at', desc=True).limit(limit)
   314→        result = query.execute()
   315→        tasks = result.data or []
   316→        
   317→        # Calculate statistics
   318→        status_dist = Counter(t.get('status') for t in tasks)
   319→        type_dist = Counter(t.get('task_type') for t in tasks)
   320→        
   321→        # Calculate timing statistics
   322→        processing_times = []
   323→        queue_times = []
   324→        
   325→        for task in tasks:
   326→            if task.get('generation_started_at') and task.get('generation_processed_at'):
   327→                try:
   328→                    started = datetime.fromisoformat(task['generation_started_at'].replace('Z', '+00:00'))
   329→                    processed = datetime.fromisoformat(task['generation_processed_at'].replace('Z', '+00:00'))
   330→                    processing_times.append((processed - started).total_seconds())
   331→                except Exception as e:
   332→                    print(f"[DEBUG] Failed to parse processing times for task {task.get('id')}: {e}")
   333→                    pass
   334→            
   335→            if task.get('created_at') and task.get('generation_started_at'):
   336→                try:
   337→                    created = datetime.fromisoformat(task['created_at'].replace('Z', '+00:00'))
   338→                    started = datetime.fromisoformat(task['generation_started_at'].replace('Z', '+00:00'))
   339→                    queue_times.append((started - created).total_seconds())
   340→                except Exception as e:
   341→                    print(f"[DEBUG] Failed to parse queue times for task {task.get('id')}: {e}")
   342→                    pass
   343→        
   344→        timing_stats = {
   345→            'avg_processing_seconds': sum(processing_times) / len(processing_times) if processing_times else None,
   346→            'avg_queue_seconds': sum(queue_times) / len(queue_times) if queue_times else None,
   347→            'total_with_timing': len(processing_times)
   348→        }
   349→        
   350→        # Get recent failures from logs
   351→        recent_failures = []
   352→        if hours:
   353→            cutoff = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(hours=hours)
   354→        else:
   355→            cutoff = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(hours=24)
   356→        
   357→        error_logs = self.log_client.get_logs(
   358→            start_time=cutoff,
   359→            log_level='ERROR',
   360→            limit=100
   361→        )
   362→        
   363→        # Extract task failures
   364→        for log in error_logs:
   365→            if log.get('task_id'):
   366→                recent_failures.append({
   367→                    'task_id': log['task_id'],
   368→                    'timestamp': log['timestamp'],
   369→                    'message': log['message'],
   370→                    'worker_id': log.get('worker_id')
   371→                })
   372→        
   373→        return TasksSummary(
   374→            tasks=tasks,
   375→            total_count=len(tasks),
   376→            status_distribution=dict(status_dist),
   377→            task_type_distribution=dict(type_dist),
   378→            timing_stats=timing_stats,
   379→            recent_failures=recent_failures[:10]
   380→        )
   381→    
   382→    # ==================== MULTI-WORKER METHODS ====================
   383→    
   384→    def get_workers_summary(self, hours: int = 2, detailed: bool = False) -> WorkersSummary:
   385→        """Get workers summary with health status."""
   386→        # Workers table may not exist in this setup
   387→        workers = []
   388→        try:
   389→            cutoff_time = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(hours=hours)
   390→            result = self.supabase.table('workers').select('*').gte('created_at', cutoff_time.isoformat()).order('created_at', desc=True).execute()
   391→            workers = result.data or []
   392→        except Exception as e:
   393→            print(f"[DEBUG] Failed to query workers summary: {e}")
   394→
   395→        # Calculate statistics
   396→        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
   397→        status_counts = Counter(w.get('status') for w in workers)
   398→        
   399→        active_healthy = 0
   400→        active_stale = 0
   401→        
   402→        for worker in workers:
   403→            if worker.get('status') == 'active':
   404→                last_hb = worker.get('last_heartbeat')
   405→                if last_hb:
   406→                    try:
   407→                        hb_time = datetime.fromisoformat(last_hb.replace('Z', '+00:00'))
   408→                        age_seconds = (now - hb_time).total_seconds()
   409→                        if age_seconds < 60:
   410→                            active_healthy += 1
   411→                        else:
   412→                            active_stale += 1
   413→                    except Exception as e:
   414→                        print(f"[DEBUG] Failed to parse heartbeat for worker {worker.get('id')}: {e}")
   415→                        active_stale += 1
   416→        
   417→        # Get recent failures
   418→        recent_failures = []
   419→        for worker in workers:
   420→            if worker.get('status') in ['error', 'terminated']:
   421→                metadata = worker.get('metadata', {})
   422→                recent_failures.append({
   423→                    'worker_id': worker['id'],
   424→                    'status': worker['status'],
   425→                    'created_at': worker.get('created_at'),
   426→                    'error_reason': metadata.get('error_reason', 'Unknown') if isinstance(metadata, dict) else 'Unknown'
   427→                })
   428→        
   429→        # Calculate failure rate
   430→        failure_rate = None
   431→        if len(workers) >= 5:
   432→            failed = len([w for w in workers if w['status'] in ['error', 'terminated']])
   433→            failure_rate = failed / len(workers)
   434→        
   435→        return WorkersSummary(
   436→            workers=workers,
   437→            total_count=len(workers),
   438→            status_counts=dict(status_counts),
   439→            active_healthy=active_healthy,
   440→            active_stale=active_stale,
   441→            recent_failures=recent_failures[:10],
   442→            failure_rate=failure_rate
   443→        )
   444→    
   445→    # ==================== SYSTEM HEALTH ====================
   446→    
   447→    def get_system_health(self) -> SystemHealth:
   448→        """Get overall system health."""
   449→        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
   450→        
   451→        # Get workers (may not exist)
   452→        workers = []
   453→        try:
   454→            workers_result = self.supabase.table('workers').select('*').neq('status', 'terminated').execute()
   455→            workers = workers_result.data or []
   456→        except Exception as e:
   457→            print(f"[DEBUG] Failed to query workers for system health: {e}")
   458→
   459→        workers_active = len([w for w in workers if w.get('status') == 'active'])
   460→        workers_spawning = len([w for w in workers if w.get('status') == 'spawning'])
   461→        
   462→        # Count healthy workers
   463→        workers_healthy = 0
   464→        for worker in workers:
   465→            if worker.get('status') == 'active' and worker.get('last_heartbeat'):
   466→                try:
   467→                    hb_time = datetime.fromisoformat(worker['last_heartbeat'].replace('Z', '+00:00'))
   468→                    if (now - hb_time).total_seconds() < 60:
   469→                        workers_healthy += 1
   470→                except Exception as e:
   471→                    print(f"[DEBUG] Failed to parse heartbeat for worker {worker.get('id')}: {e}")
   472→                    pass
   473→        
   474→        # Get task counts
   475→        tasks_result = self.supabase.table('tasks').select('status').execute()
   476→        tasks = tasks_result.data or []
   477→        
   478→        tasks_queued = len([t for t in tasks if t['status'] == 'Queued'])
   479→        tasks_in_progress = len([t for t in tasks if t['status'] == 'In Progress'])
   480→        
   481→        # Get recent errors
   482→        error_cutoff = now - timedelta(hours=1)
   483→        recent_errors = self.log_client.get_logs(
   484→            start_time=error_cutoff,
   485→            log_level='ERROR',
   486→            limit=50
   487→        )[:10]
   488→        
   489→        return SystemHealth(
   490→            timestamp=now,
   491→            workers_active=workers_active,
   492→            workers_spawning=workers_spawning,
   493→            workers_healthy=workers_healthy,
   494→            tasks_queued=tasks_queued,
   495→            tasks_in_progress=tasks_in_progress,
   496→            recent_errors=recent_errors,
   497→            failure_rate=None,
   498→            failure_rate_status='OK'
   499→        )
   500→    
   501→    # ==================== ORCHESTRATOR STATUS ====================
   502→    
   503→    def get_orchestrator_status(self, hours: int = 1) -> OrchestratorStatus:
   504→        """Get orchestrator status from logs."""
   505→        cutoff = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(hours=hours)
   506→        
   507→        # Get recent orchestrator logs
   508→        logs = self.log_client.get_logs(
   509→            start_time=cutoff,
   510→            source_type='orchestrator_gpu',
   511→            limit=1000,
   512→            order_desc=True
   513→        )
   514→        
   515→        # Find last activity and cycle
   516→        last_activity = None
   517→        last_cycle = None
   518→        
   519→        if logs:
   520→            last_activity = datetime.fromisoformat(logs[0]['timestamp'].replace('Z', '+00:00'))
   521→            last_cycle = logs[0].get('cycle_number')
   522→        
   523→        # Determine status
   524→        if last_activity:
   525→            age_minutes = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - last_activity).total_seconds() / 60
   526→            if age_minutes < 5:
   527→                status = 'HEALTHY'
   528→            elif age_minutes < 15:
   529→                status = 'WARNING'
   530→            else:
   531→                status = 'STALE'
   532→        else:
   533→            status = 'NO_LOGS'
   534→        
   535→        # Extract cycle information
   536→        cycle_starts = [log for log in logs if 'Starting orchestrator cycle' in log.get('message', '')]
   537→        recent_cycles = []
   538→        
   539→        for log in cycle_starts[:10]:
   540→            recent_cycles.append({
   541→                'cycle_number': log.get('cycle_number'),
   542→                'timestamp': log['timestamp'],
   543→                'message': log['message']
   544→            })
   545→        
   546→        return OrchestratorStatus(
   547→            last_activity=last_activity,
   548→            last_cycle=last_cycle,
   549→            status=status,
   550→            recent_cycles=recent_cycles,
   551→            recent_logs=logs[:50]
   552→        )
   553→

     3→from dataclasses import dataclass

     7→
     8→@dataclass
     9→class TaskInfo:
    10→    """Complete task information."""
    11→    task_id: str
    12→    state: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]
    13→    logs: List[Dict[str, Any]]
    14→    child_task_info: Optional['TaskInfo'] = None  # For cascaded failures
    15→    child_tasks: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None  # All child tasks for orchestrators
    16→    
    17→    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    18→        result = {
    19→            'task_id': self.task_id,
    20→            'state': self.state,
    21→            'logs': self.logs
    22→        }
    23→        if self.child_task_info:
    24→            result['child_task_info'] = self.child_task_info.to_dict()
    25→        if self.child_tasks:
    26→            result['child_tasks'] = self.child_tasks
    27→        return result
    28→
    29→
    30→@dataclass
    31→class WorkerInfo:
    32→    """Complete worker information."""
    33→    worker_id: str
    34→    state: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]
    35→    logs: List[Dict[str, Any]]
    36→    tasks: List[Dict[str, Any]]
    37→    
    38→    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    39→        return {
    40→            'worker_id': self.worker_id,
    41→            'state': self.state,
    42→            'logs': self.logs,
    43→            'tasks': self.tasks
    44→        }
    45→
    46→
    47→@dataclass
    48→class TasksSummary:

1→"""
     2→Join Clips Orchestrator - Main handler for sequentially joining video clips.
     3→
     4→Takes a list of video clips and creates a chain of join_clips_child
     5→tasks to progressively build them into a single seamless video.
     6→
     7→__all__ is defined to ensure underscore-prefixed names are exported via import *.
     8→
     9→Pattern:
    10→    Input: [clip_A, clip_B, clip_C, clip_D]
    11→
    12→    Creates:
    13→        join_0: clip_A + clip_B -> AB.mp4 (no dependency)
    14→        join_1: AB.mp4 + clip_C -> ABC.mp4 (depends on join_0)
    15→        join_2: ABC.mp4 + clip_D -> ABCD.mp4 (depends on join_1)
    16→"""
    17→
    18→import json
    19→import subprocess
    20→from pathlib import Path
    21→from typing import Tuple, List, Optional
    22→
    23→from source import db_operations as db_ops
    24→from source.utils import download_video_if_url, upload_intermediate_file_to_storage
    25→from source.media.video import reverse_video
    26→from source.core.log import orchestrator_logger
    27→
    28→from source.task_handlers.join.shared import (
    29→    _check_existing_join_tasks,
    30→    _extract_join_settings_from_payload)
    31→from source.task_handlers.join.clip_validator import validate_clip_frames_for_join
    32→from source.task_handlers.join.task_builder import (
    33→    _create_join_chain_tasks,
    34→    _create_parallel_join_tasks)
    35→from source.task_handlers.join.vlm_enhancement import (
    36→    _extract_boundary_frames_for_vlm,
    37→    _generate_vlm_prompts_for_joins)
    38→
    39→__all__ = ["_get_video_resolution", "_handle_join_clips_orchestrator_task"]
    40→
    41→def _get_video_resolution(video_path: str | Path) -> Tuple[int, int] | None:
    42→    """
    43→    Get video resolution (width, height) using ffprobe.
    44→
    45→    Returns:
    46→        (width, height) tuple, or None if detection fails
    47→    """
    48→    try:
    49→        probe_cmd = [
    50→            'ffprobe', '-v', 'error',
    51→            '-select_streams', 'v:0',
    52→            '-show_entries', 'stream=width,height',
    53→            '-of', 'csv=p=0',
    54→            str(video_path)
    55→        ]
    56→        result = subprocess.run(probe_cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
    57→        if result.returncode != 0:
    58→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[GET_RESOLUTION] ffprobe failed: {result.stderr}")
    59→            return None
    60→        width_str, height_str = result.stdout.strip().split(',')
    61→        return (int(width_str), int(height_str))
    62→    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError, ValueError) as e:
    63→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[GET_RESOLUTION] Error detecting resolution: {e}")
    64→        return None
    65→
    66→def _handle_join_clips_orchestrator_task(
    67→    task_params_from_db: dict,
    68→    main_output_dir_base: Path,
    69→    orchestrator_task_id_str: str,
    70→    orchestrator_project_id: str | None) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    71→    """
    72→    Handle join_clips_orchestrator task - creates chained join_clips_child tasks.
    73→
    74→    Args:
    75→        task_params_from_db: Task parameters containing orchestrator_details
    76→        main_output_dir_base: Base output directory
    77→        orchestrator_task_id_str: Orchestrator task ID
    78→        orchestrator_project_id: Project ID for authorization
    79→
    80→    Returns:
    81→        (success: bool, message: str)
    82→    """
    83→    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Starting orchestrator task {orchestrator_task_id_str}")
    84→
    85→    try:
    86→        # === 1. PARSE ORCHESTRATOR PAYLOAD ===
    87→        if 'orchestrator_details' not in task_params_from_db:
    88→            orchestrator_logger.debug("[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] ERROR: orchestrator_details missing")
    89→            return False, "orchestrator_details missing"
    90→
    91→        orchestrator_payload = task_params_from_db['orchestrator_details']
    92→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Orchestrator payload keys: {list(orchestrator_payload.keys())}")
    93→
    94→        # Extract required fields
    95→        clip_list = orchestrator_payload.get("clip_list", [])
    96→        run_id = orchestrator_payload.get("run_id")
    97→        loop_first_clip = orchestrator_payload.get("loop_first_clip", False)
    98→
    99→        # === DYNAMIC CLIP_LIST FROM SEGMENT TASKS ===
   100→        segment_task_ids = orchestrator_payload.get("segment_task_ids", [])
   101→        if segment_task_ids and not clip_list:
   102→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Building clip_list from {len(segment_task_ids)} segment task outputs")
   103→
   104→            built_clip_list = []
   105→            for i, task_id in enumerate(segment_task_ids):
   106→                output_url = db_ops.get_task_output_location_from_db(task_id)
   107→                if not output_url:
   108→                    return False, f"Segment task {task_id} has no output_location (may not be complete)"
   109→
   110→                if output_url.startswith('{'):
   111→                    try:
   112→                        output_data = json.loads(output_url)
   113→                        output_url = output_data.get("output_location") or output_data.get("url") or output_url
   114→                    except json.JSONDecodeError:
   115→                        pass
   116→
   117→                built_clip_list.append({
   118→                    "url": output_url,
   119→                    "name": f"Segment {i + 1}"
   120→                })
   121→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR]   Segment {i}: {output_url[:80]}...")
   122→
   123→            clip_list = built_clip_list
   124→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Built clip_list with {len(clip_list)} clips from segment outputs")
   125→
   126→        if not run_id:
   127→            return False, "run_id is required"
   128→
   129→        # Handle loop_first_clip: reverse the first clip and use it as the second clip
   130→        if loop_first_clip:
   131→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] loop_first_clip=True - will reverse first clip to create looping effect")
   132→
   133→            if not clip_list or len(clip_list) < 1:
   134→                return False, "clip_list must contain at least 1 clip when loop_first_clip=True"
   135→
   136→            loop_temp_dir = Path(main_output_dir_base) / f"join_clips_run_{run_id}" / "loop_temp"
   137→            loop_temp_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   138→
   139→            first_clip = clip_list[0]
   140→            first_clip_url = first_clip.get("url")
   141→            first_clip_name = first_clip.get("name", "clip_0")
   142→
   143→            if not first_clip_url:
   144→                return False, "First clip in clip_list is missing 'url' field"
   145→
   146→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Downloading first clip for reversal: {first_clip_url[:80]}...")
   147→            local_first_clip_path = download_video_if_url(
   148→                first_clip_url,
   149→                download_target_dir=loop_temp_dir,
   150→                task_id_for_logging=orchestrator_task_id_str,
   151→                descriptive_name="first_clip_for_loop"
   152→            )
   153→
   154→            if not local_first_clip_path or not Path(local_first_clip_path).exists():
   155→                return False, f"Failed to download first clip: {first_clip_url}"
   156→
   157→            reversed_clip_path = loop_temp_dir / f"{first_clip_name}_reversed.mp4"
   158→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Reversing first clip...")
   159→
   160→            reversed_path = reverse_video(
   161→                local_first_clip_path,
   162→                reversed_clip_path)
   163→
   164→            if not reversed_path or not reversed_path.exists():
   165→                return False, f"Failed to reverse first clip: {local_first_clip_path}"
   166→
   167→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] \u2705 Created reversed clip locally: {reversed_path}")
   168→
   169→            reversed_filename = f"{first_clip_name}_reversed.mp4"
   170→            reversed_url = upload_intermediate_file_to_storage(
   171→                local_file_path=reversed_path,
   172→                task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str,
   173→                filename=reversed_filename)
   174→
   175→            if not reversed_url:
   176→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] \u26a0\ufe0f  Upload failed, using local path (may fail on multi-worker)")
   177→                reversed_url = str(reversed_path.resolve())
   178→            else:
   179→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] \u2705 Reversed clip uploaded: {reversed_url}")
   180→
   181→            reversed_clip_dict = {
   182→                "url": reversed_url,
   183→                "name": f"{first_clip_name}_reversed"
   184→            }
   185→
   186→            clip_list = [first_clip, reversed_clip_dict]
   187→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] clip_list overridden: [{first_clip_name}, {reversed_clip_dict['name']}]")
   188→
   189→        if not clip_list or len(clip_list) < 2:
   190→            return False, "clip_list must contain at least 2 clips"
   191→
   192→        num_joins = len(clip_list) - 1
   193→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Processing {len(clip_list)} clips = {num_joins} join tasks")
   194→
   195→        # === EARLY IDEMPOTENCY CHECK (before expensive VLM work) ===
   196→        idempotency_check = _check_existing_join_tasks(orchestrator_task_id_str, num_joins)
   197→        if idempotency_check is not None:
   198→            return idempotency_check
   199→
   200→        # Extract join settings using shared helper
   201→        join_settings = _extract_join_settings_from_payload(orchestrator_payload)
   202→        per_join_settings = orchestrator_payload.get("per_join_settings", [])
   203→        output_base_dir = orchestrator_payload.get("output_base_dir", str(main_output_dir_base.resolve()))
   204→
   205→        # Create run-specific output directory
   206→        current_run_output_dir = Path(output_base_dir) / f"join_clips_run_{run_id}"
   207→        current_run_output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   208→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Run output directory: {current_run_output_dir}")
   209→
   210→        # === VALIDATE CLIP FRAME COUNTS (optional, enabled by default) ===
   211→        skip_validation = orchestrator_payload.get("skip_frame_validation", False)
   212→        if not skip_validation:
   213→            validation_temp_dir = current_run_output_dir / "validation_temp"
   214→            validation_temp_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   215→
   216→            is_valid, validation_message, frame_counts = validate_clip_frames_for_join(
   217→                clip_list=clip_list,
   218→                gap_frame_count=join_settings.get("gap_frame_count", 53),
   219→                context_frame_count=join_settings.get("context_frame_count", 8),
   220→                replace_mode=join_settings.get("replace_mode", False),
   221→                temp_dir=validation_temp_dir,
   222→                orchestrator_task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str)
   223→
   224→            if not is_valid:
   225→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] VALIDATION FAILED: {validation_message}")
   226→                return False, f"Clip frame validation failed: {validation_message}"
   227→
   228→            if validation_message:
   229→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] {validation_message}")
   230→
   231→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Clip frame validation complete, frame counts: {frame_counts}")
   232→        else:
   233→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Frame validation skipped (skip_frame_validation=True)")
   234→
   235→        # === DETECT RESOLUTION FROM INPUT VIDEO (when use_input_video_resolution=True) ===
   236→        if join_settings.get("use_input_video_resolution", False):
   237→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] use_input_video_resolution=True, detecting resolution from first clip...")
   238→
   239→            first_clip_url = clip_list[0].get("url")
   240→            if first_clip_url:
   241→                resolution_temp_dir = current_run_output_dir / "resolution_temp"
   242→                resolution_temp_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   243→
   244→                local_first_clip = download_video_if_url(
   245→                    first_clip_url,
   246→                    download_target_dir=resolution_temp_dir,
   247→                    task_id_for_logging=orchestrator_task_id_str,
   248→                    descriptive_name="detect_resolution"
   249→                )
   250→
   251→                if local_first_clip and Path(local_first_clip).exists():
   252→                    detected_res = _get_video_resolution(local_first_clip)
   253→                    if detected_res:
   254→                        join_settings["resolution"] = list(detected_res)
   255→                        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] \u2713 Detected resolution from input video: {detected_res}")
   256→                    else:
   257→                        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] \u26a0 Could not detect resolution, segments will detect from frames")
   258→                else:
   259→                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] \u26a0 Could not download first clip for resolution detection")
   260→
   261→        # === VLM PROMPT ENHANCEMENT (optional) ===
   262→        enhance_prompt = orchestrator_payload.get("enhance_prompt", False)
   263→        vlm_enhanced_prompts: List[Optional[str]] = [None] * num_joins
   264→
   265→        if enhance_prompt:
   266→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] enhance_prompt=True, generating VLM-enhanced prompts for {num_joins} joins")
   267→
   268→            vlm_device = orchestrator_payload.get("vlm_device", "cuda")
   269→            vlm_temp_dir = current_run_output_dir / "vlm_temp"
   270→            vlm_temp_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   271→            base_prompt = join_settings.get("prompt", "")
   272→
   273→            try:
   274→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Extracting 4 frames per join from {len(clip_list)} clips...")
   275→                image_quads = _extract_boundary_frames_for_vlm(
   276→                    clip_list=clip_list,
   277→                    temp_dir=vlm_temp_dir,
   278→                    orchestrator_task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str)
   279→
   280→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Running VLM batch on {len(image_quads)} quads (4 images each)...")
   281→                vlm_enhanced_prompts = _generate_vlm_prompts_for_joins(
   282→                    image_quads=image_quads,
   283→                    base_prompt=base_prompt,
   284→                    vlm_device=vlm_device)
   285→
   286→                valid_count = sum(1 for p in vlm_enhanced_prompts if p is not None)
   287→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] VLM enhancement complete: {valid_count}/{num_joins} prompts generated")
   288→
   289→            except (RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as vlm_error:
   290→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] VLM enhancement failed, using base prompts: {vlm_error}", exc_info=True)
   291→                vlm_enhanced_prompts = [None] * num_joins
   292→        else:
   293→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] enhance_prompt=False, using base prompt for all joins")
   294→
   295→        # === CREATE JOIN TASKS ===
   296→        use_parallel = orchestrator_payload.get("use_parallel_joins", True)
   297→
   298→        parent_generation_id = (
   299→            task_params_from_db.get("parent_generation_id")
   300→            or orchestrator_payload.get("parent_generation_id")
   301→        )
   302→
   303→        if use_parallel:
   304→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Using PARALLEL pattern (transitions in parallel + final stitch)")
   305→            success, message = _create_parallel_join_tasks(
   306→                clip_list=clip_list,
   307→                run_id=run_id,
   308→                join_settings=join_settings,
   309→                per_join_settings=per_join_settings,
   310→                vlm_enhanced_prompts=vlm_enhanced_prompts,
   311→                current_run_output_dir=current_run_output_dir,
   312→                orchestrator_task_id_str=orchestrator_task_id_str,
   313→                orchestrator_project_id=orchestrator_project_id,
   314→                orchestrator_payload=orchestrator_payload,
   315→                parent_generation_id=parent_generation_id)
   316→        else:
   317→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Using CHAIN pattern (legacy sequential)")
   318→            success, message = _create_join_chain_tasks(
   319→                clip_list=clip_list,
   320→                run_id=run_id,
   321→                join_settings=join_settings,
   322→                per_join_settings=per_join_settings,
   323→                vlm_enhanced_prompts=vlm_enhanced_prompts,
   324→                current_run_output_dir=current_run_output_dir,
   325→                orchestrator_task_id_str=orchestrator_task_id_str,
   326→                orchestrator_project_id=orchestrator_project_id,
   327→                orchestrator_payload=orchestrator_payload,
   328→                parent_generation_id=parent_generation_id)
   329→
   330→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] {message}")
   331→        return success, message
   332→
   333→    except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError, KeyError, TypeError) as e:
   334→        msg = f"Failed during join orchestration: {e}"
   335→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] ERROR: {msg}", exc_info=True)
   336→        return False, msg
   337→
   338→
   339→# Public alias for cross-module use.
   340→handle_join_clips_orchestrator_task = _handle_join_clips_orchestrator_task
   341→
