### **2. VIDEO PREPROCESSING UTILITIES**

#### **`ensure_video_fps(input_video_path, target_fps, output_dir=None, fps_tolerance=0.5)`**
**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/media/video/ffmpeg_ops.py`, lines 25-116

**Purpose:** Ensure video is at target FPS, resampling if necessary

**Signature:**
```python
def ensure_video_fps(
    input_video_path: str | Path,
    target_fps: float,
    output_dir: str | Path | None = None,
    fps_tolerance: float = 0.5,
) -> Path:
```

**Returns:** `Path` to video at target FPS
- Returns original path if already within tolerance
- Returns resampled video path if needed (saved as `{stem}_resampled_{fps}fps.mp4`)

**Raises:**
- `OSError`: Source video missing, or resampled output missing/empty
- `RuntimeError`: FFmpeg failed or timed out (600s limit)
- `ValueError`: Could not determine video FPS

**Implementation:** Uses FFmpeg's `fps` filter (not `-r` output option) for frame-accurate resampling based on timestamps

**Example:**
```python
video_16fps = ensure_video_fps(downloaded_video, 16, work_dir)
```

#### **`standardize_video_aspect_ratio(input_video_path, output_video_path, target_aspect_ratio, task_id_for_logging="")`**
**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/media/video/video_transforms.py`, lines 147-265

**Purpose:** Standardize video to target aspect ratio by center-cropping

**Signature:**
```python
def standardize_video_aspect_ratio(
    input_video_path: str | Path,
    output_video_path: str | Path,
    target_aspect_ratio: str,
    task_id_for_logging: str = ""
) -> Path | None:
```

**Returns:** `Path` to standardized video, or `None` on failure

**Key behavior:**
- Parses aspect ratio as string: `"16:9"`, `"9:16"`, `"1:1"`, `"4:3"`, `"21:9"`, or custom `"W:H"`
- Uses FFmpeg's `crop` filter for center-crop (maintains quality vs. scaling)
- If aspect ratio already matches (within 1%), copies file without re-encoding
- Handles audio with `-c:a copy` (audio stream passed through unchanged)

**Implementation steps:**
1. Get input dimensions via `ffprobe`
2. Calculate crop region (center-crop)
3. Apply `crop={new_w}:{new_h}:{crop_x}:{crop_y}` filter

---

### **3. ASPECT RATIO AUTO-DETECTION IN JOIN CLIPS** (`source/task_handlers/join/generation.py`)

**Location:** Lines 264-365 (when `aspect_ratio` param is `None`)

**Auto-detection logic:**
```python
if aspect_ratio:
    # Use explicit aspect_ratio param
    ...
else:
    # No explicit aspect ratio specified - auto-detect
    # Get dimensions of both videos using ffprobe
    start_w, start_h = get_video_dimensions(starting_video)
    end_w, end_h = get_video_dimensions(ending_video)
    
    start_aspect = start_w / start_h
    end_aspect = end_w / end_h
    
    # If aspect ratios differ by > 1%, standardize ending to match starting
    if abs(start_aspect - end_aspect) > 0.01:
        # Detect common aspect ratios or use exact dimensions
        if abs(start_aspect - 16/9) < 0.01:
            auto_aspect_ratio = "16:9"
        elif abs(start_aspect - 9/16) < 0.01:
            auto_aspect_ratio = "9:16"
        elif abs(start_aspect - 1.0) < 0.01:
            auto_aspect_ratio = "1:1"
        elif abs(start_aspect - 4/3) < 0.01:
            auto_aspect_ratio = "4:3"
        elif abs(start_aspect - 21/9) < 0.01:
            auto_aspect_ratio = "21:9"
        else:
            # Fallback: use exact dimensions
            auto_aspect_ratio = f"{start_w}:{start_h}"
        
        # Standardize ending_video to match
        standardize_video_aspect_ratio(
            ending_video, 
            standardized_end_path,
            auto_aspect_ratio,
            task_id_for_logging=task_id)
```

**Key features:**
- Compares starting and ending video aspect ratios
- Uses common aspect ratio constants (`16:9`, `9:16`, `1:1`, etc.) with fuzzy matching (±1%)
- Falls back to exact dimensions if no common match
- Only standardizes ending video if difference > 1%

---

### **4. DOWNLOAD UTILITIES** (`source/utils/download_utils.py`)

#### **`download_video_if_url(video_url_or_path, download_target_dir, task_id_for_logging=None, descriptive_name=None)`**
**Location:** Lines 236-251

**Signature:**
```python
def download_video_if_url(
    video_url_or_path: str,
    download_target_dir: Path | str | None,
    task_id_for_logging: str | None = "generic_task",
    descriptive_name: str | None = None
) -> str:
```

**Returns:** 
- Local file path (string) if downloaded from URL
- Original input string if not a URL or download failed

**Behavior:**
- Checks if input is HTTP/HTTPS URL
- If yes and `download_target_dir` provided: downloads to unique path
- If not URL or no download dir: returns original string unchanged
- 10-minute timeout for large videos

**File naming:**
- Uses `_get_unique_target_path()` to generate unique filename
- Format: `{base_name}_{HHMMSS}_{random_6_hex}.mp4`

#### **`download_image_if_url(image_url_or_path, download_target_dir, task_id_for_logging=None, debug_mode=False, descriptive_name=None)`**
**Location:** Lines 253-271

**Same interface as above but with:**
- 5-minute timeout (for images, smaller files)
- Default extension: `.jpg`
- Default stem: `"image"`

#### **`_download_file_if_url()` (Generic internal function)**
**Location:** Lines 161-234

**Signature:**
```python
def _download_file_if_url(
    file_url_or_path: str,
    download_target_dir: Path | str | None,
    task_id_for_logging: str | None = "generic_task",
    descriptive_name: str | None = None,
    default_extension: str = ".jpg",
```

- Catches: `requests.exceptions.RequestException`, `OSError`

#### **Resolution Utilities** (`source/utils/resolution_utils.py`)

- **`parse_resolution(res_str: str)`** → `tuple[int, int]`

### **COMPLETE FLOW EXAMPLE**

Here's how these utilities work together in practice:

```python
# 1. Download if URL
video_path = download_video_if_url(
    "https://example.com/video.mp4",
    download_target_dir="/tmp/videos",
    task_id_for_logging="task_123"
)

# 2. Ensure consistent FPS
video_path = ensure_video_fps(
    video_path,
    target_fps=16,
    output_dir="/tmp/videos"
)

# 3. Standardize aspect ratio
video_path = standardize_video_aspect_ratio(
    video_path,
    "/tmp/output.mp4",
    target_aspect_ratio="16:9"
)

# 4. Prepare for upload
local_save_path, db_location = prepare_output_path_with_upload(
    task_id="task_123",
    filename="final_output.mp4",
    main_output_dir_base=Path("/outputs"),
    task_type="join_clips"
)

# 5. Get final output location (edge function will upload)
final_location = upload_and_get_final_output_location(
    local_save_path,
    db_location
)

# 6. For intermediate files needing immediate upload
public_url = upload_intermediate_file_to_storage(
    local_save_path,
    task_id="task_123",
    filename="reversed_video.mp4"
)
```

---

### **KEY TAKEAWAYS**

1. **Upload flow:** `prepare_output_path_with_upload()` → save file locally → `upload_and_get_final_output_location()` → edge function handles upload
2. **Video standardization:** Auto-detect aspect ratio by comparing starting/ending frames, then center-crop to match
3. **FPS handling:** `ensure_video_fps()` uses FFmpeg `fps` filter for frame-accurate resampling (critical for frame-indexed operations)
4. **Downloads:** Graceful fallback—returns original URL/path on any download failure
5. **Aspect ratio detection:** Fuzzy matching (±1%) for common ratios, exact dimension fallback

## Comprehensive Exploration Report: Join Clips Orchestrator Architecture

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's the complete picture of how the join clips orchestrator works:

---

### 1. **ORCHESTRATOR FLOW** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/task_handlers/join/orchestrator.py`)

**Function:** `_handle_join_clips_orchestrator_task(task_params_from_db, main_output_dir_base, orchestrator_task_id_str, orchestrator_project_id) -> Tuple[bool, str]`

**Key Flow Steps:**

1. **Clip List Resolution** (lines 95-124):
   - Extracts `clip_list` from `orchestrator_payload`
   - If `segment_task_ids` provided and `clip_list` is empty, dynamically builds clip_list from segment outputs:
     - Calls `db_ops.get_task_output_location_from_db(task_id)` for each segment
     - Parses JSON if output is JSON-encoded
     - Creates clip dict with `{"url": output_url, "name": f"Segment {i+1}"}`

2. **Optional Loop First Clip** (lines 130-187):
   - If `loop_first_clip=True`:
     - Downloads first clip with `download_video_if_url()`
     - Reverses it using `reverse_video()`
     - Uploads reversed clip with `upload_intermediate_file_to_storage()`
     - Replaces clip_list with `[first_clip, reversed_clip_dict]`

3. **Frame Validation** (lines 210-233):
   - Calls `validate_clip_frames_for_join()` unless `skip_frame_validation=True`
   - Validates frame counts for safe joining
   - Returns proportional reduction recommendations if clips are too short

4. **Resolution Detection** (lines 235-259):
   - If `use_input_video_resolution=True`:
     - Downloads first clip with `download_video_if_url()`
     - Calls internal `_get_video_resolution()` which uses ffprobe
     - Sets `join_settings["resolution"]` to detected `[width, height]`

5. **VLM Enhancement** (lines 262-293):
   - If `enhance_prompt=True`:
     - Extracts 4 boundary frames per join with `_extract_boundary_frames_for_vlm()`
     - Generates enhanced prompts with `_generate_vlm_prompts_for_joins()`
     - Returns list of `vlm_enhanced_prompts` (or None for each join on failure)

6. **Task Creation** (lines 296-328):
   - Chooses between two patterns:
     - **PARALLEL** (default, `use_parallel_joins=True`): Creates N-1 transition tasks independently
     - **CHAIN** (legacy): Creates sequential join tasks with dependencies

---

### 2. **TASK BUILDER** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/task_handlers/join/task_builder.py`)

#### **Parallel Pattern:** `_create_parallel_join_tasks(...) -> Tuple[bool, str]`

**Phase 1: Create Transition Tasks (lines 195-244)**
- For each pair of clips (N-1 transitions):
  - Build `transition_payload` with:
    - `starting_video_path`: clip_start URL
    - `ending_video_path`: clip_end URL
    - `transition_only: True`
    - Merged settings (`task_join_settings`)
    - VLM-enhanced prompt if available
  - Call `db_ops.add_task_to_db(payload, "join_clips_segment", dependant_on=None)`
  - Collect `trans_task_id` in `transition_task_ids` list
  - **Key:** No dependencies between transitions (all run in parallel)

**Phase 2: Create Final Stitch (lines 246-278)**
- Creates single `join_final_stitch` task:
  - `transition_task_ids`: List of ALL transition task IDs
  - Depends on ALL transitions: `db_ops.add_task_to_db(payload, "join_final_stitch", dependant_on=transition_task_ids)`
  - Receives full `clip_list` for ordering

#### **Chain Pattern:** `_create_join_chain_tasks(...) -> Tuple[bool, str]`

**Sequential Dependency Chain (lines 63-116)**
- For each join (N-1 tasks):
  - Build `join_payload` with clip URLs
  - Call `db_ops.add_task_to_db(payload, "join_clips_segment", dependant_on=previous_join_task_id)`
  - Each join depends on previous one: `previous_join_task_id = actual_db_row_id`

**Final Stitch (lines 119-146)**
- Depends on last join: `db_ops.add_task_to_db(payload, "join_final_stitch", dependant_on=previous_join_task_id)`

---

### 3. **CLIP VALIDATION** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/task_handlers/join/clip_validator.py`)

**Function:** `validate_clip_frames_for_join(clip_list, gap_frame_count, context_frame_count, replace_mode, temp_dir, orchestrator_task_id, **_kwargs) -> Tuple[bool, str, List[int]]`

**Returns:**
- `Tuple[is_valid: bool, error_message: str, frame_counts: List[int]]`

**Frame Validation Logic:**

1. **Download & Count** (lines 72-93):
   - For each clip:
     - `download_video_if_url(clip_url, temp_dir)` → local path
     - `get_video_frame_count_and_fps(local_path)` → (frames, fps)
     - Appends frames to `frame_counts`

2. **Minimum Frame Calculation** (lines 19-40):
   - `calculate_min_clip_frames(gap_frame_count, context_frame_count, replace_mode) -> int`
   - REPLACE mode: `gap_frame_count + 2 × context_frame_count`
   - Non-REPLACE mode: `2 × context_frame_count`

3. **Per-Clip Requirements** (lines 96-107):
   - **First & Last clips:** Only need half minimum (one boundary)
   - **Middle clips:** Need full minimum
   - If REPLACE mode, gaps are split between boundaries

4. **Violation Handling** (lines 117-137):
   - If violations exist, calculates proportional reduction:
     - `ratio = min_available / total_needed`
     - Returns `(True, warning_msg, frame_counts)` — still succeeds but warns
   - Suggests: `reduced_gap = max(1, int(gap_frame_count * ratio))`

---

### 4. **FPS NORMALIZATION** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/media/video/ffmpeg_ops.py`)

**Function:** `ensure_video_fps(input_video_path, target_fps, output_dir=None, fps_tolerance=0.5) -> Path`

**Signature:**
```python
def ensure_video_fps(
    input_video_path: str | Path,
    target_fps: float,
    output_dir: str | Path | None = None,
    fps_tolerance: float = 0.5,
) -> Path
```

**Returns:**
- `Path` to video at target FPS (original if already correct, resampled if not)

**Behavior:**
- Gets actual FPS with `get_video_frame_count_and_fps()`
- If `|actual_fps - target_fps| <= fps_tolerance` (default 0.5), returns original path
- Otherwise resamples using ffmpeg: `ffmpeg -vf fps={target_fps}`
- Output: `{stem}_resampled_{int(target_fps)}fps.mp4`
- Uses frame-based resampling (fps filter) for accurate frame indices

**Exceptions:** Raises `OSError`, `ValueError`, `RuntimeError` on failures

---

### 5. **ASPECT RATIO STANDARDIZATION** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/media/video/video_transforms.py`)

**Function:** `standardize_video_aspect_ratio(input_video_path, output_video_path, target_aspect_ratio, task_id_for_logging="") -> Path | None`

**Signature:**
```python
def standardize_video_aspect_ratio(
    input_video_path: str | Path,
    output_video_path: str | Path,
    target_aspect_ratio: str,  # e.g., "16:9", "9:16", "1:1", "4:3"
    task_id_for_logging: str = "") -> Path | None
```

**Returns:**
- `Path` to standardized video, or `None` on failure

**Process:**
1. Parse aspect ratio string: `"16:9"` → 16/9 = 1.778
2. Get input dimensions with ffprobe: `width, height`
3. Check if already matches (within 1% tolerance): `|src_aspect - target_aspect| < 0.01`
   - If yes, just copy file (fast path)
4. Calculate center-crop box:
   - If source wider than target: crop width, center horizontally
   - If source taller than target: crop height, center vertically
5. Apply ffmpeg crop filter: `ffmpeg -vf crop={new_w}:{new_h}:{crop_x}:{crop_y}`
6. Returns output path or None on failure

---

### 6. **AUTO-DETECTION (Aspect Ratio Fallback)** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP/source/task_handlers/join/generation.py`, lines 296-361)

**When no explicit aspect ratio specified:**

1. Gets dimensions of both videos (starting & ending) with inline ffprobe
2. Calculates aspect ratios: `start_aspect = start_w / start_h`
3. If aspect ratios differ by > 1%:
   - Maps to common aspect ratios:
     - `16/9 ≈ 1.778` → `"16:9"`
     - `9/16 ≈ 0.563` → `"9:16"`
     - `1.0` → `"1:1"`
     - `4/3 ≈ 1.333` → `"4:3"`
     - `21/9 ≈ 2.333` → `"21:9"`
     - Otherwise: `f"{start_w}:{start_h}"` (exact dimensions as ratio)
   - Standardizes ending video to match starting video
4. Returns `(True, warning, frame_counts)` even if standardization fails (non-blocking)

---

```python
    filename: str) -> str | None
```

**Returns:** Public URL string (e.g., `{SUPABASE_URL}/storage/v1/object/public/image_uploads/{storage_path}`) or `None` on failure

**Process:**
1. POST to edge function `generate-upload-url`:
   - Body: `{"task_id": task_id, "filename": filename, "content_type": content_type}`
   - Returns: `{"upload_url": "...", "storage_path": "..."}`
2. PUT file to signed URL with Content-Type header (retry logic for 502/503/504)
3. Constructs public URL from storage_path
4. Max 3 retries with exponential backoff (2^attempt)

3. Returns only valid values (>0)

---

## Key Architectural Insights

1. **Clip URLs Flow:**
   - Orchestrator gets clip_list URLs from payload OR dynamically from segment task outputs
   - URLs passed directly to child tasks in `starting_video_path` and `ending_video_path`
   - Intermediate files (reversed videos, preprocessed clips) uploaded to Supabase for cross-worker access

2. **Validation Strategy:**
   - Frame validation is **non-blocking** — warnings suggest reduced parameters but don't fail
   - Aspect ratio auto-detection happens **only when no explicit ratio provided**
   - FPS tolerance is **flexible** (±0.5fps by default)

3. **Parallel vs. Chain:**
   - **Parallel** avoids re-encoding (quality preservation)
   - **Chain** legacy pattern with sequential dependencies
   - Both converge to `join_final_stitch` task that receives full clip_list for ordering

4. **Error Handling:**
   - Aspect ratio standardization failure is non-blocking (warnings logged)
   - VLM enhancement failure is non-blocking (falls back to base prompt)
   - Upload failures for intermediate files fall back to local paths (single-worker only)

I need to design an implementation plan for lifting clip preprocessing (FPS normalization + aspect ratio standardization) into the join clips orchestrator. Here's the full context:

## Problem
The parallel join architecture has a bug: the `join_clips_segment` task preprocesses clips (FPS normalization, aspect ratio cropping) before generating transitions, then records frame counts/indices against preprocessed clips. But `join_final_stitch` downloads the raw original clips and tries to use those frame indices — causing crashes when clips have different FPS or aspect ratios.

Example: A clip at 24fps/768x512 (122 frames) gets preprocessed by the segment to 16fps/512x512 (81 frames). The transition records `clip2_total_frames: 81`. Final stitch downloads the original (122 frames) and fails: `Frame count mismatch too large: expected 104, got 71`.

## Solution
Move preprocessing to the orchestrator so both segments and final stitch receive already-preprocessed clip URLs. The orchestrator already downloads clips for validation — we'll extend this to preprocess, validate preprocessed clips, and upload preprocessed versions to Supabase for cross-worker access.

## Key existing utilities (all in /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Headless-Wan2GP):

1. **`ensure_video_fps(input_video_path, target_fps, output_dir, fps_tolerance=0.5) -> Path`** in `source/media/video/ffmpeg_ops.py`
   - Returns original path if already at target FPS, resampled path otherwise
   - Raises OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError

2. **`standardize_video_aspect_ratio(input_video_path, output_video_path, target_aspect_ratio, task_id_for_logging) -> Path | None`** in `source/media/video/video_transforms.py`
   - Center-crops to target aspect ratio. Returns Path or None on failure.
   - target_aspect_ratio is a string like "16:9", "1:1", etc.

3. **`upload_intermediate_file_to_storage(local_file_path, task_id, filename) -> str | None`** in `source/utils/output_path_utils.py`
   - Uploads to Supabase and returns public URL. Returns None on failure.
   - Already imported in orchestrator.py

4. **`download_video_if_url(url, download_target_dir, task_id_for_logging, descriptive_name) -> str`** in `source/utils/download_utils.py`
   - Already imported in orchestrator.py

5. **`_get_video_resolution(video_path) -> (int, int) | None`** in orchestrator.py
   - Uses ffprobe. Already exists in the orchestrator.

6. **`validate_clip_frames_for_join(clip_list, gap_frame_count, context_frame_count, replace_mode, temp_dir, orchestrator_task_id) -> (bool, str, [int])`** in `clip_validator.py`
   - Downloads clips, counts frames, validates sufficiency

7. **Aspect ratio auto-detection** in `generation.py:296-365`:
   - Gets dimensions of both videos via ffprobe
   - If aspect ratios differ >1%, detects common ratio (16:9, 9:16, 1:1, etc.) from first clip
   - Standardizes second clip to match first

## Current orchestrator flow (orchestrator.py):
```
1. Parse payload, resolve clip_list
2. Handle loop_first_clip (download, reverse, upload, replace clip_list)
3. Idempotency check
4. Extract join_settings
5. Validate clip frame counts (downloads clips, counts frames)
6. Detect resolution from first clip (if use_input_video_resolution)
7. VLM enhancement (downloads clips for boundary frames)
8. Create child tasks (segments + final_stitch)
```

## Current task_builder.py parallel pattern:
- Segment tasks get `starting_video_path` and `ending_video_path` from `clip_list[idx].get("url")` 
- Final stitch gets full `clip_list`
- If orchestrator modifies `clip_list` before calling task builder, preprocessed URLs flow to all children

## Constraints:
- Preprocessed clips must be uploaded to Supabase (workers may be different machines)
- If upload fails, fall back to local path with warning (pattern from loop_first_clip)
- `use_input_video_fps=True` means skip FPS normalization
- Aspect ratio can be explicit (from join_settings) or auto-detected from first clip
- Frame validation should use preprocessed frame counts (more accurate)
- Keep generation.py's existing preprocessing as a no-op safety net (no changes to generation.py)
- Keep final_stitch.py unchanged (it already works correctly with consistent clips)

## Design requirements:
- Create a new file `source/task_handlers/join/clip_preprocessor.py` (consistent with existing clip_validator.py, vlm_enhancement.py)
- Single function that handles: download → FPS normalize → aspect ratio standardize → validate frames → upload → return new clip_list
- Fold the existing resolution detection (use_input_video_resolution) into this step since we already have the clips downloaded
- Remove the separate validation and resolution detection blocks from orchestrator.py, replaced by single preprocessing call

Please design a detailed implementation plan with:
1. The new clip_preprocessor.py file structure and function signature
2. Exact changes to orchestrator.py (what to add, what to remove)
3. Any import changes needed
4. Error handling approach
5. How the preprocessed clip_list flows through task_builder to segments and final_stitch
6. Edge cases to handle

```
   110→                if output_url.startswith('{'):
   111→                    try:
   112→                        output_data = json.loads(output_url)
   113→                        output_url = output_data.get("output_location") or output_data.get("url") or output_url
   114→                    except json.JSONDecodeError:
   115→                        pass
   116→
   117→                built_clip_list.append({
   118→                    "url": output_url,
   119→                    "name": f"Segment {i + 1}"
   120→                })
   121→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR]   Segment {i}: {output_url[:80]}...")
   122→
   123→            clip_list = built_clip_list
   124→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Built clip_list with {len(clip_list)} clips from segment outputs")
   125→
   126→        if not run_id:
   127→            return False, "run_id is required"
   128→
   129→        # Handle loop_first_clip: reverse the first clip and use it as the second clip
   130→        if loop_first_clip:
   131→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] loop_first_clip=True - will reverse first clip to create looping effect")
   132→
   133→            if not clip_list or len(clip_list) < 1:
   134→                return False, "clip_list must contain at least 1 clip when loop_first_clip=True"
   135→
   136→            loop_temp_dir = Path(main_output_dir_base) / f"join_clips_run_{run_id}" / "loop_temp"
   137→            loop_temp_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   138→
   139→            first_clip = clip_list[0]
   140→            first_clip_url = first_clip.get("url")
   141→            first_clip_name = first_clip.get("name", "clip_0")
   142→
   143→            if not first_clip_url:
   144→                return False, "First clip in clip_list is missing 'url' field"
   145→
   146→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Downloading first clip for reversal: {first_clip_url[:80]}...")
   147→            local_first_clip_path = download_video_if_url(
   148→                first_clip_url,
   149→                download_target_dir=loop_temp_dir,
   150→                task_id_for_logging=orchestrator_task_id_str,
   151→                descriptive_name="first_clip_for_loop"
   152→            )
   153→
   154→            if not local_first_clip_path or not Path(local_first_clip_path).exists():
   155→                return False, f"Failed to download first clip: {first_clip_url}"
   156→
   157→            reversed_clip_path = loop_temp_dir / f"{first_clip_name}_reversed.mp4"
   158→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Reversing first clip...")
   159→
   160→            reversed_path = reverse_video(
   161→                local_first_clip_path,
   162→                reversed_clip_path)
   163→
   164→            if not reversed_path or not reversed_path.exists():
   165→                return False, f"Failed to reverse first clip: {local_first_clip_path}"
   166→
   167→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] \u2705 Created reversed clip locally: {reversed_path}")
   168→
   169→            reversed_filename = f"{first_clip_name}_reversed.mp4"
   170→            reversed_url = upload_intermediate_file_to_storage(
   171→                local_file_path=reversed_path,
   172→                task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str,
   173→                filename=reversed_filename)
   174→
   175→            if not reversed_url:
   176→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] \u26a0\ufe0f  Upload failed, using local path (may fail on multi-worker)")
   177→                reversed_url = str(reversed_path.resolve())
   178→            else:
   179→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] \u2705 Reversed clip uploaded: {reversed_url}")
   180→
   181→            reversed_clip_dict = {
   182→                "url": reversed_url,
   183→                "name": f"{first_clip_name}_reversed"
   184→            }
   185→
   186→            clip_list = [first_clip, reversed_clip_dict]
   187→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] clip_list overridden: [{first_clip_name}, {reversed_clip_dict['name']}]")
   188→
   189→        if not clip_list or len(clip_list) < 2:
   190→            return False, "clip_list must contain at least 2 clips"
   191→
   192→        num_joins = len(clip_list) - 1
   193→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Processing {len(clip_list)} clips = {num_joins} join tasks")
   194→
   195→        # === EARLY IDEMPOTENCY CHECK (before expensive VLM work) ===
   196→        idempotency_check = _check_existing_join_tasks(orchestrator_task_id_str, num_joins)
   197→        if idempotency_check is not None:
   198→            return idempotency_check
   199→
   200→        # Extract join settings using shared helper
   201→        join_settings = _extract_join_settings_from_payload(orchestrator_payload)
   202→        per_join_settings = orchestrator_payload.get("per_join_settings", [])
   203→        output_base_dir = orchestrator_payload.get("output_base_dir", str(main_output_dir_base.resolve()))
   204→
   205→        # Create run-specific output directory
   206→        current_run_output_dir = Path(output_base_dir) / f"join_clips_run_{run_id}"
   207→        current_run_output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   208→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Run output directory: {current_run_output_dir}")
   209→
   210→        # === VALIDATE CLIP FRAME COUNTS (optional, enabled by default) ===
   211→        skip_validation = orchestrator_payload.get("skip_frame_validation", False)
   212→        if not skip_validation:
   213→            validation_temp_dir = current_run_output_dir / "validation_temp"
   214→            validation_temp_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   215→
   216→            is_valid, validation_message, frame_counts = validate_clip_frames_for_join(
   217→                clip_list=clip_list,
   218→                gap_frame_count=join_settings.get("gap_frame_count", 53),
   219→                context_frame_count=join_settings.get("context_frame_count", 8),
   220→                replace_mode=join_settings.get("replace_mode", False),
   221→                temp_dir=validation_temp_dir,
   222→                orchestrator_task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str)
   223→
   224→            if not is_valid:
   225→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] VALIDATION FAILED: {validation_message}")
   226→                return False, f"Clip frame validation failed: {validation_message}"
   227→
   228→            if validation_message:
   229→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] {validation_message}")
   230→
   231→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Clip frame validation complete, frame counts: {frame_counts}")
   232→        else:
   233→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Frame validation skipped (skip_frame_validation=True)")
   234→
   235→        # === DETECT RESOLUTION FROM INPUT VIDEO (when use_input_video_resolution=True) ===
   236→        if join_settings.get("use_input_video_resolution", False):
   237→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] use_input_video_resolution=True, detecting resolution from first clip...")
   238→
   239→            first_clip_url = clip_list[0].get("url")
   240→            if first_clip_url:
   241→                resolution_temp_dir = current_run_output_dir / "resolution_temp"
   242→                resolution_temp_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   243→
   244→                local_first_clip = download_video_if_url(
   245→                    first_clip_url,
   246→                    download_target_dir=resolution_temp_dir,
   247→                    task_id_for_logging=orchestrator_task_id_str,
   248→                    descriptive_name="detect_resolution"
   249→                )
   250→
   251→                if local_first_clip and Path(local_first_clip).exists():
   252→                    detected_res = _get_video_resolution(local_first_clip)
   253→                    if detected_res:
   254→                        join_settings["resolution"] = list(detected_res)
   255→                        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] \u2713 Detected resolution from input video: {detected_res}")
   256→                    else:
   257→                        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] \u26a0 Could not detect resolution, segments will detect from frames")
   258→                else:
   259→                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] \u26a0 Could not download first clip for resolution detection")
   260→
   261→        # === VLM PROMPT ENHANCEMENT (optional) ===
   262→        enhance_prompt = orchestrator_payload.get("enhance_prompt", False)
   263→        vlm_enhanced_prompts: List[Optional[str]] = [None] * num_joins
   264→
   265→        if enhance_prompt:
   266→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] enhance_prompt=True, generating VLM-enhanced prompts for {num_joins} joins")
   267→
   268→            vlm_device = orchestrator_payload.get("vlm_device", "cuda")
   269→            vlm_temp_dir = current_run_output_dir / "vlm_temp"
   270→            vlm_temp_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   271→            base_prompt = join_settings.get("prompt", "")
   272→
   273→            try:
   274→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Extracting 4 frames per join from {len(clip_list)} clips...")
   275→                image_quads = _extract_boundary_frames_for_vlm(
   276→                    clip_list=clip_list,
   277→                    temp_dir=vlm_temp_dir,
   278→                    orchestrator_task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str)
   279→
   280→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Running VLM batch on {len(image_quads)} quads (4 images each)...")
   281→                vlm_enhanced_prompts = _generate_vlm_prompts_for_joins(
   282→                    image_quads=image_quads,
   283→                    base_prompt=base_prompt,
   284→                    vlm_device=vlm_device)
   285→
   286→                valid_count = sum(1 for p in vlm_enhanced_prompts if p is not None)
   287→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] VLM enhancement complete: {valid_count}/{num_joins} prompts generated")
   288→
   289→            except (RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as vlm_error:
   290→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] VLM enhancement failed, using base prompts: {vlm_error}", exc_info=True)
   291→                vlm_enhanced_prompts = [None] * num_joins
   292→        else:
   293→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] enhance_prompt=False, using base prompt for all joins")
   294→
   295→        # === CREATE JOIN TASKS ===
   296→        use_parallel = orchestrator_payload.get("use_parallel_joins", True)
   297→
   298→        parent_generation_id = (
   299→            task_params_from_db.get("parent_generation_id")
   300→            or orchestrator_payload.get("parent_generation_id")
   301→        )
   302→
   303→        if use_parallel:
   304→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Using PARALLEL pattern (transitions in parallel + final stitch)")
   305→            success, message = _create_parallel_join_tasks(
   306→                clip_list=clip_list,
   307→                run_id=run_id,
   308→                join_settings=join_settings,
   309→                per_join_settings=per_join_settings,
   310→                vlm_enhanced_prompts=vlm_enhanced_prompts,
   311→                current_run_output_dir=current_run_output_dir,
   312→                orchestrator_task_id_str=orchestrator_task_id_str,
   313→                orchestrator_project_id=orchestrator_project_id,
   314→                orchestrator_payload=orchestrator_payload,
   315→                parent_generation_id=parent_generation_id)
   316→        else:
   317→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] Using CHAIN pattern (legacy sequential)")
   318→            success, message = _create_join_chain_tasks(
   319→                clip_list=clip_list,
   320→                run_id=run_id,
   321→                join_settings=join_settings,
   322→                per_join_settings=per_join_settings,
   323→                vlm_enhanced_prompts=vlm_enhanced_prompts,
   324→                current_run_output_dir=current_run_output_dir,
   325→                orchestrator_task_id_str=orchestrator_task_id_str,
   326→                orchestrator_project_id=orchestrator_project_id,
   327→                orchestrator_payload=orchestrator_payload,
   328→                parent_generation_id=parent_generation_id)
   329→
   330→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] {message}")
   331→        return success, message
   332→
   333→    except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError, KeyError, TypeError) as e:
   334→        msg = f"Failed during join orchestration: {e}"
   335→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_ORCHESTRATOR] ERROR: {msg}", exc_info=True)
   336→        return False, msg
   337→
   338→
   339→# Public alias for cross-module use.
   340→handle_join_clips_orchestrator_task = _handle_join_clips_orchestrator_task
   341→
```

```
1→"""
     2→Clip validation and frame calculation for join operations.
     3→
     4→Validates that clips have sufficient frames for safe joining
     5→and calculates minimum frame requirements.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→from typing import Tuple, List
    10→
    11→from source.utils import download_video_if_url, get_video_frame_count_and_fps
    12→from source.core.log import task_logger
    13→
    14→__all__ = [
    15→    "calculate_min_clip_frames",
    16→    "validate_clip_frames_for_join",
    17→]
    18→
    19→def calculate_min_clip_frames(gap_frame_count: int, context_frame_count: int, replace_mode: bool) -> int:
    20→    """
    21→    Calculate the minimum number of frames a clip must have to safely join.
    22→
    23→    In REPLACE mode, we need:
    24→        gap_frame_count + 2 * context_frame_count <= min_clip_frames
    25→
    26→    This ensures that context frames don't overlap with previously blended regions
    27→    in chained joins, avoiding the "double-blending" artifact.
    28→
    29→    Args:
    30→        gap_frame_count: Number of frames in the generated gap/transition
    31→        context_frame_count: Number of context frames from each clip boundary
    32→        replace_mode: Whether REPLACE mode is enabled
    33→
    34→    Returns:
    35→        Minimum required frames for each clip
    36→    """
    37→    if replace_mode:
    38→        return gap_frame_count + 2 * context_frame_count
    39→    else:
    40→        return 2 * context_frame_count
    41→
    42→def validate_clip_frames_for_join(
    43→    clip_list: List[dict],
    44→    gap_frame_count: int,
    45→    context_frame_count: int,
    46→    replace_mode: bool,
    47→    temp_dir: Path,
    48→    orchestrator_task_id: str,
    49→    **_kwargs
    50→) -> Tuple[bool, str, List[int]]:
    51→    """
    52→    Validate that all clips have enough frames for safe joining.
    53→
    54→    Args:
    55→        clip_list: List of clip dicts with 'url' keys
    56→        gap_frame_count: Gap frames for transition
    57→        context_frame_count: Context frames from each boundary
    58→        replace_mode: Whether REPLACE mode is enabled
    59→        temp_dir: Directory to download clips for frame counting
    60→        orchestrator_task_id: Task ID for logging
    61→
    62→    Returns:
    63→        Tuple of (is_valid, error_message, frame_counts_per_clip)
    64→    """
    65→    min_frames = calculate_min_clip_frames(gap_frame_count, context_frame_count, replace_mode)
    66→    task_logger.debug(f"[VALIDATE_CLIPS] Minimum required frames per clip: {min_frames}")
    67→    task_logger.debug(f"[VALIDATE_CLIPS]   (gap={gap_frame_count} + 2\u00d7context={context_frame_count}, replace_mode={replace_mode})")
    68→
    69→    frame_counts = []
    70→    violations = []
    71→
    72→    for idx, clip in enumerate(clip_list):
    73→        clip_url = clip.get("url")
    74→        if not clip_url:
    75→            return False, f"Clip {idx} missing 'url' field", []
    76→
    77→        # Download clip to count frames
    78→        local_path = download_video_if_url(
    79→            clip_url,
    80→            download_target_dir=temp_dir,
    81→            task_id_for_logging=orchestrator_task_id,
    82→            descriptive_name=f"validate_clip_{idx}"
    83→        )
    84→
    85→        if not local_path or not Path(local_path).exists():
    86→            return False, f"Failed to download clip {idx} for validation: {clip_url}", []
    87→
    88→        # Get frame count
    89→        frames, fps = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(str(local_path))
    90→        if not frames:
    91→            return False, f"Could not determine frame count for clip {idx}", []
    92→
    93→        frame_counts.append(frames)
    94→        task_logger.debug(f"[VALIDATE_CLIPS] Clip {idx}: {frames} frames (min required: {min_frames})")
    95→
    96→        # First and last clips only need half the minimum (only one boundary)
    97→        is_first = (idx == 0)
    98→        is_last = (idx == len(clip_list) - 1)
    99→
   100→        if is_first or is_last:
   101→            if replace_mode:
   102→                gap_from_side = gap_frame_count // 2 if is_first else (gap_frame_count - gap_frame_count // 2)
   103→                required = context_frame_count + gap_from_side
   104→            else:
   105→                required = context_frame_count
   106→        else:
   107→            required = min_frames
   108→
   109→        if frames < required:
   110→            violations.append({
   111→                "idx": idx,
   112→                "frames": frames,
   113→                "required": required,
   114→                "shortfall": required - frames
   115→            })
   116→
   117→    if violations:
   118→        min_available = min(frame_counts)
   119→        total_needed = gap_frame_count + 2 * context_frame_count
   120→        ratio = min_available / total_needed
   121→        reduced_gap = max(1, int(gap_frame_count * ratio))
   122→        reduced_context = max(1, int(context_frame_count * ratio))
   123→
   124→        warning_parts = []
   125→        for v in violations:
   126→            warning_parts.append(f"Clip {v['idx']}: {v['frames']} frames < {v['required']} required")
   127→
   128→        warning_msg = (
   129→            f"[PROPORTIONAL_REDUCTION] Some clips are shorter than ideal:\n  "
   130→            + "\n  ".join(warning_parts)
   131→            + f"\n  Original settings: gap={gap_frame_count}, context={context_frame_count}"
   132→            + f"\n  Will reduce to approximately: gap\u2248{reduced_gap}, context\u2248{reduced_context} ({ratio:.0%} of original)"
   133→            + f"\n  Transitions will be shorter but still generated successfully."
   134→        )
   135→        task_logger.debug(f"[VALIDATE_CLIPS] {warning_msg}")
   136→
   137→        return True, warning_msg, frame_counts
   138→
   139→    task_logger.debug(f"[VALIDATE_CLIPS] All {len(clip_list)} clips have sufficient frames")
   140→    return True, "", frame_counts
   141→
```

```
1→"""
     2→Join task creation for chain and parallel patterns.
     3→
     4→Creates join_clips_segment and join_final_stitch tasks in the database,
     5→supporting both the legacy sequential chain pattern and the newer parallel pattern.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→from typing import Tuple, List, Optional
    10→
    11→from source import db_operations as db_ops
    12→from source.core.log import orchestrator_logger
    13→from source.task_handlers.join.shared import _check_orchestrator_cancelled
    14→
    15→__all__ = [
    16→    "_create_join_chain_tasks",
    17→    "_create_parallel_join_tasks",
    18→    "create_join_chain_tasks",
    19→    "create_parallel_join_tasks",
    20→]
    21→
    22→def _create_join_chain_tasks(
    23→    clip_list: List[dict],
    24→    run_id: str,
    25→    join_settings: dict,
    26→    per_join_settings: List[dict],
    27→    vlm_enhanced_prompts: List[Optional[str]],
    28→    current_run_output_dir: Path,
    29→    orchestrator_task_id_str: str,
    30→    orchestrator_project_id: str | None,
    31→    orchestrator_payload: dict,
    32→    parent_generation_id: str | None) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    33→    """
    34→    Core logic: Create chained join_clips_segment tasks (LEGACY - sequential pattern).
    35→
    36→    DEPRECATED: Use _create_parallel_join_tasks for better quality (avoids re-encoding).
    37→
    38→    Args:
    39→        clip_list: List of clip dicts with 'url' and optional 'name' keys
    40→        run_id: Unique run identifier
    41→        join_settings: Base settings for all join tasks
    42→        per_join_settings: Per-join overrides (list, one per join)
    43→        vlm_enhanced_prompts: VLM-generated prompts (or None for each join)
    44→        current_run_output_dir: Output directory for this run
    45→        orchestrator_task_id_str: Orchestrator task ID
    46→        orchestrator_project_id: Project ID for authorization
    47→        orchestrator_payload: Full orchestrator payload for reference
    48→        parent_generation_id: Parent generation ID for variant linking
    49→
    50→    Returns:
    51→        (success: bool, message: str)
    52→    """
    53→    num_joins = len(clip_list) - 1
    54→
    55→    if num_joins < 1:
    56→        return False, "clip_list must contain at least 2 clips"
    57→
    58→    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CORE] Creating {num_joins} join tasks in dependency chain")
    59→
    60→    previous_join_task_id = None
    61→    joins_created = 0
    62→
    63→    for idx in range(num_joins):
    64→        clip_start = clip_list[idx]
    65→        clip_end = clip_list[idx + 1]
    66→
    67→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CORE] Creating join {idx}: {clip_start.get('name', 'clip')} + {clip_end.get('name', 'clip')}")
    68→
    69→        # Merge global settings with per-join overrides
    70→        task_join_settings = join_settings.copy()
    71→        if idx < len(per_join_settings):
    72→            task_join_settings.update(per_join_settings[idx])
    73→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CORE] Applied per-join overrides for join {idx}")
    74→
    75→        # Apply VLM-enhanced prompt if available (overrides base prompt)
    76→        if idx < len(vlm_enhanced_prompts) and vlm_enhanced_prompts[idx] is not None:
    77→            task_join_settings["prompt"] = vlm_enhanced_prompts[idx]
    78→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CORE] Join {idx}: Using VLM-enhanced prompt")
    79→
    80→        # Build join payload
    81→        join_payload = {
    82→            "orchestrator_task_id_ref": orchestrator_task_id_str,
    83→            "orchestrator_run_id": run_id,
    84→            "project_id": orchestrator_project_id,
    85→            "join_index": idx,
    86→            "is_first_join": (idx == 0),
    87→            "is_last_join": (idx == num_joins - 1),
    88→            "child_order": idx,
    89→            "skip_generation": True,
    90→            "starting_video_path": clip_start.get("url") if idx == 0 else None,
    91→            "ending_video_path": clip_end.get("url"),
    92→            **task_join_settings,
    93→            "current_run_base_output_dir": str(current_run_output_dir.resolve()),
    94→            "join_output_dir": str((current_run_output_dir / f"join_{idx}").resolve()),
    95→            "orchestrator_details": orchestrator_payload,
    96→        }
    97→
    98→        # === CANCELLATION CHECK ===
    99→        cancel_msg = _check_orchestrator_cancelled(
   100→            orchestrator_task_id_str,
   101→            f"aborting join creation at index {idx} ({joins_created} joins already created)")
   102→        if cancel_msg:
   103→            return False, cancel_msg
   104→
   105→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CORE] Submitting join {idx} to database, depends_on={previous_join_task_id}")
   106→
   107→        actual_db_row_id = db_ops.add_task_to_db(
   108→            task_payload=join_payload,
   109→            task_type_str="join_clips_segment",
   110→            dependant_on=previous_join_task_id
   111→        )
   112→
   113→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CORE] Join {idx} created with DB ID: {actual_db_row_id}")
   114→
   115→        previous_join_task_id = actual_db_row_id
   116→        joins_created += 1
   117→
   118→    # === Create join_final_stitch that depends on the last join ===
   119→    context_frame_count = join_settings.get("context_frame_count", 8)
   120→
   121→    final_stitch_payload = {
   122→        "orchestrator_task_id_ref": orchestrator_task_id_str,
   123→        "orchestrator_run_id": run_id,
   124→        "project_id": orchestrator_project_id,
   125→        "parent_generation_id": parent_generation_id,
   126→        "clip_list": clip_list,
   127→        "transition_task_ids": [previous_join_task_id],
   128→        "chain_mode": True,
   129→        "blend_frames": min(context_frame_count, 15),
   130→        "fps": join_settings.get("fps") or orchestrator_payload.get("fps", 16),
   131→        "audio_url": orchestrator_payload.get("audio_url"),
   132→        "current_run_base_output_dir": str(current_run_output_dir.resolve()),
   133→    }
   134→
   135→    cancel_msg = _check_orchestrator_cancelled(
   136→        orchestrator_task_id_str,
   137→        f"aborting before final stitch ({joins_created} joins cancelled)")
   138→    if cancel_msg:
   139→        return False, cancel_msg
   140→
   141→    final_stitch_task_id = db_ops.add_task_to_db(
   142→        task_payload=final_stitch_payload,
   143→        task_type_str="join_final_stitch",
   144→        dependant_on=previous_join_task_id
   145→    )
   146→
   147→    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CORE] Final stitch task created with DB ID: {final_stitch_task_id}")
   148→    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CORE] Complete: {joins_created} chain joins + 1 final stitch = {joins_created + 1} total tasks")
   149→
   150→    return True, f"Successfully enqueued {joins_created} chain joins + 1 final stitch for run {run_id}"
   151→
   152→def _create_parallel_join_tasks(
   153→    clip_list: List[dict],
   154→    run_id: str,
   155→    join_settings: dict,
   156→    per_join_settings: List[dict],
   157→    vlm_enhanced_prompts: List[Optional[str]],
   158→    current_run_output_dir: Path,
   159→    orchestrator_task_id_str: str,
   160→    orchestrator_project_id: str | None,
   161→    orchestrator_payload: dict,
   162→    parent_generation_id: str | None) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
   163→    """
   164→    Create parallel join tasks with a final stitch (NEW - parallel pattern).
   165→
   166→    This pattern avoids quality loss from re-encoding:
   167→    1. Create N-1 transition tasks in parallel (no dependencies between them)
   168→    2. Create a single join_final_stitch task that depends on ALL transition tasks
   169→
   170→    Args:
   171→        clip_list: List of clip dicts with 'url' and optional 'name' keys
   172→        run_id: Unique run identifier
   173→        join_settings: Base settings for all join tasks
   174→        per_join_settings: Per-join overrides (list, one per join)
   175→        vlm_enhanced_prompts: VLM-generated prompts (or None for each join)
   176→        current_run_output_dir: Output directory for this run
   177→        orchestrator_task_id_str: Orchestrator task ID
   178→        orchestrator_project_id: Project ID for authorization
   179→        orchestrator_payload: Full orchestrator payload for reference
   180→        parent_generation_id: Parent generation ID for variant linking
   181→
   182→    Returns:
   183→        (success: bool, message: str)
   184→    """
   185→    num_joins = len(clip_list) - 1
   186→
   187→    if num_joins < 1:
   188→        return False, "clip_list must contain at least 2 clips"
   189→
   190→    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_PARALLEL] Creating {num_joins} parallel transition tasks + 1 final stitch")
   191→
   192→    transition_task_ids = []
   193→
   194→    # === Phase 1: Create transition tasks in parallel (no dependencies) ===
   195→    for idx in range(num_joins):
   196→        clip_start = clip_list[idx]
   197→        clip_end = clip_list[idx + 1]
   198→
   199→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_PARALLEL] Creating transition {idx}: {clip_start.get('name', 'clip')} \u2192 {clip_end.get('name', 'clip')}")
   200→
   201→        task_join_settings = join_settings.copy()
   202→        if idx < len(per_join_settings):
   203→            task_join_settings.update(per_join_settings[idx])
   204→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_PARALLEL] Applied per-join overrides for transition {idx}")
   205→
   206→        if idx < len(vlm_enhanced_prompts) and vlm_enhanced_prompts[idx] is not None:
   207→            task_join_settings["prompt"] = vlm_enhanced_prompts[idx]
   208→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_PARALLEL] Transition {idx}: Using VLM-enhanced prompt")
   209→
   210→        transition_payload = {
   211→            "orchestrator_task_id_ref": orchestrator_task_id_str,
   212→            "orchestrator_run_id": run_id,
   213→            "project_id": orchestrator_project_id,
   214→            "join_index": idx,
   215→            "transition_index": idx,
   216→            "is_first_join": False,
   217→            "is_last_join": False,
   218→            "child_order": idx,
   219→            "skip_generation": True,
   220→            "starting_video_path": clip_start.get("url"),
   221→            "ending_video_path": clip_end.get("url"),
   222→            "transition_only": True,
   223→            **task_join_settings,
   224→            "current_run_base_output_dir": str(current_run_output_dir.resolve()),
   225→            "join_output_dir": str((current_run_output_dir / f"transition_{idx}").resolve()),
   226→            "orchestrator_details": orchestrator_payload,
   227→        }
   228→
   229→        cancel_msg = _check_orchestrator_cancelled(
   230→            orchestrator_task_id_str,
   231→            f"aborting transition creation at index {idx} ({len(transition_task_ids)} transitions already created)")
   232→        if cancel_msg:
   233→            return False, cancel_msg
   234→
   235→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_PARALLEL] Submitting transition {idx} to database (no dependency)")
   236→
   237→        trans_task_id = db_ops.add_task_to_db(
   238→            task_payload=transition_payload,
   239→            task_type_str="join_clips_segment",
   240→            dependant_on=None
   241→        )
   242→
   243→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_PARALLEL] Transition {idx} created with DB ID: {trans_task_id}")
   244→        transition_task_ids.append(trans_task_id)
   245→
   246→    # === Phase 2: Create final stitch task that depends on ALL transitions ===
   247→    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_PARALLEL] Creating final stitch task, depends on {len(transition_task_ids)} transitions")
   248→
   249→    context_frame_count = join_settings.get("context_frame_count", 8)
   250→
   251→    final_stitch_payload = {
   252→        "orchestrator_task_id_ref": orchestrator_task_id_str,
   253→        "orchestrator_run_id": run_id,
   254→        "project_id": orchestrator_project_id,
   255→        "parent_generation_id": parent_generation_id,
   256→        "clip_list": clip_list,
   257→        "transition_task_ids": transition_task_ids,
   258→        "blend_frames": min(context_frame_count, 15),
   259→        "fps": join_settings.get("fps") or orchestrator_payload.get("fps", 16),
   260→        "audio_url": orchestrator_payload.get("audio_url"),
   261→        "current_run_base_output_dir": str(current_run_output_dir.resolve()),
   262→    }
   263→
   264→    cancel_msg = _check_orchestrator_cancelled(
   265→        orchestrator_task_id_str,
   266→        f"aborting before final stitch ({len(transition_task_ids)} transitions cancelled)")
   267→    if cancel_msg:
   268→        return False, cancel_msg
   269→
   270→    final_stitch_task_id = db_ops.add_task_to_db(
   271→        task_payload=final_stitch_payload,
   272→        task_type_str="join_final_stitch",
   273→        dependant_on=transition_task_ids
   274→    )
   275→
   276→    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_PARALLEL] Final stitch task created with DB ID: {final_stitch_task_id}")
   277→    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_PARALLEL] Complete: {num_joins} transitions + 1 final stitch = {num_joins + 1} total tasks")
   278→
   279→    return True, f"Successfully enqueued {num_joins} parallel transitions + 1 final stitch for run {run_id}"
   280→
   281→
   282→# Public aliases for cross-module use.
   283→create_join_chain_tasks = _create_join_chain_tasks
   284→create_parallel_join_tasks = _create_parallel_join_tasks
   285→
```

```
1→"""FFmpeg-based video operations: creation, FPS conversion, frame extraction to video."""
     2→import subprocess
```
