    25→def ensure_video_fps(
    26→    input_video_path: str | Path,
    27→    target_fps: float,
    28→    output_dir: str | Path | None = None,
    29→    fps_tolerance: float = 0.5,
    30→) -> Path:
    31→    """
    32→    Ensure a video is at the target FPS, resampling if necessary.
    33→
    34→    This is important when frame indices are calculated for a specific FPS
    35→    (e.g., from frontend) but the actual video file may be at a different FPS.
    36→
    37→    Args:
    38→        input_video_path: Path to source video
    39→        target_fps: Desired FPS (e.g., 16)
    40→        output_dir: Directory for resampled video (defaults to same dir as source)
    41→        fps_tolerance: Maximum FPS difference before resampling (default 0.5)
    42→
    43→    Returns:
    44→        Path to video at target FPS (original if already correct, resampled if not)
    45→
    46→    Raises:
    47→        OSError: If the source video does not exist or the resampled output is missing/empty
    48→        RuntimeError: If FFmpeg fails or times out
    49→        ValueError: If the video FPS cannot be determined
    50→
    51→    Example:
    52→        # Ensure video is at 16fps before frame-based extraction
    53→        video_16fps = ensure_video_fps(downloaded_video, 16, work_dir)
    54→        extract_frame_range_to_video(video_16fps, output, 0, 252, 16)
    55→    """
    56→    input_video_path = Path(input_video_path)
    57→
    58→    # Validate target_fps is not None
    59→    if target_fps is None:
    60→        generation_logger.warning(f"[ENSURE_FPS] target_fps is None, defaulting to 16")
    61→        target_fps = 16
    62→
    63→    if not input_video_path.exists():
    64→        raise OSError(f"Video does not exist: {input_video_path}")
    65→
    66→    # Get actual fps
    67→    actual_frames, actual_fps = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(str(input_video_path))
    68→    if not actual_fps:
    69→        raise ValueError(f"Could not determine video FPS: {input_video_path}")
    70→
    71→    # Check if resampling is needed
    72→    if abs(actual_fps - target_fps) <= fps_tolerance:
    73→        generation_logger.debug(f"[ENSURE_FPS] Video already at target FPS: {actual_fps} fps (target: {target_fps}, tolerance: \u00b1{fps_tolerance})")
    74→        return input_video_path
    75→
    76→    # Need to resample
    77→    generation_logger.debug(f"[ENSURE_FPS] FPS mismatch: actual={actual_fps}, target={target_fps}")
    78→    generation_logger.debug(f"[ENSURE_FPS] Resampling {input_video_path.name} to {target_fps} fps...")
    79→
    80→    # Determine output path
    81→    if output_dir is None:
    82→        output_dir = input_video_path.parent
    83→    output_dir = Path(output_dir)
    84→    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    85→
    86→    resampled_path = output_dir / f"{input_video_path.stem}_resampled_{int(target_fps)}fps.mp4"
    87→
    88→    # Use fps filter (not -r output option) for accurate frame-based resampling
    89→    # The fps filter selects frames based on timestamps, ensuring frame N = time N/fps
    90→    # This is critical for frame-accurate extraction where frame indices must match timestamps
    91→    resample_cmd = [
    92→        'ffmpeg', '-y',
    93→        '-i', str(input_video_path),
    94→        '-vf', f'fps={target_fps}',
    95→        '-an',  # No audio for now
    96→        str(resampled_path)
    97→    ]
    98→
    99→    try:
   100→        result = subprocess.run(resample_cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=600)
   101→    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as e:
   102→        raise RuntimeError(f"FFmpeg resample timed out after 600s for {input_video_path}") from e
   103→    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError) as e:
   104→        raise RuntimeError(f"FFmpeg resample failed for {input_video_path}: {e}") from e
   105→
   106→    if result.returncode != 0:
   107→        raise RuntimeError(f"FFmpeg resample failed (rc={result.returncode}): {result.stderr[:500]}")
   108→
   109→    if not resampled_path.exists() or resampled_path.stat().st_size == 0:
   110→        raise OSError(f"Resampled video missing or empty: {resampled_path}")
   111→
   112→    # Verify result
   113→    new_frames, new_fps = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(str(resampled_path))
   114→    generation_logger.debug(f"[ENSURE_FPS] Resampled: {new_frames} frames @ {new_fps} fps")
   115→
   116→    return resampled_path
   117→
   118→
   119→def extract_frame_range_to_video(
   120→    input_video_path: str | Path,
   121→    output_video_path: str | Path,
   122→    start_frame: int,
   123→    end_frame: int | None,
   124→    fps: float,
   125→) -> Path:
   126→    """
   127→    Extract a range of frames from a video to a new video file using FFmpeg.
   128→
   129→    Uses FFmpeg's select filter with frame-accurate selection (not time-based).
   130→    This is the canonical function for frame extraction - use this instead of
   131→    inline FFmpeg commands.
   132→
   133→    Args:
   134→        input_video_path: Path to source video file
   135→        output_video_path: Path for output video file
   136→        start_frame: First frame to include (0-indexed, inclusive)
   137→        end_frame: Last frame to include (0-indexed, inclusive), or None for all remaining frames
   138→        fps: Output framerate
   139→
   140→    Returns:
   141→        Path to extracted video
   142→
   143→    Raises:
   144→        OSError: If source video does not exist or output is missing/empty
   145→        ValueError: If frame range is invalid or frame count cannot be determined
   146→        RuntimeError: If FFmpeg fails, times out, or output frame count is wrong
   147→
   148→    Examples:
   149→        # Extract frames 0-252 (253 frames)
   150→        extract_frame_range_to_video(src, out, 0, 252, 16)
   151→
   152→        # Extract frames from 13 onwards (skip first 13)
   153→        extract_frame_range_to_video(src, out, 13, None, 16)
   154→    """
   155→    input_video_path = Path(input_video_path)
   156→    output_video_path = Path(output_video_path)
   157→    output_video_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   158→
   159→    # Verify source exists
   160→    if not input_video_path.exists():
   161→        raise OSError(f"Source video does not exist: {input_video_path}")
   162→
   163→    # Get source properties - use ffprobe for accurate frame count
   164→    source_frames = get_video_frame_count_ffprobe(str(input_video_path))
   165→    _, source_fps = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(str(input_video_path))
   166→
   167→    if not source_frames:
   168→        # Fallback to OpenCV if ffprobe fails
   169→        source_frames, source_fps = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(str(input_video_path))
   170→        generation_logger.warning(f"[EXTRACT_RANGE] Using OpenCV frame count (ffprobe failed): {source_frames}")
   171→
   172→    if not source_frames:
   173→        raise ValueError(f"Could not determine source video frame count: {input_video_path}")
   174→
   175→    # Validate range
   176→    if start_frame < 0:
   177→        raise ValueError(f"start_frame cannot be negative: {start_frame}")
   178→
   179→    if end_frame is not None and end_frame >= source_frames:
   180→        raise ValueError(f"end_frame {end_frame} >= source frames {source_frames}")
   181→
   182→    # Build filter based on whether we have an end_frame
   183→    if end_frame is not None:
   184→        # Extract specific range: frames start_frame to end_frame (inclusive)
   185→        filter_str = f"select=between(n\\,{start_frame}\\,{end_frame}),setpts=N/FR/TB"
   186→        expected_frames = end_frame - start_frame + 1
   187→        range_desc = f"frames {start_frame}-{end_frame} ({expected_frames} frames)"
   188→    else:
   189→        # Extract from start_frame onwards (skip first start_frame frames)
   190→        filter_str = f"select=gte(n\\,{start_frame}),setpts=N/FR/TB"
   191→        expected_frames = source_frames - start_frame
   192→        range_desc = f"frames {start_frame}-{source_frames-1} ({expected_frames} frames)"
   193→
   194→    generation_logger.debug(f"[EXTRACT_RANGE] Source: {input_video_path.name} ({source_frames} frames @ {source_fps} fps)")
   195→    generation_logger.debug(f"[EXTRACT_RANGE] Extracting: {range_desc}")
   196→
   197→    cmd = [
   198→        'ffmpeg', '-y',
   199→        '-i', str(input_video_path),
   200→        '-vf', filter_str,
   201→        '-c:v', 'libx264',
   202→        '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p',
   203→        '-preset', 'slow',  # Better quality at same bitrate
   204→        '-crf', '10',  # Near-lossless quality for intermediate files
   205→        '-r', str(fps),
   206→        '-an',  # No audio
   207→        str(output_video_path)
   208→    ]
   209→
   210→    try:
   211→        result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=300)
   212→    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as e:
   213→        raise RuntimeError(f"FFmpeg frame extraction timed out after 300s for {input_video_path}") from e
   214→    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError) as e:
   215→        raise RuntimeError(f"FFmpeg frame extraction failed for {input_video_path}: {e}") from e
   216→
   217→    if result.returncode != 0:
   218→        raise RuntimeError(f"FFmpeg frame extraction failed (rc={result.returncode}): {result.stderr[:500]}")
   219→
   220→    if not output_video_path.exists() or output_video_path.stat().st_size == 0:
   221→        raise OSError(f"Output video missing or empty: {output_video_path}")
   222→
   223→    # Verify frame count (allow small tolerance for OpenCV/FFmpeg discrepancies)
   224→    actual_frames, _ = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(str(output_video_path))
   225→    if actual_frames is None:
   226→        raise RuntimeError(f"Could not verify output frame count for {output_video_path}")
   227→
   228→    # Allow up to 3 frames difference - OpenCV and FFmpeg often disagree on frame counts
   229→    # especially for end-of-video segments or variable framerate videos
   230→    frame_diff = abs(actual_frames - expected_frames)
   231→    if frame_diff > 3:
   232→        raise RuntimeError(f"Frame count mismatch too large: expected {expected_frames}, got {actual_frames} (diff: {frame_diff})")
   233→    elif frame_diff > 0:
   234→        generation_logger.warning(f"[EXTRACT_RANGE] Minor frame count difference: expected {expected_frames}, got {actual_frames} (diff: {frame_diff}, within tolerance)")
   235→
   236→    generation_logger.debug(f"[EXTRACT_RANGE] Extracted {actual_frames} frames to {output_video_path.name}")
   237→    return output_video_path
   238→
   239→
   240→def create_video_from_frames_list(
   241→    frames_list: list[np.ndarray],
   242→    output_path: str | Path,
   243→    fps: int,
   244→    resolution: tuple[int, int],
   245→    *,
   246→    standardize_colorspace: bool = False
   247→) -> Path:
   248→    """Creates a video from a list of NumPy BGR frames using FFmpeg subprocess.
   249→
   250→    Uses streaming to pipe frames to FFmpeg incrementally, avoiding loading
   251→    all frame data into memory at once. This is critical for large videos
   252→    (e.g., 2000+ frames at 1080p would otherwise require 30+ GB RAM).
   253→
   254→    Args:
   255→        frames_list: List of BGR numpy arrays
   256→        output_path: Output video path
   257→        fps: Frames per second
   258→        resolution: (width, height) tuple
   259→        standardize_colorspace: If True, adds BT.709 colorspace standardization
   260→
   261→    Returns:
   262→        Path object of the successfully written file
   263→
   264→    Raises:
   265→        ValueError: If no valid frames are provided
   266→        RuntimeError: If FFmpeg fails, times out, or output is missing/empty
   267→        OSError: If FFmpeg is not installed or file system errors occur
   268→    """
   269→
   270→    output_path_obj = Path(output_path)
   271→    output_path_mp4 = output_path_obj.with_suffix('.mp4')
   272→    output_path_mp4.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   273→
   274→    generation_logger.debug(f"[CREATE_VIDEO] Creating video: {output_path_mp4}")
   275→    generation_logger.debug(f"[CREATE_VIDEO] Input: {len(frames_list)} frames, resolution={resolution}, fps={fps}")
   276→
   277→    ffmpeg_cmd = [
   278→        "ffmpeg", "-y",
   279→        "-loglevel", "error",
   280→        "-f", "rawvideo",
   281→        "-vcodec", "rawvideo",
   282→        "-pix_fmt", "bgr24",
   283→        "-s", f"{resolution[0]}x{resolution[1]}",
   284→        "-r", str(fps),
   285→        "-i", "-",
   286→        "-c:v", "libx264",
   287→        "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p",
   288→        "-preset", "slow",  # Better quality at same bitrate
   289→        "-crf", "10",  # Near-lossless quality for intermediate files
   290→    ]
   291→
   292→    # Add colorspace standardization if requested
   293→    if standardize_colorspace:
   294→        ffmpeg_cmd.extend([
   295→            "-vf", "format=yuv420p,colorspace=bt709:iall=bt709:fast=1",
   296→            "-color_primaries", "bt709",
   297→            "-color_trc", "bt709",
   298→            "-colorspace", "bt709",
   299→        ])
   300→
   301→    ffmpeg_cmd.append(str(output_path_mp4.resolve()))
   302→
   303→    # Count valid frames first (without storing them)
   304→    valid_count = 0
   305→    skipped_none = 0
   306→    skipped_invalid = 0
   307→    for frame_np in frames_list:
   308→        if frame_np is None or not isinstance(frame_np, np.ndarray):
   309→            skipped_none += 1
   310→        elif len(frame_np.shape) != 3 or frame_np.shape[2] != 3:
   311→            skipped_invalid += 1
   312→        else:
   313→            valid_count += 1
   314→
   315→    if skipped_none > 0:
   316→        generation_logger.warning(f"[CREATE_VIDEO] Will skip {skipped_none} None/invalid frames")
   317→    if skipped_invalid > 0:
   318→        generation_logger.warning(f"[CREATE_VIDEO] Will skip {skipped_invalid} non-RGB frames")
   319→
   320→    if valid_count == 0:
   321→        raise ValueError(f"No valid frames to process! Input had {len(frames_list)} frames, all invalid")
   322→
   323→    generation_logger.debug(f"[CREATE_VIDEO] Streaming {valid_count} frames to FFmpeg (memory-efficient mode)")
   324→
   325→    try:
   326→        # Use Popen to stream frames incrementally instead of loading all into memory
   327→        proc = subprocess.Popen(
   328→            ffmpeg_cmd,
   329→            stdin=subprocess.PIPE,
   330→            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
   331→            stderr=subprocess.PIPE
   332→        )
   333→    except FileNotFoundError as e:
   334→        raise OSError("FFmpeg not found - is it installed?") from e
   335→    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError) as e:
   336→        raise RuntimeError(f"Failed to start FFmpeg process: {e}") from e
   337→
   338→    frames_written = 0
   339→    resize_warnings = 0
   340→
   341→    for frame_idx, frame_np in enumerate(frames_list):
   342→        # Skip invalid frames
   343→        if frame_np is None or not isinstance(frame_np, np.ndarray):
   344→            continue
   345→        if len(frame_np.shape) != 3 or frame_np.shape[2] != 3:
   346→            continue
   347→
   348→        # Ensure uint8
   349→        if frame_np.dtype != np.uint8:
   350→            frame_np = frame_np.astype(np.uint8)
   351→
   352→        # Resize if needed
   353→        if frame_np.shape[0] != resolution[1] or frame_np.shape[1] != resolution[0]:
   354→            try:
   355→                frame_np = cv2.resize(frame_np, resolution, interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
   356→            except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e:
   357→                if resize_warnings < 5:
   358→                    generation_logger.warning(f"[CREATE_VIDEO] Failed to resize frame {frame_idx}: {e}")
   359→                resize_warnings += 1
   360→                continue
   361→
   362→        # Write frame bytes directly to FFmpeg stdin
   363→        try:
   364→            proc.stdin.write(frame_np.tobytes())
   365→            frames_written += 1
   366→        except BrokenPipeError:
   367→            generation_logger.error(f"[CREATE_VIDEO] FFmpeg pipe broken after {frames_written} frames")
   368→            break
   369→
   370→    if resize_warnings > 5:
   371→        generation_logger.warning(f"[CREATE_VIDEO] {resize_warnings} total resize failures (only first 5 logged)")
   372→
   373→    # Close stdin to signal end of input
   374→    proc.stdin.close()
   375→
   376→    generation_logger.debug(f"[CREATE_VIDEO] Wrote {frames_written} frames to FFmpeg, waiting for encoding...")
   377→
   378→    # Read stderr in background thread to avoid blocking
   379→    stderr_output = []
   380→    def read_stderr():
   381→        try:
   382→            stderr_output.append(proc.stderr.read())
   383→        except OSError as e:
   384→            generation_logger.debug(f"[CREATE_VIDEO] Failed to read FFmpeg stderr: {e}")
   385→
   386→    stderr_thread = threading.Thread(target=read_stderr)
   387→    stderr_thread.start()
   388→
   389→    try:
   390→        proc.wait(timeout=300)  # 5 minute timeout for encoding
   391→    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   392→        proc.kill()
   393→        raise RuntimeError(f"FFmpeg timed out after 300 seconds creating video {output_path_mp4}")
   394→    finally:
   395→        stderr_thread.join(timeout=5)
   396→
   397→    stderr = stderr_output[0] if stderr_output else b""
   398→
   399→    if proc.returncode == 0:
   400→        if output_path_mp4.exists() and output_path_mp4.stat().st_size > 0:
   401→            generation_logger.debug(f"[CREATE_VIDEO] SUCCESS: Created {output_path_mp4} ({output_path_mp4.stat().st_size} bytes)")
   402→            return output_path_mp4
   403→        raise RuntimeError(f"FFmpeg succeeded but output file missing or empty: {output_path_mp4}")
   404→    else:
   405→        stderr_str = stderr.decode('utf-8', errors='replace') if stderr else "no stderr"
   406→        if output_path_mp4.exists():
   407→            try:
   408→                output_path_mp4.unlink()
   409→            except OSError as e:
   410→                generation_logger.debug(f"[CREATE_VIDEO] Failed to clean up partial output file {output_path_mp4}: {e}")
   411→        raise RuntimeError(f"FFmpeg failed (rc={proc.returncode}): {stderr_str[:500]}")
   412→
   413→
   414→def apply_saturation_to_video_ffmpeg(
   415→    input_video_path: str | Path,
   416→    output_video_path: str | Path,
   417→    saturation_level: float,
   418→    preset: str = "medium"
   419→) -> bool:
   420→    """Applies a saturation adjustment to the full video using FFmpeg's eq filter.
   421→    Returns: True if FFmpeg succeeds and the output file exists & is non-empty, else False.
   422→    """
   423→    inp = Path(input_video_path)
   424→    outp = Path(output_video_path)
   425→    outp.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   426→
   427→    cmd = [
   428→        "ffmpeg", "-y",
   429→        "-i", str(inp.resolve()),
   430→        "-vf", f"eq=saturation={saturation_level}",
   431→        "-c:v", "libx264",
   432→        "-crf", "10",  # Near-lossless quality for intermediate files
   433→        "-preset", preset,
   434→        "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p",
   435→        "-an",
   436→        str(outp.resolve())
   437→    ]
   438→
   439→    try:
   440→        subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", timeout=600)
   441→        if outp.exists() and outp.stat().st_size > 0:
   442→            return True
   443→        return False
   444→    except subprocess.CalledProcessError:
   445→        return False
   446→
   447→
   448→def video_has_audio(video_path: str | Path) -> bool:
   449→    """Check if a video file contains an audio stream."""
   450→    try:
   451→        result = subprocess.run(
   452→            ["ffprobe", "-v", "quiet", "-select_streams", "a",
   453→             "-show_entries", "stream=codec_type", "-of", "csv=p=0",
   454→             str(video_path)],
   455→            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10,
   456→        )
   457→        return "audio" in result.stdout
   458→    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):
   459→        return False
   460→
   461→
   462→def mux_audio_from_segments(
   463→    silent_video: str | Path,
   464→    segment_videos: list[str | Path],
   465→    output_path: str | Path,
   466→    overlap_frames: list[int] | None = None,
   467→    fps: float = 24.0,
   468→) -> Path | None:
   469→    """Concatenate audio from segment videos and mux onto a silent stitched video.
   470→
   471→    Extracts audio from each segment, trims overlap regions to match the video
   472→    stitch, concatenates, and muxes the result onto *silent_video*.
   473→
   474→    Args:
   475→        silent_video: Path to the stitched video (no audio).
   476→        segment_videos: Ordered list of segment video paths (with audio).
   477→        output_path: Destination path for the video+audio result.
   478→        overlap_frames: Frame overlaps between adjacent segments (len = n-1).
   479→        fps: Frames per second (used to convert overlap frames to seconds).
   480→
   481→    Returns:
   482→        Path to the output video with audio, or None on failure.
   483→    """
   484→    silent_video = Path(silent_video)
   485→    output_path = Path(output_path)
   486→
   487→    # Quick check: do any segments have audio?
   488→    segments_with_audio = [p for p in segment_videos if video_has_audio(p)]
   489→    if not segments_with_audio:
   490→        generation_logger.debug("[AUDIO_MUX] No segment videos contain audio — skipping audio mux")
   491→        return None
   492→
   493→    generation_logger.debug(
   494→        f"[AUDIO_MUX] {len(segments_with_audio)}/{len(segment_videos)} segments have audio, "
   495→        f"muxing onto stitched video"
   496→    )
   497→
   498→    try:
   499→        import tempfile
   500→
   501→        work_dir = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="audio_mux_"))
   502→        overlaps = overlap_frames or [0] * (len(segment_videos) - 1)
   503→
   504→        # --- Step 1: Extract audio from each segment, trimming overlaps ---
   505→        audio_parts: list[Path] = []
   506→        for i, seg_path in enumerate(segment_videos):
   507→            seg_path = Path(seg_path)
   508→            if not video_has_audio(seg_path):
   509→                # Generate silence matching this segment's duration
   510→                try:
   511→                    dur_result = subprocess.run(
   512→                        ["ffprobe", "-v", "quiet", "-show_entries", "format=duration",
   513→                         "-of", "csv=p=0", str(seg_path)],
   514→                        capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10,
   515→                    )
   516→                    duration = float(dur_result.stdout.strip())
   517→                except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError, ValueError):
   518→                    duration = 1.0
   519→
   520→                # Trim overlap from start (except first segment)
   521→                if i > 0 and i - 1 < len(overlaps):
   522→                    trim_start = overlaps[i - 1] / fps
   523→                    duration = max(0.01, duration - trim_start)
   524→
   525→                silence_path = work_dir / f"silence_{i}.aac"
   526→                subprocess.run(
   527→                    ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-f", "lavfi", "-i",
   528→                     f"anullsrc=channel_layout=stereo:sample_rate=44100",
   529→                     "-t", f"{duration:.4f}", "-c:a", "aac", str(silence_path)],
   530→                    capture_output=True, timeout=30,
   531→                )
   532→                if silence_path.exists():
   533→                    audio_parts.append(silence_path)
   534→                continue
   535→
   536→            part_path = work_dir / f"audio_{i}.aac"
   537→
   538→            # Build ffmpeg command with optional trim
   539→            cmd = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-i", str(seg_path), "-vn", "-c:a", "aac"]
   540→
   541→            if i > 0 and i - 1 < len(overlaps) and overlaps[i - 1] > 0:
   542→                # Trim overlap seconds from the beginning of this segment's audio
   543→                trim_seconds = overlaps[i - 1] / fps
   544→                cmd.extend(["-ss", f"{trim_seconds:.4f}"])
   545→
   546→            cmd.append(str(part_path))
   547→            subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, timeout=120)
   548→
   549→            if part_path.exists() and part_path.stat().st_size > 0:
   550→                audio_parts.append(part_path)
   551→
   552→        if not audio_parts:
   553→            generation_logger.debug("[AUDIO_MUX] No audio parts extracted — skipping")
   554→            return None
   555→
   556→        # --- Step 2: Concatenate audio parts ---
   557→        concat_list = work_dir / "concat.txt"
   558→        concat_list.write_text(
   559→            "\n".join(f"file '{p.resolve()}'" for p in audio_parts),
   560→            encoding="utf-8",
   561→        )
   562→
   563→        merged_audio = work_dir / "merged_audio.aac"
   564→        subprocess.run(
   565→            ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-f", "concat", "-safe", "0",
   566→             "-i", str(concat_list), "-c:a", "aac", str(merged_audio)],
   567→            capture_output=True, timeout=120,
   568→        )
   569→
   570→        if not merged_audio.exists() or merged_audio.stat().st_size == 0:
   571→            generation_logger.warning("[AUDIO_MUX] Audio concatenation failed")
   572→            return None
   573→
   574→        # --- Step 3: Mux merged audio onto the silent video ---
   575→        # Use -shortest so the output matches the video duration
   576→        subprocess.run(
   577→            ["ffmpeg", "-y",
   578→             "-i", str(silent_video),
   579→             "-i", str(merged_audio),
   580→             "-c:v", "copy", "-c:a", "aac", "-shortest",
   581→             str(output_path)],
   582→            capture_output=True, timeout=120,
   583→        )
   584→
   585→        if output_path.exists() and output_path.stat().st_size > 0:
   586→            generation_logger.debug(f"[AUDIO_MUX] Successfully muxed audio → {output_path}")
   587→            # Clean up temp dir
   588→            import shutil
   589→            shutil.rmtree(work_dir, ignore_errors=True)
   590→            return output_path
   591→
   592→        generation_logger.warning("[AUDIO_MUX] Final mux produced empty output")
   593→        return None
   594→
   595→    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError, ValueError) as e:
   596→        generation_logger.warning(f"[AUDIO_MUX] Audio mux failed: {e}")
   597→        return None
   598→
```

    28→def adjust_frame_brightness(frame: np.ndarray, brightness_adjust: float) -> np.ndarray:
    29→    if brightness_adjust == 0:
    30→        return frame
    31→    factor = 1 + brightness_adjust
    32→    adjusted = np.clip(frame.astype(np.float32) * factor, 0, 255).astype(np.uint8)
    33→    return adjusted
    34→
    35→def apply_brightness_to_video_frames(input_video_path: str, output_video_path: Path, brightness_adjust: float, task_id_for_logging: str) -> Path | None:
    36→    """
    37→    Applies brightness adjustment to a video by processing its frames.
    38→    A brightness_adjust of 0 means no change. Negative values darken, positive values brighten.

    42→
    43→        total_frames, fps = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(input_video_path)
    44→        if total_frames is None or fps is None or total_frames == 0:
    45→            headless_logger.error(f"Task {task_id_for_logging}: Could not get frame count or fps for {input_video_path}, or video has 0 frames.", task_id=task_id_for_logging)
    46→            return None
    47→
    48→        frames = extract_frames_from_video(input_video_path)
    49→        if frames is None:
    50→            headless_logger.error(f"Task {task_id_for_logging}: Could not extract frames from {input_video_path}", task_id=task_id_for_logging)
    51→            return None
    52→
    53→        adjusted_frames = []
    54→        first_frame = None
    55→        for frame in frames:
    56→            if first_frame is None:
    57→                first_frame = frame
    58→            adjusted_frame = adjust_frame_brightness(frame, brightness_adjust)
    59→            adjusted_frames.append(adjusted_frame)
    60→
    61→        if not adjusted_frames or first_frame is None:
    62→            headless_logger.error(f"Task {task_id_for_logging}: No frames to write for brightness-adjusted video.", task_id=task_id_for_logging)

    64→
    65→        h, w, _ = first_frame.shape
    66→        resolution = (w, h)

    68→        created_video_path = create_video_from_frames_list(adjusted_frames, output_video_path, fps, resolution)

    75→def reverse_video(

    79→    Reverse a video using FFmpeg (play it backwards) with visually lossless quality.
    80→
    81→    Uses CRF 17 encoding which is visually indistinguishable from the original.
    82→
    83→    Args:
    84→        input_video_path: Path to input video
    85→        output_video_path: Path for reversed output video
    86→
    87→    Returns:
    88→        Path to reversed video if successful, None otherwise

    91→    output_path = Path(output_video_path)

    95→        return None
    96→
    97→    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    98→
    99→    # Get input video FPS to preserve it exactly
   100→    _, input_fps = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(str(input_path))
   101→    if not input_fps:
   102→        input_fps = 16  # Default fallback
   103→
   104→    # Use ffmpeg to reverse the video with near-lossless quality
   105→    # -crf 10: Near-lossless for intermediate files (minimizes generation loss)
   106→    # -preset slow: Better compression efficiency, maintains quality

   108→    cmd = [

   111→        '-vf', 'reverse',
   112→        '-an',  # No audio (reversed audio sounds bad anyway)
   113→        '-c:v', 'libx264',
   114→        '-crf', '10',  # Near-lossless quality for intermediate files
   115→        '-preset', 'slow',  # Better quality at same bitrate

   135→        original_frames, _ = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(str(input_path))
   136→
   137→        generation_logger.debug(f"[REVERSE_VIDEO] \u2705 Successfully reversed video: {original_frames} -> {reversed_frames} frames @ {reversed_fps} fps (CRF 17)")
   138→        return output_path

   147→def standardize_video_aspect_ratio(
   148→    input_video_path: str | Path,
   149→    output_video_path: str | Path,
   150→    target_aspect_ratio: str,
   151→    task_id_for_logging: str = "") -> Path | None:
   152→    """
   153→    Standardize video to target aspect ratio by center-cropping.
   154→
   155→    Args:
   156→        input_video_path: Path to input video
   157→        output_video_path: Path where standardized video will be saved
   158→        target_aspect_ratio: Target aspect ratio as string (e.g., "16:9", "9:16", "1:1")
   159→        task_id_for_logging: Task ID for logging
   160→
   161→    Returns:
   162→        Path to standardized video if successful, None otherwise
   163→    """
   164→    import subprocess
   165→    from pathlib import Path
   166→
   167→    input_path = Path(input_video_path)
   168→    output_path = Path(output_video_path)
   169→
   170→    if not input_path.exists():
   171→        generation_logger.debug(f"[STANDARDIZE_ASPECT] Task {task_id_for_logging}: Input video not found: {input_path}")
   172→        return None
   173→
   174→    # Parse aspect ratio

   177→        if len(ar_parts) != 2:
   178→            raise ValueError(f"Invalid aspect ratio format: {target_aspect_ratio}")
   179→        target_w, target_h = float(ar_parts[0]), float(ar_parts[1])
   180→        target_aspect = target_w / target_h
   181→    except (ValueError, TypeError) as e:

   183→        return None

   185→    # Get input video dimensions using ffprobe
   186→    try:
   187→        probe_cmd = [
   188→            'ffprobe', '-v', 'error',
   189→            '-select_streams', 'v:0',
   190→            '-show_entries', 'stream=width,height',
   191→            '-of', 'csv=p=0',
   192→            str(input_path)
   193→        ]
   194→        result = subprocess.run(probe_cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
   195→        if result.returncode != 0:
   196→            generation_logger.debug(f"[STANDARDIZE_ASPECT] Task {task_id_for_logging}: ffprobe failed: {result.stderr}")
   197→            return None
   198→
   199→        width_str, height_str = result.stdout.strip().split(',')
   200→        src_w, src_h = int(width_str), int(height_str)
   201→        src_aspect = src_w / src_h
   202→
   203→        generation_logger.debug(f"[STANDARDIZE_ASPECT] Task {task_id_for_logging}: Input video: {src_w}x{src_h} (aspect: {src_aspect:.3f})")
   204→        generation_logger.debug(f"[STANDARDIZE_ASPECT] Task {task_id_for_logging}: Target aspect: {target_aspect:.3f}")
   205→
   206→    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError, ValueError) as e:
   207→        generation_logger.debug(f"[STANDARDIZE_ASPECT] Task {task_id_for_logging}: Failed to get video dimensions: {e}")
   208→        return None
   209→
   210→    # Check if aspect ratio is already correct (within 1% tolerance)
   211→    if abs(src_aspect - target_aspect) < 0.01:
   212→        generation_logger.debug(f"[STANDARDIZE_ASPECT] Task {task_id_for_logging}: Video already has target aspect ratio, copying...")
   213→        try:
   214→            import shutil
   215→            shutil.copy2(input_path, output_path)
   216→            return output_path
   217→        except OSError as e:
   218→            generation_logger.debug(f"[STANDARDIZE_ASPECT] Task {task_id_for_logging}: Failed to copy video: {e}")
   219→            return None
   220→
   221→    # Calculate crop dimensions
   222→    if src_aspect > target_aspect:
   223→        # Source is wider - crop width
   224→        new_w = int(src_h * target_aspect)
   225→        new_h = src_h
   226→        crop_x = (src_w - new_w) // 2
   227→        crop_y = 0
   228→    else:
   229→        # Source is taller - crop height
   230→        new_w = src_w
   231→        new_h = int(src_w / target_aspect)
   232→        crop_x = 0
   233→        crop_y = (src_h - new_h) // 2
   234→
   235→    generation_logger.debug(f"[STANDARDIZE_ASPECT] Task {task_id_for_logging}: Center-cropping to {new_w}x{new_h}")
   236→
   237→    # Apply crop using ffmpeg
   238→    try:
   239→        crop_cmd = [
   240→            'ffmpeg', '-y', '-i', str(input_path),
   241→            '-vf', f'crop={new_w}:{new_h}:{crop_x}:{crop_y}',
   242→            '-c:a', 'copy',  # Copy audio stream if present
   243→            str(output_path)
   244→        ]
   245→
   246→        result = subprocess.run(crop_cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=300)
   247→        if result.returncode != 0:
   248→            generation_logger.debug(f"[STANDARDIZE_ASPECT] Task {task_id_for_logging}: ffmpeg crop failed: {result.stderr}")
   249→            return None
   250→
   251→        if not output_path.exists() or output_path.stat().st_size == 0:
   252→            generation_logger.debug(f"[STANDARDIZE_ASPECT] Task {task_id_for_logging}: Output video not created or empty")
   253→            return None
   254→
   255→        generation_logger.debug(f"[STANDARDIZE_ASPECT] Task {task_id_for_logging}: Successfully standardized video to {target_aspect_ratio}")
   256→        return output_path
   257→
   258→    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError) as e:
   259→        generation_logger.debug(f"[STANDARDIZE_ASPECT] Task {task_id_for_logging}: Failed to crop video: {e}")
   260→        if output_path.exists():
   261→            try:
   262→                output_path.unlink()
   263→            except OSError as e_cleanup:
   264→                generation_logger.debug(f"[STANDARDIZE_ASPECT] Task {task_id_for_logging}: DEBUG: Failed to clean up output {output_path}: {e_cleanup}")
   265→        return None
   266→
   267→def add_audio_to_video(
   268→    input_video_path: str | Path,
   269→    audio_url: str,
   270→    output_video_path: str | Path,
   271→    temp_dir: str | Path) -> Path | None:
   272→    """
   273→    Add audio to a video file, trimming audio to match video duration.
   274→
   275→    If the audio is longer than the video, it will be trimmed to match.
   276→    If the audio is shorter than the video, the video will have audio only
   277→    for the duration of the audio (remainder will be silent).
   278→
   279→    Args:
   280→        input_video_path: Path to input video (no audio or audio will be replaced)
   281→        audio_url: URL or local path to audio file (mp3, wav, etc.)
   282→        output_video_path: Path for output video with audio
   283→        temp_dir: Directory for temporary files (audio download)
   284→
   285→    Returns:
   286→        Path to output video with audio, or None if failed
   287→    """
   288→    input_video_path = Path(input_video_path)
   289→    output_video_path = Path(output_video_path)
   290→    temp_dir = Path(temp_dir)
   291→
   292→    if not input_video_path.exists():
   293→        generation_logger.debug(f"[ADD_AUDIO] Input video does not exist: {input_video_path}")
   294→        return None
   295→
   296→    if not audio_url:
   297→        generation_logger.debug(f"[ADD_AUDIO] No audio URL provided")
   298→        return None
   299→
   300→    generation_logger.debug(f"[ADD_AUDIO] Adding audio to video")
   301→    generation_logger.debug(f"[ADD_AUDIO]   Video: {input_video_path}")
   302→    generation_logger.debug(f"[ADD_AUDIO]   Audio: {audio_url[:80]}...")
   303→
   304→    try:
   305→        # Download audio if it's a URL
   306→        if audio_url.startswith(('http://', 'https://')):
   307→            import requests
   308→            import uuid
   309→
   310→            # Determine file extension from URL or default to mp3
   311→            audio_ext = '.mp3'
   312→            url_lower = audio_url.lower()
   313→            for ext in ['.mp3', '.wav', '.aac', '.m4a', '.ogg', '.flac']:
   314→                if ext in url_lower:
   315→                    audio_ext = ext
   316→                    break
   317→
   318→            audio_filename = f"temp_audio_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}{audio_ext}"
   319→            local_audio_path = temp_dir / audio_filename
   320→
   321→            generation_logger.debug(f"[ADD_AUDIO] Downloading audio to {local_audio_path}...")
   322→
   323→            response = requests.get(audio_url, stream=True, timeout=60)
   324→            response.raise_for_status()
   325→
   326→            with open(local_audio_path, 'wb') as f:
   327→                for chunk in response.iter_content(chunk_size=8192):
   328→                    f.write(chunk)
   329→
   330→            generation_logger.debug(f"[ADD_AUDIO] Audio downloaded: {local_audio_path.stat().st_size} bytes")
   331→        else:
   332→            local_audio_path = Path(audio_url)
   333→            if not local_audio_path.exists():
   334→                generation_logger.debug(f"[ADD_AUDIO] Local audio file does not exist: {audio_url}")
   335→                return None
   336→
   337→        # Get video duration for logging
   338→        try:
   339→            probe_cmd = [
   340→                'ffprobe', '-v', 'error',
   341→                '-show_entries', 'format=duration',
   342→                '-of', 'default=noprint_wrappers=1:nokey=1',
   343→                str(input_video_path)
   344→            ]
   345→            result = subprocess.run(probe_cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
   346→            video_duration = float(result.stdout.strip()) if result.returncode == 0 else None
   347→            if video_duration:
   348→                generation_logger.debug(f"[ADD_AUDIO] Video duration: {video_duration:.2f}s")
   349→        except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError, ValueError) as e:
   350→            generation_logger.debug(f"[ADD_AUDIO] DEBUG: Failed to probe video duration: {e}")
   351→            video_duration = None
   352→
   353→        # Mux video with audio using FFmpeg
   354→        # -shortest: Stop when shortest stream ends (trims audio to video length)
   355→        # -c:v copy: Don't re-encode video (fast)
   356→        # -c:a aac: Encode audio to AAC for compatibility
   357→        output_video_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   358→
   359→        ffmpeg_cmd = [
   360→            'ffmpeg', '-y',
   361→            '-i', str(input_video_path),
   362→            '-i', str(local_audio_path),
   363→            '-c:v', 'copy',           # Copy video stream (no re-encoding)
   364→            '-c:a', 'aac',            # Encode audio to AAC
   365→            '-b:a', '192k',           # Audio bitrate
   366→            '-shortest',              # Trim to shortest stream (video)
   367→            '-map', '0:v:0',          # Take video from first input
   368→            '-map', '1:a:0',          # Take audio from second input
   369→            str(output_video_path)
   370→        ]
   371→
   372→        generation_logger.debug(f"[ADD_AUDIO] Running FFmpeg to mux audio...")
   373→        result = subprocess.run(ffmpeg_cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=300)
   374→
   375→        if result.returncode != 0:
   376→            generation_logger.debug(f"[ADD_AUDIO] FFmpeg failed: {result.stderr[:500] if result.stderr else 'No error message'}")
   377→            return None
   378→
   379→        if not output_video_path.exists() or output_video_path.stat().st_size == 0:
   380→            generation_logger.debug(f"[ADD_AUDIO] Output file not created or empty")
   381→            return None
   382→
   383→        # Clean up downloaded audio if we downloaded it
   384→        if audio_url.startswith(('http://', 'https://')):
   385→            try:
   386→                local_audio_path.unlink()
   387→            except OSError as e:
   388→                generation_logger.debug(f"[ADD_AUDIO] DEBUG: Failed to clean up downloaded audio file {local_audio_path}: {e}")
   389→
   390→        generation_logger.debug(f"[ADD_AUDIO] Successfully added audio to video: {output_video_path}")
   391→        generation_logger.debug(f"[ADD_AUDIO]   Output size: {output_video_path.stat().st_size} bytes")
   392→
   393→        return output_video_path
   394→
   395→    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError) as e:
   396→        generation_logger.debug(f"[ADD_AUDIO] Error adding audio: {e}", exc_info=True)
   397→        if output_video_path.exists():
   398→            try:
   399→                output_video_path.unlink()
   400→            except OSError as e_cleanup:
   401→                generation_logger.debug(f"[ADD_AUDIO] DEBUG: Failed to clean up output file {output_video_path}: {e_cleanup}")
   402→        return None
   403→
   404→def overlay_start_end_images_above_video(
   405→    start_image_path: str | Path,
   406→    end_image_path: str | Path,
   407→    input_video_path: str | Path,
   408→    output_video_path: str | Path) -> bool:
   409→    """Creates a composite video that shows *start_image* (left) and *end_image* (right)
   410→    on a row above the *input_video*.
   411→
   412→    Layout:
   413→        START | END  (static images, full video duration)
   414→        ----------------------
   415→               VIDEO           (original video frames)
   416→
   417→    The resulting video keeps the original width of *input_video*.  Each image is
   418→    scaled to exactly half that width and the same height as the video to ensure
   419→    perfect alignment.  The final output therefore has a height of
   420→    ``video_height * 2`` and a width equal to ``video_width``.
   421→
   422→    Args:
   423→        start_image_path: Path to the starting image.
   424→        end_image_path:   Path to the ending image.
   425→        input_video_path: Source video that was generated.
   426→        output_video_path: Desired path for the composite video.
   427→
   428→    Returns:
   429→        True if the composite video was created successfully, else False.
   430→    """
   431→    try:
   432→        start_image_path = Path(start_image_path)
   433→        end_image_path = Path(end_image_path)
   434→        input_video_path = Path(input_video_path)
   435→        output_video_path = Path(output_video_path)
   436→
   437→        if not (start_image_path.exists() and end_image_path.exists() and input_video_path.exists()):
   438→            generation_logger.debug(
   439→                f"overlay_start_end_images_above_video: One or more input paths are missing.\n"
   440→                f"  start_image_path = {start_image_path}\n"
   441→                f"  end_image_path   = {end_image_path}\n"
   442→                f"  input_video_path = {input_video_path}"
   443→            )
   444→            return False
   445→
   446→        # ---------------------------------------------------------
   447→        #   Preferred implementation: MoviePy (simpler, robust)
   448→        # ---------------------------------------------------------
   449→        if _MOVIEPY_AVAILABLE:
   450→            try:
   451→                video_clip = mpe.VideoFileClip(str(input_video_path))
   452→
   453→                half_width_px = int(video_clip.w / 2)
   454→
   455→                img1_clip = mpe.ImageClip(str(start_image_path)).resize(width=half_width_px).set_duration(video_clip.duration)
   456→                img2_clip = mpe.ImageClip(str(end_image_path)).resize(width=half_width_px).set_duration(video_clip.duration)
   457→
   458→                # top row (images side-by-side)
   459→                top_row = mpe.clips_array([[img1_clip, img2_clip]])
   460→
   461→                # Build composite video
   462→                final_h = top_row.h + video_clip.h
   463→                composite = mpe.CompositeVideoClip([
   464→                    top_row.set_position((0, 0)),
   465→                    video_clip.set_position((0, top_row.h))
   466→                ], size=(video_clip.w, final_h))
   467→
   468→                # Write video
   469→                composite.write_videofile(
   470→                    str(output_video_path.with_suffix('.mp4')),
   471→                    codec="libx264",
   472→                    audio=False,
   473→                    fps=video_clip.fps or fps,
   474→                    preset="veryfast")
   475→
   476→                video_clip.close(); img1_clip.close(); img2_clip.close(); composite.close()
   477→
   478→                if output_video_path.exists() and output_video_path.stat().st_size > 0:
   479→                    return True
   480→                else:
   481→                    generation_logger.debug("overlay_start_end_images_above_video: MoviePy output missing after write.")
   482→            except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e_mov:
   483→                generation_logger.debug(f"overlay_start_end_images_above_video: MoviePy path failed \u2013 {e_mov}. Falling back to ffmpeg.")
   484→
   485→        # ---------------------------------------------------------
   486→        #   Fallback: FFmpeg filter_complex (no MoviePy or MoviePy failed)
   487→        # ---------------------------------------------------------
   488→        if not _MOVIEPY_AVAILABLE:
   489→            try:
   490→                # ---------------------------------------------------------
   491→                #   Determine the resolution of the **input video**
   492→                # ---------------------------------------------------------
   493→                cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(input_video_path))
   494→                if not cap.isOpened():
   495→                    generation_logger.debug(f"overlay_start_end_images_above_video: Could not open video {input_video_path}")
   496→                    return False
   497→                video_width = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH))
   498→                video_height = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT))
   499→                cap.release()
   500→
   501→                if video_width == 0 or video_height == 0:
   502→                    generation_logger.debug(
   503→                        f"overlay_start_end_images_above_video: Failed to read resolution from {input_video_path}"
   504→                    )
   505→                    return False
   506→
   507→                half_width = video_width // 2  # Integer division for even-pixel alignment
   508→
   509→                # Ensure output directory exists
   510→                output_video_path = output_video_path.with_suffix('.mp4')
   511→                output_video_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   512→
   513→                # ---------------------------------------------------------
   514→                #   Build & run ffmpeg command
   515→                # ---------------------------------------------------------
   516→                # 1) Scale both images to half width / full height
   517→                # 2) hstack -> side-by-side static banner (labelled [top])
   518→                # 3) vstack banner with original video (labelled [vid])
   519→                # NOTE: We explicitly scale the *video* as well into label [vid]
   520→                #       to guarantee width/height match.  This is mostly defensive --
   521→                #       if the video is already the desired size the scale is a NOP.
   522→
   523→                filter_complex = (
   524→                    f"[1:v]scale={half_width}:{video_height}[left];"  # scale start img
   525→                    f"[2:v]scale={half_width}:{video_height}[right];"  # scale end img
   526→                    f"[left][right]hstack=inputs=2[top];"              # combine images
   527→                    f"[0:v]scale={video_width}:{video_height}[vid];"   # ensure video size
   528→                    f"[top][vid]vstack=inputs=2[output]"              # stack banner + video
   529→                )
   530→
   531→                # Determine FPS from the input video for consistent output
   532→                fps = 0.0
   533→                try:
   534→                    cap2 = cv2.VideoCapture(str(input_video_path))
   535→                    if cap2.isOpened():
   536→                        fps = cap2.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)
   537→                    cap2.release()
   538→                except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e:
   539→                    generation_logger.debug(f"overlay_start_end_images_above_video: DEBUG: Failed to read FPS from {input_video_path}: {e}")
   540→                    fps = 0.0
   541→                if fps is None or fps <= 0.1:
   542→                    fps = 16  # sensible default
   543→
   544→                ffmpeg_cmd = [
   545→                    "ffmpeg", "-y",  # overwrite output
   546→                    "-loglevel", "error",
   547→                    "-i", str(input_video_path),
   548→                    "-loop", "1", "-i", str(start_image_path),
   549→                    "-loop", "1", "-i", str(end_image_path),
   550→                    "-filter_complex", filter_complex,
   551→                    "-map", "[output]",
   552→                    "-r", str(int(round(fps))),  # set output fps
   553→                    "-shortest",  # stop when primary video stream ends
   554→                    "-c:v", "libx264", "-crf", "10", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p",
   555→                    "-movflags", "+faststart",
   556→                    str(output_video_path.resolve()),
   557→                ]
   558→
   559→                generation_logger.debug(f"overlay_start_end_images_above_video: Running ffmpeg to create composite video.\nCommand: {' '.join(ffmpeg_cmd)}")
   560→
   561→                proc = subprocess.run(ffmpeg_cmd, capture_output=True, timeout=600)
   562→                if proc.returncode != 0:
   563→                    generation_logger.debug(
   564→                        f"overlay_start_end_images_above_video: ffmpeg failed (returncode={proc.returncode}).\n"
   565→                        f"stderr: {proc.stderr.decode(errors='ignore')[:500]}"
   566→                    )
   567→                    # Clean up partially written file if any
   568→                    if output_video_path.exists():
   569→                        try:
   570→                            output_video_path.unlink()
   571→                        except OSError as e_cleanup:
   572→                            generation_logger.debug(f"overlay_start_end_images_above_video: DEBUG: Failed to clean up partial output {output_video_path}: {e_cleanup}")
   573→                    return False
   574→
   575→                if not output_video_path.exists() or output_video_path.stat().st_size == 0:
   576→                    generation_logger.debug(
   577→                        f"overlay_start_end_images_above_video: Output video not created or empty at {output_video_path}"
   578→                    )
   579→                    return False
   580→
   581→                return True
   582→
   583→            except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError) as e_ffmpeg:
   584→                generation_logger.debug(f"overlay_start_end_images_above_video: ffmpeg failed \u2013 {e_ffmpeg}. Falling back to MoviePy.")
   585→                return False
   586→
   587→    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e_ov:
   588→        generation_logger.debug(f"overlay_start_end_images_above_video: Exception \u2013 {e_ov}", exc_info=True)
   589→        try:
   590→            if output_video_path and output_video_path.exists():
   591→                output_video_path.unlink()
   592→        except OSError as e_cleanup:
   593→            generation_logger.debug(f"overlay_start_end_images_above_video: DEBUG: Failed to clean up output {output_video_path}: {e_cleanup}")
   594→        return False
   595→
```

    20→def _get_task_type_directory(task_type: str) -> str:
    21→    """
    22→    Map task types to their output subdirectories.
    23→
    24→    Single-level directory structure: outputs/{task_type}/{files}
    25→    Examples:
    26→    - vace -> outputs/vace/
    27→    - travel_orchestrator -> outputs/travel_orchestrator/
    28→    - extract_frame -> outputs/extract_frame/
    29→
    30→    Args:
    31→        task_type: The task type string (e.g., 'vace', 't2v', 'travel_orchestrator')
    32→
    33→    Returns:
    34→        Task type as directory name (e.g., 'vace', 'travel_orchestrator')
    35→    """
    36→    # Simply return the task type as the directory name
    37→    # This creates outputs/{task_type}/ structure
    38→    return task_type if task_type else 'misc'
    39→
    40→def prepare_output_path(
    41→    task_id: str,
    42→    filename: str,
    43→    main_output_dir_base: Path,
    44→    task_type: str | None = None,  # NEW PARAMETER
    45→    *,
    46→    custom_output_dir: str | Path | None = None
    47→) -> tuple[Path, str]:
    48→    """
    49→    Prepares the output path for a task's artifact.
    50→
    51→    If `custom_output_dir` is provided, it's used as the base. Otherwise,
    52→    the output is placed in a subdirectory based on task_type
    53→    or directly in `main_output_dir_base` (backwards compatibility).
    54→
    55→    Args:
    56→        task_id: Unique task identifier
    57→        filename: Output filename
    58→        main_output_dir_base: Base output directory (from worker --main-output-dir)
    59→        task_type: Optional task type for subdirectory organization
    60→        custom_output_dir: Optional custom output directory (overrides all)
    61→
    62→    Returns:
    63→        Tuple of (Path object for file location, string path for database)
    64→    """
    65→    # Decide base directory for the file
    66→    if custom_output_dir:
    67→        output_dir_for_task = Path(custom_output_dir)
    68→        headless_logger.debug(f"Task {task_id}: Using custom output directory: {output_dir_for_task}", task_id=task_id)
    69→    else:
    70→        # Create task-type-specific subdirectory structure
    71→        if task_type:
    72→            type_subdir = _get_task_type_directory(task_type)
    73→            output_dir_for_task = main_output_dir_base / type_subdir
    74→            headless_logger.debug(
    75→                f"Task {task_id}: Using task-type subdirectory: {output_dir_for_task} "
    76→                f"(task_type='{task_type}' -> '{type_subdir}')",
    77→                task_id=task_id
    78→            )
    79→        else:
    80→            # Backwards compatibility: No task_type provided, use root directory
    81→            output_dir_for_task = main_output_dir_base
    82→            headless_logger.debug(
    83→                f"Task {task_id}: No task_type provided, using root output directory: {output_dir_for_task} "
    84→                f"(backwards compatibility)",
    85→                task_id=task_id
    86→            )
    87→
    88→        # To avoid name collisions we prefix the filename with the task_id
    89→        # Skip prefixing for files with UUID patterns (they guarantee uniqueness)
    90→        import re
    91→        # Match UUID pattern: _HHMMSS_uuid6.ext
    92→        uuid_pattern = r'_\d{6}_[a-f0-9]{6}\.(mp4|png|jpg|jpeg)$'
    93→        has_uuid_pattern = re.search(uuid_pattern, filename, re.IGNORECASE)
    94→
    95→        if not filename.startswith(task_id) and not has_uuid_pattern:
    96→            filename = f"{task_id}_{filename}"
    97→
    98→    output_dir_for_task.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    99→
   100→    final_save_path = output_dir_for_task / filename
   101→
   102→    # Handle filename conflicts by adding _1, _2, etc.
   103→    if final_save_path.exists():
   104→        stem = final_save_path.stem
   105→        suffix = final_save_path.suffix
   106→        counter = 1
   107→        while final_save_path.exists():
   108→            new_filename = f"{stem}_{counter}{suffix}"
   109→            final_save_path = output_dir_for_task / new_filename
   110→            counter += 1
   111→        headless_logger.debug(f"Task {task_id}: Filename conflict resolved - using {final_save_path.name}", task_id=task_id)
   112→
   113→    # Build DB path string - use relative path to current working directory
   114→    try:
   115→        db_output_location = str(final_save_path.relative_to(Path.cwd()))
   116→    except ValueError:
   117→        db_output_location = str(final_save_path.resolve())
   118→
   119→    headless_logger.debug(f"Task {task_id}: final_save_path='{final_save_path}', db_output_location='{db_output_location}'", task_id=task_id)
   120→
   121→    return final_save_path, db_output_location
   122→
   123→def sanitize_filename_for_storage(filename: str) -> str:
   124→    """
   125→    Sanitizes a filename to be safe for storage systems like Supabase Storage.
   126→
   127→    Removes characters that are invalid for S3/Supabase storage keys:
   128→    - Control characters (0x00-0x1F, 0x7F-0x9F)
   129→    - Special characters: etc.
   130→    - Path separators and other problematic characters
   131→
   132→    Args:
   133→        filename: Original filename that may contain unsafe characters
   134→
   135→    Returns:
   136→        Sanitized filename safe for storage systems
   137→    """
   138→    import re
   139→
   140→    # Define characters to remove (includes the special character causing the issue)
   141→    # This is based on S3/Supabase storage key restrictions and common filesystem issues
   142→    unsafe_chars = r'[\u00a7\u00ae\u00a9\u2122@\u00b7\u00ba\u00bd\u00be\u00bf\u00a1~\x00-\x1F\x7F-\x9F<>:"/\\|?*,]'
   143→
   144→    # Replace unsafe characters with empty string
   145→    sanitized = re.sub(unsafe_chars, '', filename)
   146→
   147→    # Replace multiple consecutive spaces/dots with single ones
   148→    sanitized = re.sub(r'[ \.]{2,}', ' ', sanitized)
   149→
   150→    # Remove leading/trailing whitespace and dots
   151→    sanitized = sanitized.strip(' .')
   152→
   153→    # Ensure we have a non-empty filename
   154→    if not sanitized:
   155→        sanitized = "sanitized_file"
   156→
   157→    return sanitized
   158→
   159→def prepare_output_path_with_upload(
   160→    task_id: str,
   161→    filename: str,
   162→    main_output_dir_base: Path,
   163→    task_type: str | None = None,  # NEW PARAMETER
   164→    *,
   165→    custom_output_dir: str | Path | None = None
   166→) -> tuple[Path, str]:
   167→    """
   168→    Prepares the output path for a task's artifact and handles Supabase upload if configured.
   169→
   170→    Args:
   171→        task_id: Unique task identifier
   172→        filename: Output filename
   173→        main_output_dir_base: Base output directory (from worker --main-output-dir)
   174→        task_type: Optional task type for subdirectory organization
   175→        custom_output_dir: Optional custom output directory (overrides all)
   176→
   177→    Returns:
   178→        tuple[Path, str]: (local_file_path, db_output_location)
   179→        - local_file_path: Where to save the file locally (for generation)
   180→        - db_output_location: What to store in the database (local path or Supabase URL)
   181→    """
   182→    # Sanitize filename BEFORE any processing to prevent storage upload issues
   183→    original_filename = filename
   184→    sanitized_filename = sanitize_filename_for_storage(filename)
   185→
   186→    if original_filename != sanitized_filename:
   187→        headless_logger.debug(f"Task {task_id}: Sanitized filename '{original_filename}' -> '{sanitized_filename}'", task_id=task_id)
   188→
   189→    # First, get the local path where we'll save the file (using sanitized filename)
   190→    # Forward task_type parameter
   191→    local_save_path, initial_db_location = prepare_output_path(
   192→        task_id, sanitized_filename, main_output_dir_base,
   193→        task_type=task_type,  # Forward task_type
   194→        custom_output_dir=custom_output_dir
   195→    )
   196→
   197→    # Return the local path for now - we'll handle Supabase upload after file is created
   198→    return local_save_path, initial_db_location
   199→
   200→def upload_and_get_final_output_location(
   201→    local_file_path: Path,
   202→    initial_db_location: str) -> str:
   203→    """
   204→    Returns the local file path. Upload is now handled by the edge function.
   205→
   206→    Args:
   207→        local_file_path: Path to the local file
   208→        initial_db_location: The initial DB location (local path)
   209→
   210→    Returns:
   211→        str: Local file path (upload now handled by edge function)
   212→    """
   213→    # Edge function will handle the upload, so we just return the local path
   214→    headless_logger.debug(f"File ready for edge function upload: {local_file_path}")
   215→    return str(local_file_path.resolve())
   216→
   217→def upload_intermediate_file_to_storage(
   218→    local_file_path: Path,
   219→    task_id: str,
   220→    filename: str) -> str | None:
   221→    """
   222→    Upload an intermediate file to Supabase storage for cross-worker access.
   223→
   224→    This is used when orchestrators create intermediate files (like reversed videos)
   225→    that need to be accessible by child tasks running on different workers.
   226→
   227→    Includes retry logic for transient failures (502/503/504, timeouts, network errors).
   228→
   229→    Args:
   230→        local_file_path: Path to the local file to upload
   231→        task_id: Task ID for organizing uploads
   232→        filename: Filename to use in storage
   233→
   234→    Returns:
   235→        Public URL of the uploaded file, or None on failure
   236→    """
   237→    import httpx
   238→    import mimetypes
   239→
   240→    # Retry configuration
   241→    RETRYABLE_STATUS_CODES = {502, 503, 504}
   242→    MAX_RETRIES = 3
   243→
   244→    # Check if Supabase is configured (read URL from canonical config set by worker.py)
   245→    from source.core.db import config as _db_config
   246→    SUPABASE_URL = _db_config.SUPABASE_URL
   247→    SUPABASE_KEY = os.environ.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") or os.environ.get("SUPABASE_ANON_KEY")
   248→
   249→    if not SUPABASE_URL or not SUPABASE_KEY:
   250→        headless_logger.warning(f"[UPLOAD_INTERMEDIATE] Supabase not configured, cannot upload", task_id=task_id)
   251→        return None
   252→
   253→    if not local_file_path.exists():
   254→        headless_logger.warning(f"[UPLOAD_INTERMEDIATE] File not found: {local_file_path}", task_id=task_id)
   255→        return None
   256→
   257→    try:
   258→        headers = {
   259→            "Authorization": f"Bearer {SUPABASE_KEY}",
   260→            "apikey": SUPABASE_KEY,
   261→            "Content-Type": "application/json"
   262→        }
   263→
   264→        # Step 1: Get signed upload URL - WITH RETRY
   265→        generate_url_edge = f"{SUPABASE_URL.rstrip('/')}/functions/v1/generate-upload-url"
   266→        content_type = mimetypes.guess_type(str(local_file_path))[0] or 'application/octet-stream'
   267→
   268→        upload_url_resp = None
   269→        for attempt in range(MAX_RETRIES):
   270→            try:
   271→                upload_url_resp = httpx.post(
   272→                    generate_url_edge,
   273→                    headers=headers,
   274→                    json={
   275→                        "task_id": task_id,
   276→                        "filename": filename,
   277→                        "content_type": content_type
   278→                    },
   279→                    timeout=30 + (attempt * 15)
   280→                )
   281→
   282→                if upload_url_resp.status_code == 200:
   283→                    break
   284→                elif upload_url_resp.status_code in RETRYABLE_STATUS_CODES and attempt < MAX_RETRIES - 1:
   285→                    wait_time = 2 ** attempt
   286→                    headless_logger.warning(f"[UPLOAD_INTERMEDIATE] generate-upload-url got {upload_url_resp.status_code}, retrying in {wait_time}s...", task_id=task_id)
   287→                    time.sleep(wait_time)
   288→                    continue
   289→                else:
   290→                    break  # Non-retryable error
   291→
   292→            except (httpx.HTTPError, OSError, ValueError) as e:
   293→                if attempt < MAX_RETRIES - 1:
   294→                    wait_time = 2 ** attempt
   295→                    headless_logger.warning(f"[UPLOAD_INTERMEDIATE] generate-upload-url error, retrying in {wait_time}s: {e}", task_id=task_id)
   296→                    time.sleep(wait_time)
   297→                    continue
   298→                else:
   299→                    headless_logger.error(f"[UPLOAD_INTERMEDIATE] [EDGE_FAIL:generate-upload-url:NETWORK] {e}", task_id=task_id)
   300→                    return None
   301→
   302→        if not upload_url_resp or upload_url_resp.status_code != 200:
   303→            error_text = upload_url_resp.text[:200] if upload_url_resp else "No response"
   304→            error_code = upload_url_resp.status_code if upload_url_resp else "N/A"
   305→            headless_logger.error(f"[UPLOAD_INTERMEDIATE] [EDGE_FAIL:generate-upload-url:HTTP_{error_code}] {error_text}", task_id=task_id)
   306→            return None
   307→
   308→        upload_data = upload_url_resp.json()
   309→        upload_url = upload_data.get("upload_url")
   310→        storage_path = upload_data.get("storage_path")
   311→
   312→        if not upload_url:
   313→            headless_logger.error(f"[UPLOAD_INTERMEDIATE] No upload_url in response", task_id=task_id)
   314→            return None
   315→
   316→        # Step 2: Upload file via signed URL - WITH RETRY
   317→        # IMPORTANT: do NOT read entire file into memory (can be large).
   318→        file_size_mb = local_file_path.stat().st_size / BYTES_PER_KIBIBYTE / BYTES_PER_KIBIBYTE
   319→        headless_logger.debug(f"[UPLOAD_INTERMEDIATE] Uploading {local_file_path.name} ({file_size_mb:.1f} MB)", task_id=task_id)
   320→
   321→        put_resp = None
   322→        for attempt in range(MAX_RETRIES):
   323→            try:
   324→                with open(local_file_path, 'rb') as f:
   325→                    put_resp = httpx.put(
   326→                        upload_url,
   327→                        headers={"Content-Type": content_type},
   328→                        content=f,
   329→                        timeout=300 + (attempt * 60)  # 5 min base, +1 min per retry
   330→                    )
   331→
   332→                if put_resp.status_code in [200, 201]:
   333→                    break
   334→                elif put_resp.status_code in RETRYABLE_STATUS_CODES and attempt < MAX_RETRIES - 1:
   335→                    wait_time = 2 ** attempt
   336→                    headless_logger.warning(f"[UPLOAD_INTERMEDIATE] storage-upload got {put_resp.status_code}, retrying in {wait_time}s...", task_id=task_id)
   337→                    time.sleep(wait_time)
   338→                    continue
   339→                else:
   340→                    break  # Non-retryable error
   341→
   342→            except (httpx.HTTPError, OSError, ValueError) as e:
   343→                if attempt < MAX_RETRIES - 1:
   344→                    wait_time = 2 ** attempt
   345→                    headless_logger.warning(f"[UPLOAD_INTERMEDIATE] storage-upload error, retrying in {wait_time}s: {e}", task_id=task_id)
   346→                    time.sleep(wait_time)
   347→                    continue
   348→                else:
   349→                    headless_logger.error(f"[UPLOAD_INTERMEDIATE] [EDGE_FAIL:storage-upload:NETWORK] {e}", task_id=task_id)
   350→                    return None
   351→
   352→        if not put_resp or put_resp.status_code not in [200, 201]:
   353→            error_text = put_resp.text[:200] if put_resp else "No response"
   354→            error_code = put_resp.status_code if put_resp else "N/A"
   355→            headless_logger.error(f"[UPLOAD_INTERMEDIATE] [EDGE_FAIL:storage-upload:HTTP_{error_code}] {error_text}", task_id=task_id)
   356→            return None
   357→
   358→        # Construct public URL
   359→        public_url = f"{SUPABASE_URL}/storage/v1/object/public/image_uploads/{storage_path}"
   360→        headless_logger.debug(f"[UPLOAD_INTERMEDIATE] Uploaded to: {public_url}", task_id=task_id)
   361→
   362→        return public_url
   363→
   364→    except (httpx.HTTPError, OSError, ValueError) as e:
   365→        headless_logger.error(f"[UPLOAD_INTERMEDIATE] Exception: {e}", task_id=task_id, exc_info=True)
   366→        return None
   367→
   368→def wait_for_file_stable(path: Path | str, checks: int = 3, interval: float = 1.0) -> bool:
   369→    """Return True when the file size stays constant for a few consecutive checks.
   370→    Useful to make sure long-running encoders have finished writing before we
   371→    copy/move the file.
   372→    """
   373→    p = Path(path)
   374→    if not p.exists():
   375→        return False
   376→    last_size = p.stat().st_size
   377→    stable_count = 0
   378→    for _ in range(checks):
   379→        time.sleep(interval)
   380→        new_size = p.stat().st_size
   381→        if new_size == last_size and new_size > 0:
   382→            stable_count += 1
   383→            if stable_count >= checks - 1:
   384→                return True
   385→        else:
   386→            stable_count = 0
   387→            last_size = new_size
   388→    return False
   389→
```

    25→def _check_orchestrator_cancelled(orchestrator_task_id: str, context_msg: str, **_kwargs) -> str | None:
    26→    """Check if orchestrator was cancelled and cancel children if so.
    27→
    28→    Returns an error message string if cancelled, None if still active.
    29→    """
    30→    status = db_ops.get_task_current_status(orchestrator_task_id)
    31→    if status and status.lower() in ('cancelled', 'canceled'):
    32→        task_logger.debug(f"[CANCELLATION] Join orchestrator {orchestrator_task_id} was cancelled - {context_msg}", task_id=orchestrator_task_id)
    33→        db_ops.cancel_orchestrator_children(orchestrator_task_id, reason="Orchestrator cancelled by user")
    34→        return f"Orchestrator cancelled: {context_msg}"
    35→    return None

    79→def _check_existing_join_tasks(
    80→    orchestrator_task_id_str: str,
    81→    num_joins: int,
    82→    **_kwargs
    83→) -> Optional[TaskResult]:
    84→    """
    85→    Check for existing child tasks (idempotency check).
    86→
    87→    Handles both patterns:
    88→    - Chain pattern: num_joins join_clips_segment tasks
    89→    - Parallel pattern: num_joins join_clips_segment (transitions) + 1 join_final_stitch
    90→
    91→    Returns:
    92→        None if no existing tasks or should proceed with creation.
    93→        TaskResult if should return early (complete/failed/in-progress).
    94→    """
    95→    task_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CORE] Checking for existing child tasks", task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str)
    96→    existing_child_tasks = db_ops.get_orchestrator_child_tasks(orchestrator_task_id_str)
    97→    existing_joins = existing_child_tasks.get('join_clips_segment', [])
    98→    existing_final_stitch = existing_child_tasks.get('join_final_stitch', [])
    99→
   100→    # Determine which pattern was used
   101→    is_parallel_pattern = len(existing_final_stitch) > 0
   102→
   103→    if not existing_joins and not existing_final_stitch:
   104→        return None
   105→
   106→    task_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CORE] Found {len(existing_joins)} join tasks, {len(existing_final_stitch)} final stitch tasks", task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str)
   107→
   108→    # Check completion status helper
   109→    def is_complete(task):
   110→        return (task.get('status', '') or '').lower() == 'complete'
   111→
   112→    def is_terminal_failure(task):
   113→        status = task.get('status', '').lower()
   114→        return status in ('failed', 'cancelled', 'canceled', 'error')
   115→
   116→    if is_parallel_pattern:
   117→        # === PARALLEL PATTERN ===
   118→        if len(existing_joins) < num_joins:
   119→            return None
   120→
   121→        all_tasks = existing_joins + existing_final_stitch
   122→        any_failed = any(is_terminal_failure(t) for t in all_tasks)
   123→
   124→        if any_failed:
   125→            failed_tasks = [t for t in all_tasks if is_terminal_failure(t)]
   126→            error_msg = f"{len(failed_tasks)} task(s) failed/cancelled"
   127→            task_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CORE] FAILED: {error_msg}", task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str)
   128→            return TaskResult.failed(error_msg)
   129→
   130→        if existing_final_stitch and is_complete(existing_final_stitch[0]):
   131→            final_stitch = existing_final_stitch[0]
   132→            final_output = final_stitch.get('output_location', 'Completed via idempotency')
   133→            task_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CORE] COMPLETE (parallel): Final stitch done, output: {final_output}", task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str)
   134→            return TaskResult.orchestrator_complete(output_path=final_output, thumbnail_url="")
   135→
   136→        trans_complete = sum(1 for j in existing_joins if is_complete(j))
   137→        stitch_status = "complete" if existing_final_stitch and is_complete(existing_final_stitch[0]) else "pending"
   138→        task_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CORE] IDEMPOTENT (parallel): {trans_complete}/{num_joins} transitions, stitch: {stitch_status}", task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str)
   139→        return TaskResult.orchestrating(f"Parallel: {trans_complete}/{num_joins} transitions complete, stitch: {stitch_status}")
   140→
   141→    else:
   142→        # === CHAIN PATTERN (legacy) ===
   143→        if len(existing_joins) < num_joins:
   144→            return None
   145→
   146→        task_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CORE] All {num_joins} join tasks already exist (chain pattern)", task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str)
   147→
   148→        all_joins_complete = all(is_complete(join) for join in existing_joins)
   149→        any_join_failed = any(is_terminal_failure(join) for join in existing_joins)
   150→
   151→        if any_join_failed:
   152→            failed_joins = [j for j in existing_joins if is_terminal_failure(j)]
   153→            error_msg = f"{len(failed_joins)} join task(s) failed/cancelled"
   154→            task_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CORE] FAILED: {error_msg}", task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str)
   155→            return TaskResult.failed(error_msg)
   156→
   157→        if all_joins_complete:
   158→            def get_join_index(task):
   159→                params = task.get('task_params', {})
   160→                if isinstance(params, str):
   161→                    try:
   162→                        params = json.loads(params)
   163→                    except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError):
   164→                        return 0
   165→                return params.get('join_index', 0)
   166→
   167→            sorted_joins = sorted(existing_joins, key=get_join_index)
   168→            final_join = sorted_joins[-1]
   169→            final_output = final_join.get('output_location', 'Completed via idempotency')
   170→
   171→            final_params = final_join.get('task_params', {})
   172→            if isinstance(final_params, str):
   173→                try:
   174→                    final_params = json.loads(final_params)
   175→                except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError):
   176→                    final_params = {}
   177→
   178→            final_thumbnail = final_params.get('thumbnail_url', '')
   179→
   180→            task_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CORE] COMPLETE: All joins finished, final output: {final_output}", task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str)
   181→            return TaskResult.orchestrator_complete(output_path=final_output, thumbnail_url=final_thumbnail)
   182→
   183→        complete_count = sum(1 for j in existing_joins if is_complete(j))
   184→        task_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CORE] IDEMPOTENT: {complete_count}/{num_joins} joins complete", task_id=orchestrator_task_id_str)
   185→        return TaskResult.orchestrating(f"Join tasks in progress: {complete_count}/{num_joins} complete")
   186→
   187→
   188→# Public aliases for cross-module use.
   189→check_orchestrator_cancelled = _check_orchestrator_cancelled
   190→extract_join_settings_from_payload = _extract_join_settings_from_payload
   191→check_existing_join_tasks = _check_existing_join_tasks
   192→
