    28→def _extract_boundary_frames_for_vlm(
    29→    clip_list: List[dict],
    30→    temp_dir: Path,
    31→    orchestrator_task_id: str) -> List[Tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str], Optional[str], Optional[str]]]:
    32→    """
    33→    Extract boundary frames from clips for VLM prompt generation.
    34→
    35→    For each join (clip[i] -> clip[i+1]), extracts 4 frames:
    36→    - First frame from clip[i] (scene context)
    37→    - Last frame from clip[i] (boundary)
    38→    - First frame from clip[i+1] (boundary)
    39→    - Last frame from clip[i+1] (scene context)
    40→
    41→    Args:
    42→        clip_list: List of clip dicts with 'url' keys

    44→        orchestrator_task_id: Task ID for logging

    47→        List of (start_first, start_boundary, end_boundary, end_last) tuples for each join
    48→    """
    49→    image_quads = []
    50→    num_joins = len(clip_list) - 1
    51→
    52→    # Cache downloaded videos and their extracted frames to avoid re-downloading
    53→    clip_data_cache = {}
    54→
    55→    for idx in range(num_joins):
    56→        clip_start = clip_list[idx]
    57→        clip_end = clip_list[idx + 1]
    58→
    59→        start_url = clip_start.get("url")
    60→        end_url = clip_end.get("url")
    61→
    62→        if not start_url or not end_url:

    64→            image_quads.append((None, None, None, None))
    65→            continue
    66→
    67→        try:
    68→            # === Extract frames from clip_start ===
    69→            if start_url in clip_data_cache:
    70→                start_frames, start_first_path, start_last_path = clip_data_cache[start_url]

    89→                cv2.imwrite(str(start_last_path), cv2.cvtColor(start_frames[-1], cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR))

    92→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_EXTRACT] Join {idx}: Extracted {len(start_frames)} frames from start clip")
    93→
    94→            start_boundary_idx = len(start_frames) - 1
    95→            start_boundary_path = temp_dir / f"vlm_clip{idx}_boundary.jpg"

    97→
    98→            # === Extract frames from clip_end ===
    99→            if end_url in clip_data_cache:
   100→                end_frames, end_first_path, end_last_path = clip_data_cache[end_url]

   119→                cv2.imwrite(str(end_last_path), cv2.cvtColor(end_frames[-1], cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR))

   121→                clip_data_cache[end_url] = (end_frames, str(end_first_path), str(end_last_path))
   122→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_EXTRACT] Join {idx}: Extracted {len(end_frames)} frames from end clip")
   123→
   124→            end_boundary_idx = 0
   125→            end_boundary_path = temp_dir / f"vlm_clip{idx+1}_boundary.jpg"
   126→            cv2.imwrite(str(end_boundary_path), cv2.cvtColor(end_frames[end_boundary_idx], cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR))
   127→

   135→
   136→        except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e:
   137→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_EXTRACT] Join {idx}: ERROR extracting frames: {e}")
   138→            image_quads.append((None, None, None, None))
   139→
   140→    return image_quads
   141→
   142→def _generate_join_transition_prompt(
   143→    start_first_path: str,
   144→    start_boundary_path: str,
   145→    end_boundary_path: str,
   146→    end_last_path: str,
   147→    base_prompt: str,
   148→    extender) -> str:
   149→    """
   150→    Generate a single transition prompt for join_clips using 4 boundary images.
   151→
   152→    Args:
   153→        start_first_path: Path to first frame of starting clip
   154→        start_boundary_path: Path to last frame of starting clip
   155→        end_boundary_path: Path to first frame of ending clip
   156→        end_last_path: Path to last frame of ending clip
   157→        base_prompt: Base prompt for context
   158→        extender: QwenPromptExpander instance
   159→
   160→    Returns:
   161→        Generated prompt
   162→    """
   163→    from PIL import Image
   164→
   165→    start_first = Image.open(start_first_path).convert("RGB")
   166→    start_boundary = Image.open(start_boundary_path).convert("RGB")
   167→    end_boundary = Image.open(end_boundary_path).convert("RGB")
   168→    end_last = Image.open(end_last_path).convert("RGB")
   169→
   170→    images = [start_first, start_boundary, end_boundary, end_last]
   171→    combined_width = sum(img.width for img in images)
   172→    combined_height = max(img.height for img in images)
   173→    combined_img = Image.new('RGB', (combined_width, combined_height))
   174→
   175→    x_offset = 0
   176→    for img in images:
   177→        combined_img.paste(img, (x_offset, 0))
   178→        x_offset += img.width
   179→
   180→    base_prompt_text = base_prompt if base_prompt and base_prompt.strip() else "a video sequence"
   181→
   182→    query = f"""Look at these 4 frames from a video, left to right in time order.
   183→
   184→The MIDDLE TWO frames show where we need to generate a transition.
   185→
   186→Context from user: {base_prompt_text}
   187→
   188→Write a short video prompt describing what happens between the middle frames. Include:
   189→1. The motion or action
   190→2. The visual style
   191→3. Key details in the scene
   192→
   193→Keep it under 50 words total. Just write the prompt, nothing else.
   194→
   195→Example: "A cat jumps from table to chair. Realistic home video style. Sunny kitchen with wooden furniture."
   196→
   197→Example: "Camera pans right across city skyline. Cinematic drone footage at sunset. Skyscrapers with orange light reflections."
   198→
   199→Your prompt:"""
   200→
   201→    system_prompt = "Write a short video description prompt. Under 50 words. No explanations, just the prompt."
   202→
   203→    result = extender.extend_with_img(
   204→        prompt=query,
   205→        system_prompt=system_prompt,
   206→        image=combined_img
   207→    )
   208→
   209→    return result.prompt.strip()
   210→
   211→def _generate_vlm_prompts_for_joins(
   212→    image_quads: List[Tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str], Optional[str], Optional[str]]],
   213→    base_prompt: str,
   214→    vlm_device: str) -> List[Optional[str]]:
   215→    """
   216→    Generate VLM-enhanced prompts for ALL joins using 4-image quads.
   217→
   218→    Args:
   219→        image_quads: List of (start_first, start_boundary, end_boundary, end_last) tuples
   220→        base_prompt: Base prompt to use as VLM context
   221→        vlm_device: Device for VLM inference ('cuda' or 'cpu')
   222→
   223→    Returns:
   224→        List of enhanced prompts (None for joins with missing image quads)
   225→    """
   226→    import torch
   227→
   228→    num_joins = len(image_quads)
   229→    result = [None] * num_joins
   230→
   231→    valid_quads = []
   232→    valid_indices = []
   233→
   234→    for idx in range(num_joins):
   235→        quad = image_quads[idx]
   236→        if all(path is not None for path in quad):
   237→            valid_quads.append(quad)
   238→            valid_indices.append(idx)
   239→        else:
   240→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_PROMPTS] Join {idx}: Skipping - missing image(s) in quad")
   241→
   242→    if not valid_quads:
   243→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_PROMPTS] No valid image quads for VLM processing")
   244→        return result
   245→
   246→    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_PROMPTS] Processing {len(valid_quads)}/{num_joins} joins with VLM (4 images each)")
   247→    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_PROMPTS] Base prompt context: '{base_prompt[:80]}...'" if base_prompt else "[VLM_PROMPTS] No base prompt (VLM will infer from frames)")
   248→
   249→    try:
   250→        # Add Wan2GP to path for imports
   251→        wan_dir = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent.parent / "Wan2GP"
   252→        if str(wan_dir) not in sys.path:
   253→            sys.path.insert(0, str(wan_dir))
   254→
   255→        from Wan2GP.shared.utils.prompt_extend import QwenPromptExpander
   256→        from source.media.vlm.model import download_qwen_vlm_if_needed
   257→
   258→        if torch.cuda.is_available():
   259→            gpu_mem_before = torch.cuda.memory_allocated() / BYTES_PER_GB
   260→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_PROMPTS] GPU memory BEFORE VLM load: {gpu_mem_before:.2f} GB")
   261→
   262→        local_model_path = wan_dir / "ckpts" / "Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct"
   263→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_PROMPTS] Checking for model at {local_model_path}...")
   264→        download_qwen_vlm_if_needed(local_model_path)
   265→
   266→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_PROMPTS] Initializing Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct...")
   267→        extender = QwenPromptExpander(
   268→            model_name=str(local_model_path),
   269→            device=vlm_device,
   270→            is_vl=True
   271→        )
   272→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_PROMPTS] Model loaded (initially on CPU, moves to {vlm_device} for inference)")
   273→
   274→        for i, quad in enumerate(valid_quads):
   275→            idx = valid_indices[i]
   276→            start_first, start_boundary, end_boundary, end_last = quad
   277→            try:
   278→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_PROMPTS] Processing join {idx} ({i+1}/{len(valid_quads)})...")
   279→
   280→                enhanced = _generate_join_transition_prompt(
   281→                    start_first_path=start_first,
   282→                    start_boundary_path=start_boundary,
   283→                    end_boundary_path=end_boundary,
   284→                    end_last_path=end_last,
   285→                    base_prompt=base_prompt,
   286→                    extender=extender)
   287→
   288→                result[idx] = enhanced
   289→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_PROMPTS] Join {idx}: {enhanced[:100]}...")
   290→
   291→            except (RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError) as e:
   292→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_PROMPTS] Join {idx}: ERROR - {e}")
   293→
   294→        # Cleanup VLM
   295→        if torch.cuda.is_available():
   296→            gpu_mem_before_cleanup = torch.cuda.memory_allocated() / BYTES_PER_GB
   297→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_CLEANUP] GPU memory BEFORE cleanup: {gpu_mem_before_cleanup:.2f} GB")
   298→
   299→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_CLEANUP] Cleaning up VLM model and processor...")
   300→        try:
   301→            del extender.model
   302→            del extender.processor
   303→            del extender
   304→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_CLEANUP] \u2705 Successfully deleted VLM objects")
   305→        except (RuntimeError, AttributeError) as e:
   306→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_CLEANUP] \u26a0\ufe0f  Error during deletion: {e}")
   307→
   308→        import gc
   309→        collected = gc.collect()
   310→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_CLEANUP] Garbage collected {collected} objects")
   311→
   312→        if torch.cuda.is_available():
   313→            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   314→            gpu_mem_after = torch.cuda.memory_allocated() / BYTES_PER_GB
   315→            gpu_freed = gpu_mem_before_cleanup - gpu_mem_after
   316→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_CLEANUP] GPU memory AFTER cleanup: {gpu_mem_after:.2f} GB")
   317→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_CLEANUP] GPU memory freed: {gpu_freed:.2f} GB")
   318→
   319→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_CLEANUP] \u2705 VLM cleanup complete")
   320→        return result
   321→
   322→    except (RuntimeError, ValueError, OSError, ImportError) as e:
   323→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[VLM_PROMPTS] ERROR in VLM processing: {e}", exc_info=True)
   324→        return result
   325→
   326→
   327→# Public aliases for cross-module use.
   328→extract_boundary_frames_for_vlm = _extract_boundary_frames_for_vlm
   329→generate_join_transition_prompt = _generate_join_transition_prompt
   330→generate_vlm_prompts_for_joins = _generate_vlm_prompts_for_joins
   331→

1→"""
     2→Join final stitch handler - stitch all clips and transitions together in one pass.
     3→
     4→This is the second phase of the parallel join architecture:
     5→1. Multiple join_clips_segment tasks generate transitions in parallel (transition_only=True)
     6→2. This task stitches all original clips + transitions together in a single encode pass
     7→"""
     8→
     9→import json
    10→from pathlib import Path
    11→from typing import Tuple
    12→
    13→# Import shared utilities
    14→from ...utils import (
    15→    get_video_frame_count_and_fps,
    16→    download_video_if_url,
    17→    prepare_output_path_with_upload,
    18→    upload_and_get_final_output_location)

    24→from ... import db_operations as db_ops

    26→
    27→def handle_join_final_stitch(
    28→    task_params_from_db: dict,
    29→    main_output_dir_base: Path,
    30→    task_id: str) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    31→    """
    32→    Handle join_final_stitch task: stitch all clips and transitions together in one pass.
    33→
    34→    This is the second phase of the parallel join architecture:
    35→    1. Multiple join_clips_segment tasks generate transitions in parallel (transition_only=True)
    36→    2. This task stitches all original clips + transitions together in a single encode pass
    37→
    38→    Args:
    39→        task_params_from_db: Task parameters including:
    40→            - clip_list: List of original clip dicts with 'url' and optional 'name'
    41→            - transition_task_ids: List of transition task IDs to fetch outputs from
    42→            - gap_from_clip1: Frames to trim from end of each clip (except last)
    43→            - gap_from_clip2: Frames to trim from start of each clip (except first)
    44→            - blend_frames: Frames to crossfade at each boundary
    45→            - fps: Output FPS
    46→            - audio_url: Optional audio to add to final output
    47→        main_output_dir_base: Base output directory
    48→        task_id: Task ID for logging
    49→
    50→    Returns:
    51→        Tuple of (success: bool, output_path_or_message: str)
    52→    """
    53→    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Starting final stitch handler")
    54→
    55→    try:
    56→        # --- Chain Mode Passthrough ---
    57→        # When created by the chain pattern, the last join in the chain already produced
    58→        # the fully concatenated video. We just pass through its output (+ optional audio).
    59→        chain_mode = task_params_from_db.get("chain_mode", False)
    60→        if chain_mode:
    61→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Chain mode \u2014 passthrough from last chain join")
    62→            transition_task_ids = task_params_from_db.get("transition_task_ids", [])
    63→            if not transition_task_ids:
    64→                return False, "chain_mode=True but no transition_task_ids (last chain join ID) provided"
    65→
    66→            # The single ID is the last join in the chain
    67→            last_join_id = transition_task_ids[0]
    68→            chain_output = db_ops.get_task_output_location_from_db(last_join_id)
    69→            if not chain_output:
    70→                return False, f"Failed to get output from chain join task {last_join_id}"
    71→
    72→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Chain output from {last_join_id}: {chain_output[:100]}...")
    73→
    74→            # The chain join's output_location is the final video URL/path — use it directly.
    75→            # In chain mode, the last join_clips_child already muxes audio when is_last_join=True,
    76→            # so no additional audio processing is needed here.
    77→            audio_url = task_params_from_db.get("audio_url")
    78→            if audio_url:
    79→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Audio requested \u2014 already muxed by last chain join, passthrough")
    80→
    81→            return True, chain_output
    82→
    83→        # --- 1. Extract Parameters ---
    84→        clip_list = task_params_from_db.get("clip_list", [])
    85→        transition_task_ids = task_params_from_db.get("transition_task_ids", [])
    86→        blend_frames = task_params_from_db.get("blend_frames", 15)
    87→        target_fps = task_params_from_db.get("fps", 16)
    88→        audio_url = task_params_from_db.get("audio_url")
    89→
    90→        # NOTE: gap_from_clip1/gap_from_clip2 are intentionally NOT read from task_params
    91→        # because the orchestrator calculated them from raw gap_frame_count (before 4n+1
    92→        # quantization), while segment tasks use quantized gap_for_guide. This mismatch
    93→        # caused a 1-frame alignment bug. Gap values MUST come from each transition's
    94→        # output_location (ground truth from VACE). Fallbacks only for legacy compatibility.
    95→        gap_from_clip1 = task_params_from_db.get("gap_from_clip1", 8)  # Legacy fallback only
    96→        gap_from_clip2 = task_params_from_db.get("gap_from_clip2", 9)  # Legacy fallback only
    97→
    98→        num_clips = len(clip_list)
    99→        num_transitions = len(transition_task_ids)
   100→        expected_transitions = num_clips - 1
   101→
   102→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: {num_clips} clips, {num_transitions} transitions")
   103→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: blend_frames={blend_frames} (gap values from per-transition output, fallback={gap_from_clip1}/{gap_from_clip2})")
   104→
   105→        if num_clips < 2:
   106→            return False, "clip_list must contain at least 2 clips"
   107→
   108→        if num_transitions != expected_transitions:
   109→            return False, f"Expected {expected_transitions} transitions for {num_clips} clips, got {num_transitions}"
   110→
   111→        # --- 2. Create Working Directory ---
   112→        stitch_dir = main_output_dir_base / f"final_stitch_{task_id[:8]}"
   113→        stitch_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   114→
   115→        # --- 3. Fetch Transition Outputs ---
   116→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Fetching transition outputs...")
   117→        transitions = []
   118→
   119→        for i, trans_task_id in enumerate(transition_task_ids):
   120→            # Get the output from the completed transition task
   121→            trans_output = db_ops.get_task_output_location_from_db(trans_task_id)
   122→            if not trans_output:
   123→                return False, f"Failed to get output for transition task {trans_task_id}"
   124→
   125→            # Parse the JSON output from transition_only mode
   126→            try:
   127→                trans_data = json.loads(trans_output)
   128→                trans_url = trans_data.get("transition_url")
   129→                if not trans_url:
   130→                    return False, f"Transition task {trans_task_id} output missing transition_url"
   131→
   132→                # Extract per-transition blend values
   133→                # context_from_clip1 = frames from clip before transition
   134→                # context_from_clip2 = frames from clip after transition
   135→                ctx_clip1 = trans_data.get("context_from_clip1", blend_frames)
   136→                ctx_clip2 = trans_data.get("context_from_clip2", blend_frames)
   137→                trans_blend = trans_data.get("blend_frames", min(ctx_clip1, ctx_clip2))
   138→
   139→                trans_frames = trans_data.get("frames")
   140→                gap_frames = trans_data.get("gap_frames")
   141→
   142→                # Verify structure consistency: frames = ctx1 + gap + ctx2
   143→                if trans_frames and gap_frames:
   144→                    expected_total = ctx_clip1 + gap_frames + ctx_clip2
   145→                    if expected_total != trans_frames:
   146→                        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: \u26a0\ufe0f Transition {i} structure mismatch!")
   147→                        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH]   frames={trans_frames}, but ctx1({ctx_clip1}) + gap({gap_frames}) + ctx2({ctx_clip2}) = {expected_total}")
   148→
   149→                # Extract gap values from transition output (ground truth from VACE)
   150→                trans_gap1 = trans_data.get("gap_from_clip1")
   151→                trans_gap2 = trans_data.get("gap_from_clip2")
   152→
   153→                # Log whether we're using ground truth or fallback values
   154→                if trans_gap1 is not None and trans_gap2 is not None:
   155→                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Transition {i}: Using ground truth gap values from VACE: gap1={trans_gap1}, gap2={trans_gap2}")
   156→                else:
   157→                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: \u26a0\ufe0f Transition {i}: Missing gap values in output, using fallback: gap1={gap_from_clip1}, gap2={gap_from_clip2}")
   158→                    trans_gap1 = gap_from_clip1
   159→                    trans_gap2 = gap_from_clip2
   160→
   161→                transitions.append({
   162→                    "url": trans_url,
   163→                    "index": trans_data.get("transition_index", i),
   164→                    "frames": trans_frames,
   165→                    "gap_frames": gap_frames,
   166→                    "blend_frames": trans_blend,
   167→                    "context_from_clip1": ctx_clip1,  # For clip->transition crossfade
   168→                    "context_from_clip2": ctx_clip2,  # For transition->clip crossfade
   169→                    "gap_from_clip1": trans_gap1,
   170→                    "gap_from_clip2": trans_gap2,
   171→                    # Additional debug info from transition output
   172→                    "clip1_context_start_idx": trans_data.get("clip1_context_start_idx"),
   173→                    "clip1_context_end_idx": trans_data.get("clip1_context_end_idx"),
   174→                    "clip2_context_start_idx": trans_data.get("clip2_context_start_idx"),
   175→                    "clip2_context_end_idx": trans_data.get("clip2_context_end_idx"),
   176→                    "clip1_total_frames": trans_data.get("clip1_total_frames"),
   177→                    "clip2_total_frames": trans_data.get("clip2_total_frames"),
   178→                })
   179→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Transition {i}: frames={trans_frames}, structure=[{ctx_clip1}+{gap_frames}+{ctx_clip2}], blend={trans_blend}")
   180→            except json.JSONDecodeError:
   181→                # Fallback: treat as direct URL (legacy mode)
   182→                transitions.append({
   183→                    "url": trans_output,
   184→                    "index": i,
   185→                    "blend_frames": blend_frames,
   186→                    "context_from_clip1": blend_frames,
   187→                    "context_from_clip2": blend_frames,
   188→                })
   189→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Transition {i} (raw URL, using defaults): {trans_output}")
   190→
   191→        # Sort transitions by index
   192→        transitions.sort(key=lambda t: t["index"])
   193→
   194→        # --- ALIGNMENT VERIFICATION TABLE ---
   195→        # This is the key diagnostic: shows exactly what each transition expects
   196→        # and makes mismatches immediately obvious
   197→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: ")
   198→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] \u2554\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2557")
   199→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] \u2551                      TRANSITION ALIGNMENT TABLE                              \u2551")
   200→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] \u2560\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2563")
   201→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] \u2551 Trans \u2502 Clip1 Frames \u2502 Clip1 Ctx Idx  \u2502 Clip2 Frames \u2502 Clip2 Ctx Idx  \u2502 Gap  \u2551")
   202→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] \u2560\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2563")
   203→
   204→        for i, trans in enumerate(transitions):
   205→            clip1_total = trans.get("clip1_total_frames", "?")
   206→            clip2_total = trans.get("clip2_total_frames", "?")
   207→            ctx1_start = trans.get("clip1_context_start_idx", "?")
   208→            ctx1_end = trans.get("clip1_context_end_idx", "?")
   209→            ctx2_start = trans.get("clip2_context_start_idx", "?")
   210→            ctx2_end = trans.get("clip2_context_end_idx", "?")
   211→            gap1 = trans.get("gap_from_clip1", "?")
   212→            gap2 = trans.get("gap_from_clip2", "?")
   213→
   214→            # Format context indices
   215→            ctx1_str = f"[{ctx1_start}:{ctx1_end})" if ctx1_start != "?" else "N/A"
   216→            ctx2_str = f"[{ctx2_start}:{ctx2_end})" if ctx2_start != "?" else "N/A"
   217→
   218→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] \u2551  {i:2d}   \u2502     {str(clip1_total):4s}     \u2502 {ctx1_str:14s} \u2502     {str(clip2_total):4s}     \u2502 {ctx2_str:14s} \u2502{gap1}/{gap2:2}\u2551")
   219→
   220→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] \u255a\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u255d")
   221→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] ")
   222→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Legend: Ctx Idx = context frame indices extracted for VACE")
   223→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH]         Gap = gap_from_clip1/gap_from_clip2 (frames trimmed from each clip)")
   224→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] ")
   225→
   226→        # Validate gap values are consistent across transitions (current architecture assumes this)
   227→        if len(transitions) > 1:
   228→            first_gap1 = transitions[0].get("gap_from_clip1", gap_from_clip1)
   229→            first_gap2 = transitions[0].get("gap_from_clip2", gap_from_clip2)
   230→            for t in transitions[1:]:
   231→                t_gap1 = t.get("gap_from_clip1", gap_from_clip1)
   232→                t_gap2 = t.get("gap_from_clip2", gap_from_clip2)
   233→                if t_gap1 != first_gap1 or t_gap2 != first_gap2:
   234→                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: \u26a0\ufe0f WARNING - Inconsistent gap values across transitions!")
   235→                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH]   Transition 0: gap1={first_gap1}, gap2={first_gap2}")
   236→                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH]   Transition {t['index']}: gap1={t_gap1}, gap2={t_gap2}")
   237→
   238→        # --- 4. Download All Videos ---
   239→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Downloading clips and transitions...")
   240→
   241→        clip_paths = []
   242→        for i, clip in enumerate(clip_list):
   243→            clip_url = clip.get("url")
   244→            if not clip_url:
   245→                return False, f"Clip {i} missing 'url'"
   246→
   247→            local_path = download_video_if_url(
   248→                clip_url,
   249→                download_target_dir=stitch_dir,
   250→                task_id_for_logging=task_id,
   251→                descriptive_name=f"clip_{i}"
   252→            )
   253→            if not local_path:
   254→                return False, f"Failed to download clip {i}: {clip_url}"
   255→            clip_paths.append(Path(local_path))
   256→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Downloaded clip {i}: {local_path}")
   257→
   258→        transition_paths = []
   259→        for i, trans in enumerate(transitions):
   260→            trans_url = trans.get("url")
   261→            local_path = download_video_if_url(
   262→                trans_url,
   263→                download_target_dir=stitch_dir,
   264→                task_id_for_logging=task_id,
   265→                descriptive_name=f"transition_{i}"
   266→            )
   267→            if not local_path:
   268→                return False, f"Failed to download transition {i}: {trans_url}"
   269→            transition_paths.append(Path(local_path))
   270→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Downloaded transition {i}: {local_path}")
   271→
   272→        # --- 4a. FRAME COUNT VERIFICATION ---
   273→        # Verify actual clip frame counts match what transitions expected
   274→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Verifying clip frame counts...")
   275→        frame_count_mismatches = []
   276→
   277→        for i, clip_path in enumerate(clip_paths):
   278→            actual_frames, _ = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(str(clip_path))
   279→
   280→            # Check against transitions that reference this clip
   281→            # Transition i uses clip i as clip1 (if i < num_transitions)
   282→            if i < len(transitions):
   283→                expected_clip1 = transitions[i].get("clip1_total_frames")
   284→                if expected_clip1 is not None and expected_clip1 != actual_frames:
   285→                    frame_count_mismatches.append(
   286→                        f"Clip {i}: actual={actual_frames}, trans[{i}] expected clip1={expected_clip1}"
   287→                    )
   288→                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: \u26a0\ufe0f FRAME COUNT MISMATCH: Clip {i} has {actual_frames} frames, but transition {i} expected {expected_clip1}")
   289→
   290→            # Transition i-1 uses clip i as clip2 (if i > 0)
   291→            if i > 0:
   292→                expected_clip2 = transitions[i - 1].get("clip2_total_frames")
   293→                if expected_clip2 is not None and expected_clip2 != actual_frames:
   294→                    frame_count_mismatches.append(
   295→                        f"Clip {i}: actual={actual_frames}, trans[{i-1}] expected clip2={expected_clip2}"
   296→                    )
   297→                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: \u26a0\ufe0f FRAME COUNT MISMATCH: Clip {i} has {actual_frames} frames, but transition {i-1} expected {expected_clip2}")
   298→
   299→        if frame_count_mismatches:
   300→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: \u274c FOUND {len(frame_count_mismatches)} FRAME COUNT MISMATCHES!")
   301→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: This may indicate clips were re-encoded or transitions used different source files.")
   302→        else:
   303→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: \u2713 All clip frame counts match transition expectations")
   304→
   305→        # --- 4b. PIXEL VERIFICATION: Compare transition context frames with original clips ---
   306→        # This verifies that VACE preserved context frames pixel-identically
   307→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Verifying pixel alignment between clips and transitions...")
   308→
   309→        for i, trans in enumerate(transitions):
   310→            ctx1 = trans.get("context_from_clip1", 0)
   311→            ctx2 = trans.get("context_from_clip2", 0)
   312→
   313→            if ctx1 > 0 and i < len(clip_paths):
   314→                # Compare: clip[i]'s last ctx1 frames (before gap) vs transition's first ctx1 frames
   315→                try:
   316→                    clip_frames_list = extract_frames_from_video(str(clip_paths[i]))
   317→                    trans_frames_list = extract_frames_from_video(str(transition_paths[i]))
   318→
   319→                    if clip_frames_list and trans_frames_list:
   320→                        # Clip context: last (gap_from_clip1 + ctx1) to last gap_from_clip1 frames
   321→                        # i.e., frames that will remain after trimming, specifically the last ctx1 of those
   322→                        gap1 = trans.get("gap_from_clip1", gap_from_clip1)
   323→                        clip_ctx_start = len(clip_frames_list) - gap1 - ctx1
   324→                        clip_ctx_end = len(clip_frames_list) - gap1
   325→
   326→                        clip_context = clip_frames_list[clip_ctx_start:clip_ctx_end]
   327→                        trans_context = trans_frames_list[:ctx1]
   328→
   329→                        if len(clip_context) == len(trans_context) == ctx1:
   330→                            # Compare first and last frame of context region
   331→                            import numpy as np
   332→
   333→                            # First frame comparison
   334→                            diff_first = np.abs(clip_context[0].astype(float) - trans_context[0].astype(float)).mean()
   335→                            # Last frame comparison
   336→                            diff_last = np.abs(clip_context[-1].astype(float) - trans_context[-1].astype(float)).mean()
   337→
   338→                            if diff_first < 1.0 and diff_last < 1.0:
   339→                                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[PIXEL_CHECK] \u2713 Transition {i} START context: PIXEL IDENTICAL (diff={diff_first:.2f}, {diff_last:.2f})")
   340→                            else:
   341→                                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[PIXEL_CHECK] \u26a0\ufe0f Transition {i} START context: MISMATCH DETECTED!")
   342→                                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[PIXEL_CHECK]   First frame diff: {diff_first:.2f}, Last frame diff: {diff_last:.2f}")
   343→                                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[PIXEL_CHECK]   Clip frames [{clip_ctx_start}:{clip_ctx_end}] vs Transition frames [0:{ctx1}]")
   344→
   345→                            # === OFFSET DETECTION: Compare trans[0] against clip frames at different offsets ===
   346→                            # This helps identify if there's a systematic frame shift
   347→                            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[OFFSET_DETECT] Transition {i} START: Comparing trans[0] against clip frames at offsets -2 to +2")
   348→                            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[OFFSET_DETECT]   Expected alignment: trans[0] should match clip[{clip_ctx_start}]")
   349→
   350→                            trans_first_frame = trans_context[0].astype(float)
   351→                            offset_diffs = {}
   352→                            for offset in range(-2, 3):  # -2, -1, 0, +1, +2
   353→                                test_idx = clip_ctx_start + offset
   354→                                if 0 <= test_idx < len(clip_frames_list):
   355→                                    test_frame = clip_frames_list[test_idx].astype(float)
   356→                                    diff = np.abs(trans_first_frame - test_frame).mean()
   357→                                    offset_diffs[offset] = diff
   358→                                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[OFFSET_DETECT]   offset={offset:+d} (clip[{test_idx}]): diff={diff:.2f}")
   359→
   360→                            # Find best matching offset
   361→                            if offset_diffs:
   362→                                best_offset = min(offset_diffs, [REDACTED])
   363→                                best_diff = offset_diffs[best_offset]
   364→                                if best_offset != 0:
   365→                                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[OFFSET_DETECT]   >>> BEST MATCH at offset={best_offset:+d} (diff={best_diff:.2f}) - ALIGNMENT BUG DETECTED!")
   366→                                else:
   367→                                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[OFFSET_DETECT]   >>> Best match at offset=0 (diff={best_diff:.2f}) - alignment appears correct")
   368→
   369→                            # Also check the LAST frame of context for offset
   370→                            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[OFFSET_DETECT] Transition {i} START: Comparing trans[{ctx1-1}] against clip frames at offsets -2 to +2")
   371→                            trans_last_ctx_frame = trans_context[-1].astype(float)
   372→                            expected_last_idx = clip_ctx_end - 1  # Last frame of context
   373→                            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[OFFSET_DETECT]   Expected alignment: trans[{ctx1-1}] should match clip[{expected_last_idx}]")
   374→
   375→                            offset_diffs_last = {}
   376→                            for offset in range(-2, 3):
   377→                                test_idx = expected_last_idx + offset
   378→                                if 0 <= test_idx < len(clip_frames_list):
   379→                                    test_frame = clip_frames_list[test_idx].astype(float)
   380→                                    diff = np.abs(trans_last_ctx_frame - test_frame).mean()
   381→                                    offset_diffs_last[offset] = diff
   382→                                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[OFFSET_DETECT]   offset={offset:+d} (clip[{test_idx}]): diff={diff:.2f}")
   383→
   384→                            if offset_diffs_last:
   385→                                best_offset_last = min(offset_diffs_last, [REDACTED])
   386→                                best_diff_last = offset_diffs_last[best_offset_last]
   387→                                if best_offset_last != 0:
   388→                                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[OFFSET_DETECT]   >>> BEST MATCH at offset={best_offset_last:+d} (diff={best_diff_last:.2f}) - ALIGNMENT BUG DETECTED!")
   389→                                else:
   390→                                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[OFFSET_DETECT]   >>> Best match at offset=0 (diff={best_diff_last:.2f}) - alignment appears correct")
   391→                        else:
   392→                            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[PIXEL_CHECK] \u26a0\ufe0f Transition {i}: Frame count mismatch for START context")
   393→                            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[PIXEL_CHECK]   Expected {ctx1}, got clip={len(clip_context)}, trans={len(trans_context)}")
   394→                except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e:
   395→                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[PIXEL_CHECK] \u26a0\ufe0f Transition {i}: Error comparing START context: {e}")
   396→
   397→            if ctx2 > 0 and i + 1 < len(clip_paths):
   398→                # Compare: transition's last ctx2 frames vs clip[i+1]'s first ctx2 frames (after gap)
   399→                try:
   400→                    next_clip_frames = extract_frames_from_video(str(clip_paths[i + 1]))
   401→                    trans_frames_list = extract_frames_from_video(str(transition_paths[i]))
   402→
   403→                    if next_clip_frames and trans_frames_list:
   404→                        gap2 = trans.get("gap_from_clip2", gap_from_clip2)
   405→                        # Next clip context: frames [gap2 : gap2 + ctx2]
   406→                        next_clip_context = next_clip_frames[gap2:gap2 + ctx2]
   407→                        trans_end_context = trans_frames_list[-ctx2:]
   408→
   409→                        if len(next_clip_context) == len(trans_end_context) == ctx2:
   410→                            import numpy as np
   411→
   412→                            diff_first = np.abs(next_clip_context[0].astype(float) - trans_end_context[0].astype(float)).mean()
   413→                            diff_last = np.abs(next_clip_context[-1].astype(float) - trans_end_context[-1].astype(float)).mean()
   414→
   415→                            if diff_first < 1.0 and diff_last < 1.0:
   416→                                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[PIXEL_CHECK] \u2713 Transition {i} END context: PIXEL IDENTICAL (diff={diff_first:.2f}, {diff_last:.2f})")
   417→                            else:
   418→                                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[PIXEL_CHECK] \u26a0\ufe0f Transition {i} END context: MISMATCH DETECTED!")
   419→                                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[PIXEL_CHECK]   First frame diff: {diff_first:.2f}, Last frame diff: {diff_last:.2f}")
   420→                                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[PIXEL_CHECK]   Next clip frames [{gap2}:{gap2 + ctx2}] vs Transition frames [-{ctx2}:]")
   421→
   422→                            # === OFFSET DETECTION for END context ===
   423→                            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[OFFSET_DETECT] Transition {i} END: Comparing trans[-{ctx2}] against next_clip frames at offsets -2 to +2")
   424→                            expected_clip2_start = gap2
   425→                            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[OFFSET_DETECT]   Expected alignment: trans[-{ctx2}] should match next_clip[{expected_clip2_start}]")
   426→
   427→                            trans_end_first_frame = trans_end_context[0].astype(float)
   428→                            offset_diffs_end = {}
   429→                            for offset in range(-2, 3):
   430→                                test_idx = expected_clip2_start + offset
   431→                                if 0 <= test_idx < len(next_clip_frames):
   432→                                    test_frame = next_clip_frames[test_idx].astype(float)
   433→                                    diff = np.abs(trans_end_first_frame - test_frame).mean()
   434→                                    offset_diffs_end[offset] = diff
   435→                                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[OFFSET_DETECT]   offset={offset:+d} (next_clip[{test_idx}]): diff={diff:.2f}")
   436→
   437→                            if offset_diffs_end:
   438→                                best_offset_end = min(offset_diffs_end, [REDACTED])
   439→                                best_diff_end = offset_diffs_end[best_offset_end]
   440→                                if best_offset_end != 0:
   441→                                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[OFFSET_DETECT]   >>> BEST MATCH at offset={best_offset_end:+d} (diff={best_diff_end:.2f}) - ALIGNMENT BUG DETECTED!")
   442→                                else:
   443→                                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[OFFSET_DETECT]   >>> Best match at offset=0 (diff={best_diff_end:.2f}) - alignment appears correct")
   444→
   445→                            # Also check last frame of END context
   446→                            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[OFFSET_DETECT] Transition {i} END: Comparing trans[-1] against next_clip frames at offsets -2 to +2")
   447→                            expected_clip2_end = gap2 + ctx2 - 1
   448→                            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[OFFSET_DETECT]   Expected alignment: trans[-1] should match next_clip[{expected_clip2_end}]")
   449→
   450→                            trans_very_last_frame = trans_end_context[-1].astype(float)
   451→                            offset_diffs_end_last = {}
   452→                            for offset in range(-2, 3):
   453→                                test_idx = expected_clip2_end + offset
   454→                                if 0 <= test_idx < len(next_clip_frames):
   455→                                    test_frame = next_clip_frames[test_idx].astype(float)
   456→                                    diff = np.abs(trans_very_last_frame - test_frame).mean()
   457→                                    offset_diffs_end_last[offset] = diff
   458→                                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[OFFSET_DETECT]   offset={offset:+d} (next_clip[{test_idx}]): diff={diff:.2f}")
   459→
   460→                            if offset_diffs_end_last:
   461→                                best_offset_end_last = min(offset_diffs_end_last, [REDACTED])
   462→                                best_diff_end_last = offset_diffs_end_last[best_offset_end_last]
   463→                                if best_offset_end_last != 0:
   464→                                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[OFFSET_DETECT]   >>> BEST MATCH at offset={best_offset_end_last:+d} (diff={best_diff_end_last:.2f}) - ALIGNMENT BUG DETECTED!")
   465→                                else:
   466→                                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[OFFSET_DETECT]   >>> Best match at offset=0 (diff={best_diff_end_last:.2f}) - alignment appears correct")
   467→                        else:
   468→                            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[PIXEL_CHECK] \u26a0\ufe0f Transition {i}: Frame count mismatch for END context")
   469→                except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e:
   470→                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[PIXEL_CHECK] \u26a0\ufe0f Transition {i}: Error comparing END context: {e}")
   471→
   472→        # --- 5. Trim Clips and Build Stitch List ---
   473→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Preparing clips for stitching...")
   474→
   475→        import tempfile
   476→        stitch_videos = []
   477→        stitch_blends = []
   478→
   479→        for i, clip_path in enumerate(clip_paths):
   480→            clip_frames, clip_fps = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(str(clip_path))
   481→            if not clip_frames:
   482→                return False, f"Could not get frame count for clip {i}"
   483→
   484→            # Determine trim amounts using PER-TRANSITION gap values (ground truth from VACE)
   485→            # trim_start: how much to trim from START of this clip
   486→            #   - Uses gap_from_clip2 from the PREVIOUS transition (i-1)
   487→            #   - Because transition[i-1] connects clip[i-1] -> clip[i], and gap_from_clip2 is how much of clip[i]'s start was used as gap
   488→            # trim_end: how much to trim from END of this clip
   489→            #   - Uses gap_from_clip1 from the CURRENT transition (i)
   490→            #   - Because transition[i] connects clip[i] -> clip[i+1], and gap_from_clip1 is how much of clip[i]'s end was used as gap
   491→
   492→            if i > 0:
   493→                # Get gap_from_clip2 from previous transition (transition i-1 connects to this clip)
   494→                prev_trans = transitions[i - 1]
   495→                trim_start = prev_trans.get("gap_from_clip2", gap_from_clip2)
   496→                trim_start_source = f"trans[{i-1}].gap_from_clip2"
   497→            else:
   498→                trim_start = 0  # First clip: no start trim
   499→                trim_start_source = "none (first clip)"
   500→
   501→            if i < num_clips - 1:
   502→                # Get gap_from_clip1 from current transition (this clip connects to transition i)
   503→                curr_trans = transitions[i]
   504→                trim_end = curr_trans.get("gap_from_clip1", gap_from_clip1)
   505→                trim_end_source = f"trans[{i}].gap_from_clip1"
   506→            else:
   507→                trim_end = 0  # Last clip: no end trim
   508→                trim_end_source = "none (last clip)"
   509→
   510→            frames_to_keep = clip_frames - trim_start - trim_end
   511→            if frames_to_keep <= 0:
   512→                return False, f"Clip {i} has {clip_frames} frames but needs {trim_start + trim_end} trimmed"
   513→
   514→            # Log trim details with source of values
   515→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Clip {i}: {clip_frames} frames total")
   516→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH]   trim_start={trim_start} (from {trim_start_source}), trim_end={trim_end} (from {trim_end_source})")
   517→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH]   keeping frames [{trim_start}:{clip_frames - trim_end}] = {frames_to_keep} frames")
   518→
   519→            # Extract trimmed clip
   520→            with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix='.mp4', delete=False, dir=stitch_dir) as tf:
   521→                trimmed_path = Path(tf.name)
   522→
   523→            start_frame = trim_start
   524→            end_frame = clip_frames - trim_end - 1 if trim_end > 0 else None
   525→
   526→            try:
   527→                extract_frame_range_to_video(
   528→                    input_video_path=clip_path,
   529→                    output_video_path=trimmed_path,
   530→                    start_frame=start_frame,
   531→                    end_frame=end_frame,
   532→                    fps=target_fps)
   533→            except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e:
   534→                return False, f"Failed to trim clip {i}: {e}"
   535→
   536→            # Add to stitch list
   537→            stitch_videos.append(trimmed_path)
   538→
   539→            # Add transition after this clip (except after last clip)
   540→            if i < num_clips - 1:
   541→                # Use per-transition blend values:
   542→                # - clip[i] -> transition[i]: context_from_clip1 (context from clip i in transition)
   543→                # - transition[i] -> clip[i+1]: context_from_clip2 (context from clip i+1 in transition)
   544→                trans_info = transitions[i]
   545→                blend_clip_to_trans = trans_info.get("context_from_clip1", blend_frames)
   546→                blend_trans_to_clip = trans_info.get("context_from_clip2", blend_frames)
   547→
   548→                stitch_blends.append(blend_clip_to_trans)  # Blend between clip and transition
   549→                stitch_videos.append(transition_paths[i])
   550→                stitch_blends.append(blend_trans_to_clip)  # Blend between transition and next clip
   551→
   552→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Crossfades for transition {i}: clip\u2192trans={blend_clip_to_trans}, trans\u2192clip={blend_trans_to_clip}")
   553→
   554→        # --- 5b. FINAL FRAME ACCOUNTING ---
   555→        # Show exactly how the final video will be composed
   556→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: ")
   557→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] \u2554\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2557")
   558→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] \u2551                        FINAL VIDEO FRAME ACCOUNTING                         \u2551")
   559→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] \u2560\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2563")
   560→
   561→        final_frame_position = 0
   562→        for idx, video_path in enumerate(stitch_videos):
   563→            video_frames, _ = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(str(video_path))
   564→            if video_frames is None:
   565→                video_frames = 0
   566→
   567→            # Determine if this is a clip or transition
   568→            # Pattern: clip0, trans0, clip1, trans1, clip2, ... (clips at even indices, transitions at odd)
   569→            is_clip = (idx % 2 == 0)
   570→            source_idx = idx // 2
   571→
   572→            if is_clip:
   573→                # Get the original clip info
   574→                orig_frames, _ = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(str(clip_paths[source_idx])) if source_idx < len(clip_paths) else (0, 0)
   575→
   576→                # Reconstruct trim info
   577→                if source_idx > 0:
   578→                    t_start = transitions[source_idx - 1].get("gap_from_clip2", gap_from_clip2)
   579→                else:
   580→                    t_start = 0
   581→                if source_idx < len(transitions):
   582→                    t_end = transitions[source_idx].get("gap_from_clip1", gap_from_clip1)
   583→                else:
   584→                    t_end = 0
   585→
   586→                source_desc = f"Clip {source_idx}: frames [{t_start}:{orig_frames - t_end}]"
   587→            else:
   588→                trans_idx = source_idx
   589→                trans_info = transitions[trans_idx] if trans_idx < len(transitions) else {}
   590→                ctx1 = trans_info.get("context_from_clip1", "?")
   591→                gap = trans_info.get("gap_frames", "?")
   592→                ctx2 = trans_info.get("context_from_clip2", "?")
   593→                source_desc = f"Trans {trans_idx}: [{ctx1} ctx1 + {gap} gap + {ctx2} ctx2]"
   594→
   595→            # Get blend info
   596→            _blend_before = stitch_blends[idx - 1] if idx > 0 and idx - 1 < len(stitch_blends) else 0
   597→            blend_after = stitch_blends[idx] if idx < len(stitch_blends) else 0
   598→
   599→            end_frame = final_frame_position + video_frames - 1
   600→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] \u2551 [{final_frame_position:5d}-{end_frame:5d}] {source_desc:<45s} ({video_frames:3d}f) \u2551")
   601→
   602→            if blend_after > 0 and idx < len(stitch_videos) - 1:
   603→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] \u2551              \u2195 crossfade {blend_after} frames                                     \u2551")
   604→
   605→            final_frame_position += video_frames - blend_after  # Subtract overlap
   606→
   607→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] \u2560\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2563")
   608→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] \u2551 Expected total frames (approximate): {final_frame_position:<38d} \u2551")
   609→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] \u255a\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u2550\u255d")
   610→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] ")
   611→
   612→        # --- 6. Stitch Everything Together ---
   613→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Stitching {len(stitch_videos)} videos with {len(stitch_blends)} blend points...")
   614→
   615→        final_output_path, initial_db_location = prepare_output_path_with_upload(
   616→            task_id=task_id,
   617→            filename=f"{task_id}_joined.mp4",
   618→            main_output_dir_base=main_output_dir_base,
   619→            task_type="join_final_stitch")
   620→
   621→        try:
   622→            stitch_videos_with_crossfade(
   623→                video_paths=stitch_videos,
   624→                blend_frame_counts=stitch_blends,
   625→                output_video_path=final_output_path,
   626→                fps=target_fps,
   627→                crossfade_mode="linear_sharp",
   628→                crossfade_sharp_amt=0.3)
   629→
   630→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Successfully stitched all videos")
   631→
   632→        except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e:
   633→            return False, f"Failed to stitch videos: {e}"
   634→
   635→        # --- 7. Add Audio (if provided) ---
   636→        if audio_url:
   637→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Adding audio from {audio_url}")
   638→            try:
   639→                audio_local = download_video_if_url(
   640→                    audio_url,
   641→                    download_target_dir=stitch_dir,
   642→                    task_id_for_logging=task_id,
   643→                    descriptive_name="audio"
   644→                )
   645→                if audio_local:
   646→                    with_audio_path = final_output_path.with_name(f"{task_id}_joined_audio.mp4")
   647→                    success = add_audio_to_video(
   648→                        input_video_path=str(final_output_path),
   649→                        audio_url=audio_local,
   650→                        output_video_path=str(with_audio_path))
   651→                    if success and with_audio_path.exists():
   652→                        final_output_path = with_audio_path
   653→                        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Audio added successfully")
   654→            except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as audio_err:
   655→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Failed to add audio (continuing without): {audio_err}")
   656→
   657→        # --- 8. Verify and Upload ---
   658→        if not final_output_path.exists():
   659→            return False, "Final output file does not exist"
   660→
   661→        file_size = final_output_path.stat().st_size
   662→        if file_size == 0:
   663→            return False, "Final output file is empty"
   664→
   665→        final_frames, final_fps = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(str(final_output_path))
   666→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Final video: {final_frames} frames @ {final_fps} fps, {file_size} bytes")
   667→
   668→        # Upload
   669→        final_db_location = upload_and_get_final_output_location(
   670→            local_file_path=final_output_path,
   671→            initial_db_location=initial_db_location)
   672→
   673→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH] Task {task_id}: Complete - {final_db_location}")
   674→        return True, final_db_location
   675→
   676→    except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError, KeyError, TypeError) as e:
   677→        error_msg = f"Unexpected error in final stitch handler: {e}"
   678→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[FINAL_STITCH_ERROR] Task {task_id}: {error_msg}", exc_info=True)
   679→        return False, error_msg
   680→

   320→                if start_w and start_h and end_w and end_h:
   321→                    start_aspect = start_w / start_h
   322→                    end_aspect = end_w / end_h
   323→
   324→                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CLIPS] Task {task_id}: Starting video: {start_w}x{start_h} (aspect: {start_aspect:.3f})")
   325→                    orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CLIPS] Task {task_id}: Ending video: {end_w}x{end_h} (aspect: {end_aspect:.3f})")
   326→
   327→                    # If aspect ratios differ by more than 1%, standardize ending video to match starting video
   328→                    if abs(start_aspect - end_aspect) > 0.01:
   329→                        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CLIPS] Task {task_id}: Aspect ratios differ - standardizing ending video to match starting video")
   330→
   331→                        # Calculate aspect ratio string from starting video
   332→                        # Use common aspect ratios or create from dimensions
   333→                        if abs(start_aspect - 16/9) < 0.01:
   334→                            auto_aspect_ratio = "16:9"
   335→                        elif abs(start_aspect - 9/16) < 0.01:
   336→                            auto_aspect_ratio = "9:16"
   337→                        elif abs(start_aspect - 1.0) < 0.01:
   338→                            auto_aspect_ratio = "1:1"
   339→                        elif abs(start_aspect - 4/3) < 0.01:
   340→                            auto_aspect_ratio = "4:3"
   341→                        elif abs(start_aspect - 21/9) < 0.01:
   342→                            auto_aspect_ratio = "21:9"
   343→                        else:
   344→                            # Use exact dimensions as ratio
   345→                            auto_aspect_ratio = f"{start_w}:{start_h}"
   346→
   347→                        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CLIPS] Task {task_id}: Auto-detected aspect ratio: {auto_aspect_ratio}")
   348→
   349→                        standardized_end_path = join_clips_dir / f"end_standardized_{task_id}.mp4"
   350→                        result = standardize_video_aspect_ratio(
   351→                            input_video_path=ending_video,
   352→                            output_video_path=standardized_end_path,
   353→                            target_aspect_ratio=auto_aspect_ratio,
   354→                            task_id_for_logging=task_id)
   355→                        if result is None:
   356→                            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CLIPS_WARNING] Task {task_id}: Failed to auto-standardize ending video, proceeding with original")
   357→                        else:
   358→                            ending_video = standardized_end_path
   359→                            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CLIPS] Task {task_id}: Ending video standardized to match starting video")
   360→                    else:
   361→                        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CLIPS] Task {task_id}: Videos have matching aspect ratios, no standardization needed")
   362→
   363→            except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e:
   364→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CLIPS_WARNING] Task {task_id}: Could not check video dimensions: {e}")
   365→                orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CLIPS] Task {task_id}: Proceeding with original videos")
   366→
   367→        # --- 3. Extract Video Properties ---
   368→        try:
   369→            start_frame_count, start_fps = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(str(starting_video))
   370→            end_frame_count, end_fps = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(str(ending_video))
   371→
   372→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CLIPS] Task {task_id}: Starting video - {start_frame_count} frames @ {start_fps} fps")
   373→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CLIPS] Task {task_id}: Ending video - {end_frame_count} frames @ {end_fps} fps")
   374→
   375→        except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as e:
   376→            error_msg = f"Failed to extract video properties: {e}"
   377→            orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CLIPS_ERROR] Task {task_id}: {error_msg}")
   378→            return False, error_msg
   379→

1→"""
     2→Join clips generation handler - bridge two video clips using VACE generation.
     3→
     4→This module provides the main handle_join_clips_task function that:
     5→1. Optionally standardizes both videos to a target aspect ratio (via center-crop)
     6→2. Extracts context frames from the end of the first clip
     7→3. Extracts context frames from the beginning of the second clip
     8→4. Generates transition frames between them using VACE
     9→5. Uses mask video to preserve the context frames and only generate the gap
    10→"""
    11→
    12→import json
    13→import time
    14→from pathlib import Path
    15→from typing import Tuple

    35→    get_video_frame_count_ffprobe,
    36→    get_video_fps_ffprobe,
    37→    add_audio_to_video)
    38→from source.media.video.vace_frame_utils import (
    39→    create_guide_and_mask_for_generation,
    40→    prepare_vace_generation_params
    41→)
    42→from ... import db_operations as db_ops
    43→from ...core.log import headless_logger, orchestrator_logger
    44→
    45→from .vace_quantization import _calculate_vace_quantization
    46→
    47→def handle_join_clips_task(
    48→    task_params_from_db: dict,
    49→    main_output_dir_base: Path,
    50→    task_id: str,
    51→    task_queue = None) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
    52→    """
    53→    Handle join_clips task: bridge two video clips using VACE generation.
    54→
    55→    Args:
    56→        task_params_from_db: Task parameters including:
    57→            - starting_video_path: Path to first video clip
    58→            - ending_video_path: Path to second video clip
    59→            - context_frame_count: Number of frames to extract from each clip
    60→            - gap_frame_count: Number of frames to generate between clips (INSERT mode) or replace (REPLACE mode)

60→            - gap_frame_count: Number of frames to generate between clips (INSERT mode) or replace (REPLACE mode)
    61→            - replace_mode: Optional bool (default False). If True, gap frames REPLACE boundary frames instead of being inserted
    62→            - prompt: Generation prompt for the transition
    63→            - aspect_ratio: Optional aspect ratio (e.g., "16:9", "9:16", "1:1") to standardize both videos
    64→            - model: Optional model override (defaults to wan_2_2_vace_lightning_baseline_2_2_2)
    65→            - resolution: Optional [width, height] override
    66→            - use_input_video_resolution: Optional bool (default False). If True, uses the detected resolution from input video instead of resolution override
    67→            - fps: Optional FPS override (defaults to 16)
    68→            - use_input_video_fps: Optional bool (default False). If True, uses input video's FPS. If False, downsamples to fps param (default 16)
    69→            - max_wait_time: Optional timeout in seconds for generation (defaults to 1800s / 30 minutes)

    98→        orchestrator_logger.debug(f"[JOIN_CLIPS] Task {task_id}: Mode: {'REPLACE' if replace_mode else 'INSERT'}")

   161→def _download_file_if_url(
   162→    file_url_or_path: str,
   163→    download_target_dir: Path | str | None,
   164→    task_id_for_logging: str | None = "generic_task",
   165→    descriptive_name: str | None = None,
   166→    default_extension: str = ".jpg",
   167→    default_stem: str = "file",
   168→    file_type_label: str = "file",
   169→    timeout: int = 300
   170→) -> str:
   171→    """
   172→    Generic function to download a file from URL if needed.
   173→
   174→    Args:
   175→        file_url_or_path: URL or local path
   176→        download_target_dir: Directory to save downloaded file
   177→        task_id_for_logging: Task ID for logging
   178→        descriptive_name: Optional descriptive name for the file
   179→        default_extension: Default file extension if none detected
   180→        default_stem: Default stem for filename if none detected
   181→        file_type_label: Label for logging (e.g., "image", "video")
   182→        timeout: Request timeout in seconds
   183→
   184→    Returns:
   185→        Local file path string if downloaded, otherwise returns the original string
   186→    """
   187→    if not file_url_or_path:
   188→        return file_url_or_path
   189→
   190→    parsed_url = urlparse(file_url_or_path)
   191→    if parsed_url.scheme in ['http', 'https'] and download_target_dir:
   192→        target_dir_path = Path(download_target_dir)
   193→        try:
   194→            target_dir_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   195→            headless_logger.debug(f"Task {task_id_for_logging}: Downloading {file_type_label} from URL: {file_url_or_path} to {target_dir_path.resolve()}")
   196→
   197→            # Use a session for potential keep-alive and connection pooling
   198→            with requests.Session() as s:
   199→                response = s.get(file_url_or_path, stream=True, timeout=timeout)
   200→                response.raise_for_status()
   201→
   202→            original_filename = Path(parsed_url.path).name
   203→            original_suffix = Path(original_filename).suffix if Path(original_filename).suffix else default_extension
   204→            if not original_suffix.startswith('.'):
   205→                original_suffix = '.' + original_suffix
   206→
   207→            # Use descriptive naming if provided, otherwise fall back to improved default
   208→            if descriptive_name:
   209→                base_name_for_download = descriptive_name[:50]  # Limit length
   210→            else:
   211→                # Improved default naming
   212→                cleaned_stem = Path(original_filename).stem[:30] if Path(original_filename).stem else default_stem
   213→                base_name_for_download = f"input_{cleaned_stem}"
   214→
   215→            # _get_unique_target_path expects a Path object for target_dir
   216→            local_file_path = _get_unique_target_path(target_dir_path, base_name_for_download, original_suffix)
   217→
   218→            with open(local_file_path, 'wb') as f:
   219→                for chunk in response.iter_content(chunk_size=8192):
   220→                    if chunk:
   221→                        f.write(chunk)
   222→
   223→            headless_logger.debug(f"Task {task_id_for_logging}: Successfully downloaded {file_type_label} to {local_file_path}")
   224→            return str(local_file_path)
   225→
   226→        except requests.exceptions.RequestException as e:
   227→            headless_logger.debug(f"Task {task_id_for_logging}: ERROR downloading {file_type_label} from {file_url_or_path}: {e}")
   228→            return file_url_or_path  # Return original URL on failure
   229→        except OSError as e_dl:
   230→            headless_logger.debug(f"Task {task_id_for_logging}: ERROR saving {file_type_label} to {target_dir_path}: {e_dl}", exc_info=True)
   231→            return file_url_or_path  # Return original URL on failure
   232→    else:
   233→        # Not a URL, or no download directory provided
   234→        return file_url_or_path
   235→
